````bash
      info "allowed in ufw: ${open:-none}"
      ;;
    ufw:*)
      if is_runner; then info "ufw installed but inactive (cloud firewall assumed)"
      else warn "ufw installed but inactive (ufw enable)"; fi
      ;;
    *)
      if is_runner; then info "no host firewall (cloud firewall assumed)"
      else warn "no host firewall found (firewalld or ufw)"; fi
      ;;
  esac

  if ! have fail2ban-client && ! unit_exists fail2ban.service; then
    if is_runner; then skip "fail2ban not installed"; else warn "fail2ban not installed (SSH brute-force protection)"; fi
    return 0
  fi
  if ! systemctl is-active --quiet fail2ban 2>/dev/null && ! { have fail2ban-client && fail2ban-client ping >/dev/null 2>&1; }; then
    warn "fail2ban not active"
    return 0
  fi
  local st banned total
  if st=$(fail2ban-client status sshd 2>/dev/null); then
    banned=$(sed -nE 's/.*Currently banned:[[:space:]]*([0-9]+).*/\1/p' <<<"$st" | head -1)
    total=$(sed -nE 's/.*Total banned:[[:space:]]*([0-9]+).*/\1/p' <<<"$st" | head -1)
    ok "fail2ban active, sshd jail: ${banned:-0} banned now (${total:-0} total)"
  else
    warn "fail2ban active but no sshd jail (fail2ban-client status)"
  fi
  return 0
}

check_updates() {
  section "Updates and reboot"
  local t found="" n pending="" applies=0 conf
  for t in server-update.timer dnf5-automatic.timer dnf-automatic-install.timer dnf-automatic.timer apt-daily-upgrade.timer; do
    unit_exists "$t" || continue
    systemctl is-enabled --quiet "$t" 2>/dev/null || systemctl is-active --quiet "$t" 2>/dev/null || continue
    found="$t"; break
  done
  if [[ -z "$found" ]]; then
    # openSUSE and others: any enabled timer that looks like an update job
    found=$(systemctl list-unit-files '*.timer' --state=enabled --no-legend --plain 2>/dev/null \
      | awk '{ print $1 }' | grep -E 'zypper|yum-cron|unattended|transactional-update|packagekit-offline' | head -1 || true)
  fi

  if [[ -z "$found" ]]; then
    warn "no automatic update timer found (dnf-automatic, unattended-upgrades or zypper)"
  elif [[ "$found" == server-update.timer ]]; then
    local dup=""
    for t in dnf5-automatic.timer dnf-automatic-install.timer dnf-automatic.timer; do
      if unit_exists "$t" && systemctl is-enabled --quiet "$t" 2>/dev/null; then dup="$t"; fi
    done
    if [[ -n "$dup" ]]; then warn "server-update.timer and ${dup} are both enabled: two updaters race on the dnf lock (rerun server/install-tools.sh)"
    else ok "automatic updates: server-update.timer ($(systemctl show "$found" -p NextElapseUSecRealtime --value 2>/dev/null || true))"; fi
  elif [[ "$found" == apt-daily-upgrade.timer ]]; then
    if grep -qsE '^[^/]*APT::Periodic::Unattended-Upgrade[[:space:]]+"1"' /etc/apt/apt.conf.d/20auto-upgrades; then
      ok "unattended-upgrades enabled (${found})"
    else
      warn "${found} enabled but APT::Periodic::Unattended-Upgrade is not 1 (/etc/apt/apt.conf.d/20auto-upgrades)"
    fi
  elif [[ "$found" == dnf*automatic* ]]; then
    [[ "$found" == *install* ]] && applies=1
    for conf in /etc/dnf/automatic.conf /etc/dnf/dnf5-plugins/automatic.conf; do
      [[ -f "$conf" ]] || continue
      if grep -qE '^[[:space:]]*apply_updates[[:space:]]*=[[:space:]]*(yes|true|1)' "$conf"; then applies=1; fi
    done
    if (( applies )); then
      ok "automatic updates: ${found} (installs updates)"
    else
      warn "${found} enabled but apply_updates is not yes (updates are only downloaded or reported)"
    fi
  else
    ok "automatic updates: ${found}"
  fi

  local reboot=0 latest running
  running=$(uname -r)
  if [[ -f /var/run/reboot-required ]]; then
    reboot=1
    pending=$(tr '\n' ' ' < /var/run/reboot-required.pkgs 2>/dev/null || true)
    warn "reboot required after package updates${pending:+ (${pending% })}"
  fi
  if (( QUICK == 0 && LIMITED == 0 && reboot == 0 )); then
    local out rc=0 cmd=""
    if have needs-restarting; then cmd="needs-restarting"
    elif have dnf; then cmd="dnf needs-restarting"; fi
    if [[ -n "$cmd" ]]; then
      # shellcheck disable=SC2086
      out=$(timeout 60 $cmd -r 2>&1 </dev/null) || rc=$?
      if (( rc == 0 )); then
        ok "no reboot required (${cmd} -r)"
      elif (( rc == 1 )) && grep -qi 'reboot is required' <<<"$out"; then
        reboot=1
        warn "reboot required (${cmd} -r): core libraries or the kernel were updated since boot"
      else
        skip "${cmd} -r not usable (exit ${rc}), reboot state unknown"
      fi
    fi
  fi
  if have rpm; then
    latest=$(rpm -q kernel-core --qf '%{VERSION}-%{RELEASE}.%{ARCH}\n' 2>/dev/null | grep -v 'not installed' | sort -V | tail -1 || true)
    if [[ -z "$latest" ]]; then
      info "kernel ${running} (kernel-core not found in rpm)"
    elif [[ "$latest" != "$running" ]]; then
      if (( reboot == 0 )); then warn "newer kernel installed (${latest}), running ${running}; reboot to apply"
      else info "kernel running ${running}, newest installed ${latest}"; fi
    else
      info "kernel ${running} is the newest installed"
    fi
  elif have dpkg-query && ls /boot/vmlinuz-* >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    latest=$(find /boot -maxdepth 1 -name 'vmlinuz-*' -printf '%f\n' | sed 's/^vmlinuz-//' | sort -V | tail -1)
    if [[ "$latest" != "$running" ]]; then info "kernel running ${running}, newest installed ${latest}"
    else info "kernel ${running} is the newest installed"; fi
  fi
  if (( LIMITED == 1 && reboot == 0 )); then
    info "needs-restarting skipped (needs root)"
  elif (( QUICK == 1 && reboot == 0 )); then
    info "needs-restarting skipped (quick mode)"
  fi
  return 0
}

check_serverinit() {
  local conf=/etc/server-init.conf failed=/var/log/server-init-failed.log
  [[ -r "$conf" || -r "$failed" ]] || return 0
  [[ -f "$conf" || -s "$failed" ]] || return 0
  section "Server setup"
  if [[ -f "$conf" && -r "$conf" ]]; then
    local vals admin
    vals=$(grep -E '^(ADMIN_USER|EXTRA_PORTS|INSTALL_DOCKER)=' "$conf" | tr '\n' ' ' || true)
    info "${conf}: ${vals:-no known settings}"
    admin=$(sed -nE 's/^ADMIN_USER=["]?([A-Za-z0-9_-]+).*/\1/p' "$conf" | tail -1 || true)
    if [[ -n "$admin" ]]; then
      if id "$admin" >/dev/null 2>&1; then ok "admin user ${admin} exists"
      else warn "admin user ${admin} from ${conf} does not exist"; fi
    fi
    if [[ -f /etc/ssh/sshd_config.d/00-hardening.conf ]]; then
      info "sshd hardening drop-in present (sshd_config.d/00-hardening.conf)"
    fi
  fi
  local ct=server-cleanup.timer res last
  if unit_exists "$ct"; then
    if systemctl is-active --quiet "$ct" 2>/dev/null; then
      last=$(systemctl show server-cleanup.service -p ExecMainExitTimestamp --value 2>/dev/null || true)
      res=$(systemctl show server-cleanup.service -p Result --value 2>/dev/null || true)
      if [[ -n "$res" && "$res" != success ]]; then warn "${ct} active, last run ${last:-unknown}: ${res} (journalctl -u server-cleanup.service)"
      else ok "${ct} active, last run ${last:-not yet}"; fi
    else
      warn "${ct} installed but not active"
    fi
  fi
  if [[ -s "$failed" && ! -r "$failed" ]]; then
    skip "${failed}: not readable without root"
  elif [[ -s "$failed" ]]; then
    local n last
    n=$(grep -c . "$failed" || true)
    last=$(tail -1 "$failed" | cut -c1-110 || true)
    warn "${n} failed setup step(s) in ${failed}; last: ${last}"
  elif [[ -f "$failed" ]]; then
    ok "no failed setup steps (${failed} is empty)"
  fi
  return 0
}

# Optional section: only for security tools that are installed (common/security-tools.sh). Reads existing reports and
# files only, runs no scan, so it is cheap and -q does not change it. Prints nothing when none is installed.
check_securitytools() {
  local shown=0 f idx n age newest sig
  hdr() { if (( shown == 0 )); then section "Security tools"; shown=1; fi; }

  if have lynis && [[ -r /var/log/lynis-report.dat ]]; then
    idx=$(sed -n 's/^hardening_index=//p' /var/log/lynis-report.dat 2>/dev/null | tail -1 || true)
    if isnum "$idx"; then
      hdr
      f=$(stat -c %Y /var/log/lynis-report.dat 2>/dev/null || echo 0)
      info "lynis hardening index ${idx} (last audit $(human_age $(( $(date +%s) - f ))) ago)"
    fi
  fi

  if have rkhunter; then
    f=$(ls -1t /var/log/security-audit/rkhunter-*.log 2>/dev/null | head -1 || true)
    if [[ -n "$f" && -r "$f" ]]; then
      hdr
      n=$(grep -c '^Warning' "$f" 2>/dev/null || true)
      age=$(human_age $(( $(date +%s) - $(stat -c %Y "$f" 2>/dev/null || echo 0) )))
      if [[ "${n:-0}" -gt 0 ]]; then warn "rkhunter: ${n} warning(s) in the last run (${age} ago), see ${f}"
      else ok "rkhunter: no warnings in the last run (${age} ago)"; fi
    fi
  fi

  if have aide; then
    f=/var/lib/security-tools/aide/aide.db.gz
    if [[ -e "$f" ]]; then
      hdr
      info "aide database from $(date -d "@$(stat -c %Y "$f" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)" +%F)"
    elif [[ -f /etc/security-tools/aide.conf ]]; then
      hdr
      warn "aide installed but its database is missing (aide --config /etc/security-tools/aide.conf --init)"
    fi
  fi

  if have freshclam || have clamscan; then
    newest=0
    for f in /var/lib/clamav/*.cvd /var/lib/clamav/*.cld; do
      [[ -e "$f" ]] || continue
      sig=$(stat -c %Y "$f" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)
      if (( sig > newest )); then newest=$sig; fi
    done
    hdr
    if (( newest == 0 )); then
      warn "clamav: no signature database (run freshclam)"
    else
      age=$(( ( $(date +%s) - newest ) / 86400 ))
      if (( age > CLAM_SIG_WARN_DAYS )); then warn "clamav signatures are ${age} days old (freshclam)"
      else ok "clamav signatures ${age} day(s) old"; fi
    fi
  fi

  if have auditctl && (( ! LIMITED )); then
    hdr
    if auditctl -s 2>/dev/null | grep -q '^enabled [12]'; then ok "auditd: audit enabled"
    else warn "auditd installed but audit is not enabled"; fi
  fi
  return 0
}

check_services() {
  section "Services"
  local u
  for u in sshd.service docker.service gitlab-runner.service; do
    if [[ "$u" == "gitlab-runner.service" ]] && ! is_runner; then continue; fi
    if ! unit_exists "$u"; then
      if [[ "$u" == "sshd.service" ]] && unit_exists ssh.service; then
        u=ssh.service
      elif is_runner; then
        skip "${u} not installed"
        continue
      else
        continue
      fi
    fi
    if systemctl is-active --quiet "$u" 2>/dev/null; then
      ok "${u} active"
    else
      fail "${u} $(systemctl is-active "$u" 2>/dev/null || true)"
    fi
  done

  local t next
  for t in runner-weekly-cleanup.timer runner-disk-guard.timer runner-engine-update.timer ci-image-prewarm.timer; do
    is_runner || break
    unit_exists "$t" || continue
    if systemctl is-active --quiet "$t" 2>/dev/null; then
      next=$(systemctl show "$t" -p NextElapseUSecRealtime --value 2>/dev/null || true)
      if [[ -n "$next" && "$next" != "n/a" ]]; then
        ok "${t} active, next run ${next}"
      else
        ok "${t} active"
      fi
    else
      warn "${t} not active"
    fi
  done

  local s started result
  for s in runner-weekly-cleanup.service runner-disk-guard.service ci-image-prewarm.service; do
    is_runner || break
    unit_exists "$s" || continue
    started=$(systemctl show "$s" -p ExecMainStartTimestamp --value 2>/dev/null || true)
    result=$(systemctl show "$s" -p Result --value 2>/dev/null || true)
    if [[ -z "$started" || "$started" == "n/a" ]]; then
      info "${s%.service}: not run since boot"
    elif [[ "$result" == "success" ]]; then
      ok "${s%.service}: last run ${started}"
    else
      warn "${s%.service}: last run ${started}, result ${result} (journalctl -u ${s})"
    fi
  done

  if is_runner && [[ -s /var/log/runner-engine-update.log ]]; then
    local upd
    upd=$(grep -E ' (OK|SKIP|FAIL|DRY-RUN) ' /var/log/runner-engine-update.log | tail -1 || true)
    case "$upd" in
      *" FAIL "*) warn "Docker/GitLab Runner update: ${upd}" ;;
      "") ;;
      *) info "Docker/GitLab Runner update: ${upd}" ;;
    esac
  fi
  if is_runner && unit_exists ci-job-isolation.service; then
    if systemctl is-active --quiet ci-job-isolation.service 2>/dev/null; then ok "ci-job-isolation active (metadata and host services closed to jobs)"; else warn "ci-job-isolation not active: jobs can reach the metadata service (systemctl restart ci-job-isolation)"; fi
  fi

  if is_runner && unit_exists ci-image-prewarm.service; then
    local summary noarm failed_n
    summary=$(journalctl -t ci-image-prewarm --since -2d --no-pager -o cat 2>/dev/null | grep 'summary:' | tail -1 || true)
    if [[ -n "$summary" ]]; then
      noarm=$(sed -nE 's/.*no_arm=([0-9]+).*/\1/p' <<<"$summary")
      failed_n=$(sed -nE 's/.*failed=([0-9]+).*/\1/p' <<<"$summary")
      if (( ${failed_n:-0} > 0 )); then
        warn "image prewarm: ${failed_n} failed pulls (journalctl -t ci-image-prewarm | grep FAIL)"
      elif (( ${noarm:-0} > 0 )); then
        warn "image prewarm: ${noarm} tags without arm64 (journalctl -t ci-image-prewarm | grep 'NO ARM')"
      else
        ok "image prewarm: ${summary#*summary: }"
      fi
    fi
  fi
  return 0
}

check_runner() {
  if ! is_runner; then
    skip "runner: not a runner host (skipped)"
    return 0
  fi
  if (( LIMITED )); then
    skip "runner: needs root (skipped)"
    return 0
  fi
  section "GitLab Runner"
  if ! have gitlab-runner; then
    skip "gitlab-runner not installed"
    return 0
  fi
  info "$(gitlab-runner --version 2>/dev/null | awk '/^Version:/ { print "version " $2 }')"

  if [[ ! -f "$CFG" ]]; then
    fail "${CFG} missing"
    return 0
  fi

  local n_runners conc rc perms
  n_runners=$(grep -c '^\[\[runners\]\]' "$CFG" || true)
  conc=$(awk -F' = ' '/^concurrent/ { print $2 }' "$CFG")
  info "${n_runners:-0} runner(s) in config, concurrent = ${conc:-?}"
  if (( ${n_runners:-0} == 0 )); then
    fail "no runners registered"
  fi
  rc=$(grep -c 'request_concurrency' "$CFG" || true)
  if (( ${n_runners:-0} > 0 && ${rc:-0} < n_runners )); then
    warn "request_concurrency missing for some runners (slow job pickup)"
  elif (( ${n_runners:-0} > 0 )); then
    ok "request_concurrency set"
  fi
  perms=$(stat -c '%a' "$CFG")
  if [[ "$perms" == "600" ]]; then
    ok "config.toml permissions 600"
  else
    warn "config.toml permissions ${perms}, expected 600 (contains tokens)"
  fi

  if (( QUICK == 0 )); then
    local v valid invalid
    v=$(timeout 30 gitlab-runner verify 2>&1 || true)
    valid=$(grep -c 'is valid' <<<"$v" || true)
    invalid=$(grep -cE 'is not valid|is removed' <<<"$v" || true)
    if (( ${invalid:-0} > 0 )); then
      fail "${invalid} runner(s) rejected by GitLab (token removed or invalid)"
    elif (( ${valid:-0} > 0 )); then
      ok "${valid} runner(s) verified with GitLab"
    else
      warn "could not verify runners with GitLab"
    fi
  fi

  local jobs
  jobs=$(docker ps -q --filter label=com.gitlab.gitlab-runner.managed=true 2>/dev/null | wc -l || true)
  info "running job containers: ${jobs:-0}"

  if grep -q '^listen_address' "$CFG"; then
    if [[ "$(http_code http://127.0.0.1:9252/metrics 3)" == "200" ]]; then
      ok "metrics endpoint 127.0.0.1:9252"
    else
      fail "metrics endpoint 127.0.0.1:9252 not responding"
    fi
  fi

  local errs
  errs=$(journalctl -u gitlab-runner --since -1h --no-pager -o cat 2>/dev/null | grep -cE 'ERROR|level=error' || true)
  if (( ${errs:-0} > 0 )); then
    warn "${errs} error lines in runner log in the last hour (journalctl -u gitlab-runner --since -1h | grep ERROR)"
  else
    ok "no runner errors in the last hour"
  fi
  return 0
}

check_docker() {
  if ! is_runner && ! have docker; then
    skip "docker: not installed"
    return 0
  fi
  section "Docker"
  if ! have docker; then
    skip "docker not installed"
    return 0
  fi
  if (( LIMITED )) && ! docker info >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    skip "docker: no access (not root, not in the docker group)"
    return 0
  fi
  if ! docker info >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    fail "docker daemon not responding"
    return 0
  fi
  info "version $(docker version -f '{{.Server.Version}}' 2>/dev/null || echo unknown)"

  local mirrors
  mirrors=$(docker info -f '{{range .RegistryConfig.Mirrors}}{{.}} {{end}}' 2>/dev/null || true)
  if [[ -n "${mirrors// /}" ]]; then
    info "registry mirrors: ${mirrors}"
  fi

  local line
  while IFS= read -r line; do
    info "$line"
  done < <(docker system df --format '{{.Type}}: {{.Size}} ({{.Reclaimable}} reclaimable)' 2>/dev/null || true)
  return 0
}

check_containers() {
  if ! is_runner && ! have docker; then
    return 0
  fi
  section "Containers"
  if ! have docker || ! docker info >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    skip "docker not available (or no access)"
    return 0
  fi

  local -a ids
  mapfile -t ids < <(docker ps -q 2>/dev/null || true)
  if (( ${#ids[@]} == 0 )); then
    skip "no running containers"
    return 0
  fi

  local cores cpu_limit now
  cores=$(nproc)
  cpu_limit=$(( cores * 90 ))
  now=$(date +%s)

  local -A s_cpu s_mem s_memp s_pids
  local name cpu mem memp pids
  while IFS=$'\t' read -r name cpu mem memp pids; do
    [[ -z "$name" ]] && continue
    s_cpu[$name]="${cpu%\%}"
    s_mem[$name]="$mem"
    s_memp[$name]="${memp%\%}"
    s_pids[$name]="$pids"
  done < <(docker stats --no-stream --format '{{.Name}}\t{{.CPUPerc}}\t{{.MemUsage}}\t{{.MemPerc}}\t{{.PIDs}}' "${ids[@]}" 2>/dev/null || true)

  local tpl='{{.Name}}|{{.State.Status}}|{{if .State.Health}}{{.State.Health.Status}}{{else}}none{{end}}|{{.RestartCount}}|{{.State.OOMKilled}}|{{.State.StartedAt}}|{{.HostConfig.Memory}}|{{index .Config.Labels "com.gitlab.gitlab-runner.managed"}}'
  local state health restarts oom started memlimit job started_s age short details level reasons
  local n_total=0 n_jobs=0

  while IFS='|' read -r name state health restarts oom started memlimit job; do
    [[ -z "$name" ]] && continue
    name="${name#/}"
    n_total=$((n_total + 1))
    short="$name"
    if (( ${#short} > 30 )); then short="${short:0:27}..."; fi

    started_s=$(date -d "$started" +%s 2>/dev/null || echo "$now")
    age=$(human_age $(( now - started_s )))
    cpu="${s_cpu[$name]:-?}"
    mem="${s_mem[$name]:-?}"
    memp="${s_memp[$name]:-?}"
    pids="${s_pids[$name]:-?}"

    details="up ${age} | cpu ${cpu}% | mem ${mem}"
    if [[ "${memlimit:-0}" == "0" ]]; then details+=" (no limit)"; else details+=" (${memp}%)"; fi
    details+=" | pids ${pids} | health ${health} | restarts ${restarts}"
    if [[ "$job" == "true" ]]; then
      details="CI job | ${details}"
      n_jobs=$((n_jobs + 1))
    fi

    level=ok
    reasons=""
    if [[ "$state" != "running" ]]; then level=fail; reasons+=" state ${state};"; fi
    if [[ "$oom" == "true" ]]; then level=fail; reasons+=" OOM-killed;"; fi
    case "$health" in
      unhealthy) level=fail; reasons+=" healthcheck failing;" ;;
      starting) if [[ "$level" == ok ]]; then level=warn; fi; reasons+=" healthcheck starting;" ;;
    esac
    if (( restarts > 3 )); then
      if [[ "$level" == ok ]]; then level=warn; fi
      reasons+=" ${restarts} restarts;"
    fi
    if [[ "${memlimit:-0}" != "0" ]] && isnum "$memp"; then
      if gt "$memp" 95; then level=fail; reasons+=" memory at limit;"
      elif gt "$memp" 85; then
        if [[ "$level" == ok ]]; then level=warn; fi
        reasons+=" memory near limit;"
      fi
    fi
    if [[ "$job" != "true" ]] && isnum "$cpu" && gt "$cpu" "$cpu_limit"; then
      if [[ "$level" == ok ]]; then level=warn; fi
      reasons+=" high CPU;"
    fi

    case "$level" in
      ok)   ok   "$(printf '%-30s' "$short") ${details}" ;;
      warn) warn "$(printf '%-30s' "$short") ${details} |${reasons%;}" ;;
      fail) fail "$(printf '%-30s' "$short") ${details} |${reasons%;}" ;;
    esac
  done < <(docker inspect -f "$tpl" "${ids[@]}" 2>/dev/null || true)

  if is_runner; then
    info "${n_total} running containers, ${n_jobs} of them CI jobs"
  else
    info "${n_total} running containers"
  fi
  return 0
}

check_caches() {
  if ! is_runner; then
    local d any=0
    for d in /opt/registry-cache /opt/npm-cache /opt/monitoring /opt/logging; do
      [[ -d "$d" ]] && any=1
    done
    if (( any == 0 )); then
      skip "caches, monitoring, logging: not installed"
      return 0
    fi
  fi
  section "Caches and monitoring"
  local c
  if [[ -d /opt/registry-cache ]]; then
    c=$(http_code http://127.0.0.1:5000/v2/ 3)
    if [[ "$c" == "200" ]]; then ok "Docker Hub cache 127.0.0.1:5000"
    else fail "Docker Hub cache not responding (HTTP ${c:-000}), pulls fall back to Docker Hub"; fi
  else
    is_runner && skip "Docker Hub cache not installed"
  fi

  if [[ -d /opt/npm-cache ]]; then
    local bip
    bip=$(ip -4 -o addr show docker0 2>/dev/null | awk '{ print $4 }' | cut -d/ -f1 || true)
    c=$(http_code "http://${bip:-172.17.0.1}:4873/-/ping" 3)
    if [[ "$c" == "200" ]]; then ok "npm cache (Verdaccio) ${bip:-172.17.0.1}:4873"
    else fail "npm cache not responding (HTTP ${c:-000}), npm ci in jobs will fail"; fi
  else
    is_runner && skip "npm cache (Verdaccio) not installed"
  fi

  if [[ -d /srv/ci-cache ]]; then
    info "shared cache /srv/ci-cache: $(du -sh /srv/ci-cache 2>/dev/null | cut -f1)"
  fi

  if [[ -d /opt/monitoring ]]; then
    if [[ "$(http_code "${PROM_URL}/-/ready" 3)" == "200" ]]; then ok "Prometheus ready"
    else fail "Prometheus not ready"; fi
    if [[ "$(http_code http://127.0.0.1:3000/api/health 3)" == "200" ]]; then ok "Grafana healthy"
    else fail "Grafana not healthy"; fi
    if have jq; then
      local down firing
      down=$(curl -fsS -m 5 "${PROM_URL}/api/v1/targets" 2>/dev/null \
        | jq -r '.data.activeTargets[] | select(.health != "up") | .labels.job' 2>/dev/null | tr '\n' ' ' || true)
      if [[ -n "${down// /}" ]]; then fail "Prometheus targets down: ${down}"
      else ok "all Prometheus targets up"; fi
      firing=$(curl -fsS -m 5 "${PROM_URL}/api/v1/alerts" 2>/dev/null \
        | jq -r '.data.alerts[] | select(.state == "firing") | .labels.alertname' 2>/dev/null | sort -u | tr '\n' ' ' || true)
      if [[ -n "${firing// /}" ]]; then warn "alerts firing: ${firing}"
      else ok "no alerts firing"; fi
    fi
  else
    is_runner && skip "monitoring stack not installed"
  fi

  if [[ -d /opt/logging ]]; then
    if [[ "$(http_code http://127.0.0.1:3100/ready 3)" == "200" ]]; then ok "Loki ready"
    else fail "Loki not ready, logs are not stored"; fi
    if [[ "$(http_code http://127.0.0.1:12345/-/ready 3)" == "200" ]]; then ok "Alloy ready"
    else fail "Alloy not ready, logs are not collected"; fi
  else
    is_runner && skip "logging (Loki + Alloy) not installed"
  fi
  return 0
}

check_url() {
  local name="$1" url="$2" expect="$3" out code t
  out=$(curl -s -o /dev/null -m 10 -w '%{http_code} %{time_total}' "$url" 2>/dev/null || true)
  code="${out%% *}"
  t="${out##* }"
  if [[ " ${expect} " == *" ${code} "* ]]; then
    if gt "${t:-0}" 3; then warn "${name} slow (${t}s)"
    else ok "${name} reachable (HTTP ${code}, ${t}s)"; fi
  else
    fail "${name} unreachable (HTTP ${code:-000})"
  fi
}

check_network() {
  (( QUICK == 1 )) && return 0
  section "Network"
  local h unresolved="" hosts
  if is_runner; then
    hosts="gitlab.com registry.gitlab.com registry-1.docker.io registry.npmjs.org"
  else
    hosts="cloudflare.com"
    have docker && hosts+=" registry-1.docker.io"
  fi
  for h in $hosts; do
    getent ahostsv4 "$h" >/dev/null 2>&1 || unresolved="${unresolved}${h} "
  done
  if [[ -n "$unresolved" ]]; then fail "DNS cannot resolve: ${unresolved}"
  elif is_runner; then ok "DNS resolves GitLab, Docker Hub and npm"
  else ok "DNS resolves ${hosts}"; fi

  if is_runner; then
    check_url "gitlab.com API" "https://gitlab.com/api/v4/version" "200 401"
    check_url "GitLab registry" "https://registry.gitlab.com/v2/" "200 401"
    check_url "Docker Hub" "https://registry-1.docker.io/v2/" "200 401"
    check_url "npm registry" "https://registry.npmjs.org/" "200"
  else
    check_url "internet (HTTPS)" "https://cloudflare.com/" "200 301 302 403"
    if have docker; then check_url "Docker Hub" "https://registry-1.docker.io/v2/" "200 401"; fi
  fi

  if grep -qE '^precedence ::ffff:0:0/96' /etc/gai.conf 2>/dev/null; then
    info "IPv4 preferred over IPv6 (gai.conf)"
  fi
  return 0
}

check_trends() {
  is_runner || return 0
  section "Trends (Prometheus, last 24h)"
  if ! have jq; then
    skip "jq not installed, trend checks skipped"
    return 0
  fi
  if [[ "$(http_code "${PROM_URL}/-/ready" 3)" != "200" ]]; then
    skip "Prometheus not available, trend checks skipped"
    return 0
  fi

  local lowest hist_h
  lowest=$(prom_q 'min(prometheus_tsdb_lowest_timestamp_seconds)')
  if isnum "$lowest"; then
    hist_h=$(awk -v n="$(date +%s)" -v l="$lowest" 'BEGIN { printf "%.0f", (n - l) / 3600 }')
    if (( hist_h < 24 )); then
      info "metrics history: ${hist_h}h (less than 24h, trends are approximate)"
    else
      info "metrics history: $(( hist_h / 24 ))d $(( hist_h % 24 ))h"
    fi
  fi

  # A forecast from minutes of data mostly measures the install itself (image pulls, package updates),
  # so it needs FORECAST_MIN_HISTORY_H hours of metrics before it can warn or fail.
  local avail deriv days
  avail=$(prom_q 'node_filesystem_avail_bytes{mountpoint="/",fstype!="rootfs"}')
  deriv=$(prom_q 'deriv(node_filesystem_avail_bytes{mountpoint="/",fstype!="rootfs"}[12h])')
  if ! isnum "${hist_h:-}" || (( hist_h < FORECAST_MIN_HISTORY_H )); then
    skip "disk forecast: needs ${FORECAST_MIN_HISTORY_H}h of metrics (have ${hist_h:-0}h)"
  elif isnum "$avail" && isnum "$deriv"; then
    if awk -v d="$deriv" 'BEGIN { exit !(d < 0) }'; then
      days=$(awk -v a="$avail" -v d="$deriv" 'BEGIN { printf "%.1f", a / (-d) / 86400 }')
      if gt 3 "$days"; then
        fail "disk will be full in ~${days} days at the current rate"
      elif gt 14 "$days"; then
        warn "disk will be full in ~${days} days at the current rate"
      else
        ok "disk forecast: full in ~${days} days at the current rate"
      fi
    else
      ok "disk usage not growing over the last 12h"
    fi
  else
    skip "disk forecast: not enough data yet"
  fi

  local mem_peak p
  mem_peak=$(prom_q 'max_over_time(((1 - node_memory_MemAvailable_bytes / node_memory_MemTotal_bytes) * 100)[24h:1m])')
  if isnum "$mem_peak"; then
    p=$(num "$mem_peak" 0)
    if gt "$p" 95; then fail "memory peaked at ${p}% (OOM risk during builds)"
    elif gt "$p" 85; then warn "memory peaked at ${p}% (close to the limit during builds)"
    else ok "memory peak ${p}%"; fi
  else
    skip "memory peak: no data"
  fi

  local cpu_avg cpu_peak steal
  cpu_avg=$(prom_q 'avg_over_time((100 - avg(rate(node_cpu_seconds_total{mode="idle"}[5m])) * 100)[24h:5m])')
  cpu_peak=$(prom_q 'max_over_time((100 - avg(rate(node_cpu_seconds_total{mode="idle"}[5m])) * 100)[24h:5m])')
  if isnum "$cpu_avg" && isnum "$cpu_peak"; then
    info "CPU average $(num "$cpu_avg" 0)%, peak $(num "$cpu_peak" 0)%"
  fi
  steal=$(prom_q 'avg(rate(node_cpu_seconds_total{mode="steal"}[24h])) * 100')
  if isnum "$steal"; then
    if gt "$steal" 10; then
      warn "CPU steal $(num "$steal" 1)% (other VMs on the host slow your builds)"
    else
      ok "CPU steal $(num "$steal" 1)%"
    fi
  fi

  local up_pct
  up_pct=$(prom_q 'avg_over_time(up{job="gitlab-runner"}[24h]) * 100')
  if isnum "$up_pct"; then
    if gt 99 "$up_pct"; then warn "runner metrics available $(num "$up_pct" 1)% of the last 24h"
    else ok "runner available $(num "$up_pct" 1)% of the last 24h"; fi
  fi

  local total
  total=$(prom_q 'sum(increase(gitlab_runner_jobs_total[24h]))')
  if isnum "$total"; then
    info "jobs in the last 24h: $(num "$total" 0)"
  else
    info "jobs in the last 24h: none recorded"
  fi

  local reason n infra=0 line
  while IFS= read -r line; do
    [[ -z "$line" ]] && continue
    reason="${line%%=*}"
    n=$(num "${line##*=}" 0)
    (( n == 0 )) && continue
    if [[ "$reason" == "script_failure" ]]; then
      info "${n} jobs failed in their scripts (build or tests, not the server)"
    else
      warn "${n} jobs failed with ${reason:-unknown reason} (server or runner side)"
      infra=1
    fi
  done < <(prom_qv 'sum by (failure_reason) (increase(gitlab_runner_failed_jobs_total[24h])) > 0')
  if (( infra == 0 )); then
    ok "no server-side job failures in the last 24h"
  fi

  local p95 base
  p95=$(prom_q 'histogram_quantile(0.95, sum by (le) (rate(gitlab_runner_job_duration_seconds_bucket[24h])))')
  base=$(prom_q 'histogram_quantile(0.95, sum by (le) (rate(gitlab_runner_job_duration_seconds_bucket[7d] offset 1d)))')
  if isnum "$p95"; then
    if isnum "$base" && gt "$base" 0 && gt "$p95" "$(awk -v b="$base" 'BEGIN { print b * 1.5 }')"; then
      warn "job duration p95 $(num "$p95" 0)s, usual $(num "$base" 0)s (1.5x slower)"
    elif isnum "$base"; then
      ok "job duration p95 $(num "$p95" 0)s (usual $(num "$base" 0)s)"
    else
      ok "job duration p95 $(num "$p95" 0)s"
    fi
  else
    skip "job duration: no jobs with timing data yet"
  fi

  local q95
  q95=$(prom_q 'histogram_quantile(0.95, sum by (le) (rate(gitlab_runner_job_queue_duration_seconds_bucket[24h])))')
  if isnum "$q95"; then
    if gt "$q95" 60; then
      warn "jobs waited up to $(num "$q95" 0)s (p95) before pickup"
    else
      ok "job pickup wait p95 $(num "$q95" 0)s"
    fi
  else
    skip "job pickup wait: metric not available in this runner version or no jobs yet"
  fi

  local fired
  fired=$(prom_qv 'count by (alertname) (max_over_time(ALERTS{alertstate="firing"}[24h]))' | cut -d= -f1 | sort -u | tr '\n' ' ' || true)
  if [[ -n "${fired// /}" ]]; then
    warn "alerts fired in the last 24h: ${fired}"
  else
    ok "no alerts fired in the last 24h"
  fi
  return 0
}

# ---- brief mode: a short summary for the login screen and `srv status` -------------------------------------------
BRIEF_DIR="${HEALTH_BRIEF_DIR:-/var/lib/server-health}"
UPDATE_STATE="${SERVER_UPDATE_STATE:-/var/lib/server-update}/state"
CLEANUP_STATE="${SERVER_CLEANUP_STATE:-/var/lib/server-cleanup}"

# kv FILE KEY: value of key=value in a one-line state file (empty when missing)
kv() { sed -nE "s/(^|.* )$2=([^ ]*).*/\2/p" "$1" 2>/dev/null | head -n1 || true; }
# lvl VALUE WARN FAIL: marker for a value where higher is worse
lvl() {
  if (( $1 >= $3 )); then printf ' %sFAIL%s' "$C_FAIL" "$C_N"; elif (( $1 >= $2 )); then printf ' %sWARN%s' "$C_WARN" "$C_N"; fi
  return 0
}
ago() { # ago EPOCH: "3d4h ago" or "never"
  if [[ "${1:-}" =~ ^[0-9]+$ && "$1" -gt 0 ]]; then echo "$(human_age $(( $(date +%s) - $1 ))) ago"; else echo never; fi
}

brief_full() { # run the full report when the last one is older than 6 hours; keep its counts for the summary
  local f="$BRIEF_DIR/full" t=0 out w fl rc=0
  [[ -r "$f" ]] && t=$(kv "$f" time)
  [[ "$t" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]] || t=0
  (( $(date +%s) - t >= 21600 )) || return 0
  out=$(HEALTH_REEXEC=1 "$(readlink -f "$0")" --no-color 2>&1) || rc=$?
  ( umask 077; printf '%s\n' "$out" > "$BRIEF_DIR/last-full.log" )
  w=$(sed -nE 's/^ +[0-9]+ OK +([0-9]+) WARN +([0-9]+) FAIL.*/\1/p' <<<"$out" | tail -n1 || true)
  fl=$(sed -nE 's/^ +[0-9]+ OK +([0-9]+) WARN +([0-9]+) FAIL.*/\2/p' <<<"$out" | tail -n1 || true)
  printf 'time=%s warn=%s fail=%s\n' "$(date +%s)" "${w:-0}" "${fl:-0}" > "$f.tmp" && mv -f "$f.tmp" "$f"
  if (( ${fl:-0} > 0 )) && [[ -x "${SERVER_SBIN:-/usr/local/sbin}/server-notify" ]]; then
    "${SERVER_SBIN:-/usr/local/sbin}/server-notify" health "health: ${fl} FAIL, ${w:-0} WARN; $(grep -E '^ +FAIL' <<<"$out" | head -n2 | tr -s ' ' | tr '\n' ';' | cut -c1-300)" || true
  fi
  return 0
}

brief_render() {
  local up_s d h load dp mp mem_total mem_avail fw="" f2b="" s u r w fl st
  up_s=$(cut -d. -f1 /proc/uptime 2>/dev/null || echo 0); d=$(( up_s / 86400 )); h=$(( up_s % 86400 / 3600 ))
  load=$(cut -d' ' -f1-3 /proc/loadavg 2>/dev/null || echo "?")
  printf '%shost%s  %s  up %sd%sh  load %s  (as of %s)\n' "$C_B" "$C_N" "$(uname -n)" "$d" "$h" "$load" "$(date +%H:%M)"
  dp=$(df -P / | awk 'NR == 2 { gsub(/%/, "", $5); print $5 }')
  mem_total=$(awk '/^MemTotal:/ { print $2 }' /proc/meminfo); mem_avail=$(awk '/^MemAvailable:/ { print $2 }' /proc/meminfo)
  mp=$(( mem_total > 0 ? 100 * mem_avail / mem_total : 100 ))
  printf '%sdisk%s  / %s%% used%s   mem %s%% free%s\n' "$C_B" "$C_N" "$dp" "$(lvl "$dp" "$DISK_WARN" "$DISK_FAIL")" "$mp" \
    "$( if (( mp <= MEM_FAIL )); then lvl 1 2 1; elif (( mp <= MEM_WARN )); then lvl 1 1 2; fi )"
  if (( LIMITED )); then
    printf '%sfw%s    unknown (needs root)\n' "$C_B" "$C_N"
  else
    detect_firewall
    case "$FW_KIND:$FW_STATE" in
      firewalld:active) fw="firewalld active, open: $(firewall-cmd --list-services --list-ports 2>/dev/null | tr -s ' \n' ' ' | sed 's/ $//')" ;;
      ufw:active) fw="ufw active" ;;
      none:*) fw="none installed $(lvl 1 1 2)" ;;
      *) fw="$FW_KIND $FW_STATE $(lvl 1 2 1)" ;;
    esac
    printf '%sfw%s    %s\n' "$C_B" "$C_N" "$fw"
    if have fail2ban-client && st=$(fail2ban-client status sshd 2>/dev/null); then
      f2b=$(sed -nE 's/^[^C]*Currently banned:[[:space:]]*([0-9]+).*/\1/p' <<<"$st" | head -n1 || true)
      printf '%sf2b%s   %s banned now (sshd jail)\n' "$C_B" "$C_N" "${f2b:-0}"
    elif have fail2ban-client || unit_exists fail2ban.service; then
      printf '%sf2b%s   not running %s\n' "$C_B" "$C_N" "$(lvl 1 1 2)"
    fi
  fi
  if [[ -r "$UPDATE_STATE" ]]; then
    u=$(kv "$UPDATE_STATE" updates); r=$(kv "$UPDATE_STATE" reboot)
    printf '%supd%s   %s update(s) pending, reboot needed: %s%s  (checked %s)\n' "$C_B" "$C_N" "${u:-?}" "$( [[ "$r" == 1 ]] && echo yes || echo no )" \
      "$( [[ "$r" == 1 ]] && lvl 1 1 2 )" "$(ago "$(kv "$UPDATE_STATE" time)")"
    s="update $(ago "$(kv "$UPDATE_STATE" updated)") ($(kv "$UPDATE_STATE" uresult || true))"
  else
    printf '%supd%s   unknown: run `srv update --check`\n' "$C_B" "$C_N"
    s="update never"
  fi
  printf '%slast%s  %s, cleanup %s (%s)\n' "$C_B" "$C_N" "$s" "$(ago "$(cat "$CLEANUP_STATE/last-run" 2>/dev/null || true)")" "$(cat "$CLEANUP_STATE/last-result" 2>/dev/null || echo none)"
  if [[ -r "$BRIEF_DIR/full" ]]; then
    w=$(kv "$BRIEF_DIR/full" warn); fl=$(kv "$BRIEF_DIR/full" fail)
    printf '%scheck%s full report %s: %s FAIL, %s WARN%s\n' "$C_B" "$C_N" "$(ago "$(kv "$BRIEF_DIR/full" time)")" "${fl:-0}" "${w:-0}" \
      "$( if (( ${fl:-0} > 0 )); then printf ' %s-> run: srv health%s' "$C_FAIL" "$C_N"; elif (( ${w:-0} > 0 )); then printf ' %s-> run: srv health%s' "$C_WARN" "$C_N"; fi )"
  else
    printf '%scheck%s no full report yet (health, or wait for the timer)\n' "$C_B" "$C_N"
  fi
}

brief_main() {
  if (( WRITE )); then
    (( LIMITED == 0 )) || { echo "health --brief --write needs root" >&2; exit 1; }
    C_OK=""; C_WARN=""; C_FAIL=""; C_B=""; C_D=""; C_N=""
    mkdir -p "$BRIEF_DIR" && chmod 0755 "$BRIEF_DIR"
    brief_full
    local tmp; tmp=$(mktemp "$BRIEF_DIR/.brief.XXXXXX")
    brief_render > "$tmp"
    chmod 0644 "$tmp"; mv -f "$tmp" "$BRIEF_DIR/brief.txt"
  else
    brief_render
  fi
  exit 0
}

if (( BRIEF == 1 )); then brief_main; fi

detect_profile
detect_firewall

if (( ONLY_CONTAINERS == 1 )); then
  printf '%scontainer health%s  %s | %s | profile: %s\n' "$C_B" "$C_N" "$(uname -n)" "$(date '+%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S %Z')" "$PROFILE"
  check_containers
  print_summary_and_exit
fi

printf '%shealth report%s  %s | %s | profile: %s\n' "$C_B" "$C_N" "$(uname -n)" "$(date '+%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S %Z')" "$PROFILE"
if (( LIMITED )); then info "limited mode: not root and sudo needs a password; root-only checks skipped"; fi
if (( RUNNER_UNREGISTERED == 1 )); then info "gitlab-runner is installed but no runner is registered: checked as a plain server (--profile runner to check it)"; fi
if (( RUNNER_UNREGISTERED == 2 )); then info "runner config not readable without root: checked as a plain server (run as root or use --profile runner)"; fi
check_system
check_security
check_firewall
check_updates
check_serverinit
check_securitytools
check_services
check_runner
check_docker
check_containers
check_caches
check_network
check_trends
print_summary_and_exit
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 755 "$R/common/health"

install -d "$R/runner/scripts"
cat > "$R/runner/scripts/runner-weekly-cleanup.sh" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Weekly cleanup of the GitLab Runner host: Docker, runner caches, packages, logs, temp files.
set -Eeuo pipefail

RETENTION_DAYS="${RETENTION_DAYS:-7}"
JOURNAL_MAX="${JOURNAL_MAX:-500M}"
SHARED_CACHE_DIR="${SHARED_CACHE_DIR:-/srv/ci-cache}"
SHARED_CACHE_MAX_AGE_DAYS="${SHARED_CACHE_MAX_AGE_DAYS:-14}"
DRY_RUN="${DRY_RUN:-0}"

CACHE_DIRS=(/home/gitlab-runner/cache /home/gitlab-runner/.cache /var/lib/gitlab-runner/cache /root/.cache)
BUILD_DIRS=(/home/gitlab-runner/builds /var/lib/gitlab-runner/builds)

log()  { logger -t runner-weekly-cleanup -- "$*"; echo "$(date -u +%FT%TZ) $*"; }
disk() { df --output=used,avail,pcent -h / | tail -1 | xargs; }

run() {
  log "-> $*"
  [[ "$DRY_RUN" == "1" ]] && return 0
  "$@" || log "WARN: failed: $*"
}

wipe_dir() {
  local dir="$1" age="${2:-}"
  [[ -d "$dir" ]] || return 0
  if [[ -n "$age" ]]; then
    log "-> cleaning $dir older than ${age}d"
    [[ "$DRY_RUN" == "1" ]] && return 0
    find "$dir" -mindepth 1 -mtime +"$age" -delete 2>/dev/null || log "WARN: partial cleanup of $dir"
  else
    log "-> wiping $dir"
    [[ "$DRY_RUN" == "1" ]] && return 0
    find "$dir" -mindepth 1 -delete 2>/dev/null || log "WARN: partial wipe of $dir"
  fi
}

usage() {
  cat <<USAGE
Usage: $0 [--dry-run] [--help]
  --dry-run                   same as DRY_RUN=1: show what would be cleaned without deleting anything
Env:
  RETENTION_DAYS              keep images, builds and temp files newer than N days (default: 7)
  JOURNAL_MAX                 max systemd journal size (default: 500M)
  SHARED_CACHE_DIR            shared CI cache directory (default: /srv/ci-cache)
  SHARED_CACHE_MAX_AGE_DAYS   prune shared cache files not accessed for N days (default: 14)
  DRY_RUN=1                   show what would be cleaned without deleting anything
USAGE
}

while (( $# )); do
  case "$1" in
    -h|--help) usage; exit 0 ;;
    -n|--dry-run) DRY_RUN=1 ;;
    *) usage >&2; echo "unknown option: $1" >&2; exit 2 ;;
  esac
  shift
done
[[ $EUID -eq 0 ]] || { echo "run as root" >&2; exit 1; }

exec 9>/run/runner-weekly-cleanup.lock
flock -n 9 || { log "another cleanup is running, exit"; exit 0; }

hours=$(( RETENTION_DAYS * 24 ))
log "start (dry_run=${DRY_RUN}), disk before: $(disk)"

if command -v docker >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  # Never guess the job count: if docker does not answer, a default of 0 would wipe the caches under running jobs.
  # stderr goes to a temp file so a docker WARNING is never counted as a running job.
  ps_err=$(mktemp)
  trap 'rm -f "$ps_err"' EXIT
  if ! job_ids=$(docker ps -q --filter "label=com.gitlab.gitlab-runner.managed=true" 2>"$ps_err"); then
    log "ERROR: docker is not responding, cleanup skipped: $(cat "$ps_err")"
    exit 1
  fi
  running=$(wc -w <<<"$job_ids")
  if (( running > 0 )); then
    log "WARN: ${running} job container(s) running, skipping cache wipe"
  else
    run /usr/share/gitlab-runner/clear-docker-cache prune-volumes
    if [[ "$DRY_RUN" != "1" ]]; then
      docker volume ls -q --filter name=runner- | xargs -r docker volume rm >/dev/null 2>&1 || log "WARN: some runner volumes in use"
    fi
    for dir in "${CACHE_DIRS[@]}"; do wipe_dir "$dir"; done
  fi
  run docker container prune -f --filter "until=${hours}h"
  run docker image prune -af --filter "until=${hours}h"
  run docker builder prune -af --filter "until=${hours}h"
  run docker network prune -f --filter "until=${hours}h"
fi

for dir in "${BUILD_DIRS[@]}"; do wipe_dir "$dir" "$RETENTION_DAYS"; done

if command -v apt-get >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  run apt-get -o DPkg::Lock::Timeout=600 autoremove --purge -y -q
  run apt-get -o DPkg::Lock::Timeout=600 autoclean -q
  run apt-get -o DPkg::Lock::Timeout=600 clean -q
elif command -v dnf >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  run dnf -y -q autoremove
  run dnf -q clean all
fi

run journalctl --vacuum-time="$(( RETENTION_DAYS * 2 ))d" --vacuum-size="$JOURNAL_MAX"

if [[ "$DRY_RUN" != "1" ]]; then
  log "-> removing rotated logs older than 30d"
  find /var/log -type f \( -name '*.gz' -o -name '*-[0-9]*' -o -name '*.[0-9]' -o -name '*.old' \) -mtime +30 -delete 2>/dev/null || true
  find /tmp /var/tmp -xdev -mindepth 1 -type f -atime +"$RETENTION_DAYS" -delete 2>/dev/null || true
fi
run systemd-tmpfiles --clean

if [[ -d "$SHARED_CACHE_DIR" && "$DRY_RUN" != "1" ]]; then
  log "-> pruning ${SHARED_CACHE_DIR} files not used for ${SHARED_CACHE_MAX_AGE_DAYS}d"
  find "$SHARED_CACHE_DIR" -xdev -type f -atime +"$SHARED_CACHE_MAX_AGE_DAYS" -delete 2>/dev/null || true
  find "$SHARED_CACHE_DIR" -xdev -mindepth 2 -type d -empty -delete 2>/dev/null || true
fi

log "done, disk after: $(disk)"
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 755 "$R/runner/scripts/runner-weekly-cleanup.sh"

install -d "$R/runner/scripts"
cat > "$R/runner/scripts/runner-disk-guard.sh" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Emergency cleanup when the root filesystem crosses HARD_LIMIT percent.
set -Eeuo pipefail

HARD_LIMIT="${HARD_LIMIT:-85}"

if [[ "${1:-}" == "-h" || "${1:-}" == "--help" ]]; then
  echo "Usage: $0  (env: HARD_LIMIT=85) runs runner-weekly-cleanup.sh with RETENTION_DAYS=1 when disk usage >= HARD_LIMIT%"
  exit 0
fi

used=$(df --output=pcent / | tail -1 | tr -dc '0-9')
if (( used >= HARD_LIMIT )); then
  logger -t runner-disk-guard -- "disk ${used}% >= ${HARD_LIMIT}%, running emergency cleanup"
  RETENTION_DAYS=1 /usr/local/bin/runner-weekly-cleanup.sh
fi
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 755 "$R/runner/scripts/runner-disk-guard.sh"

install -d "$R/runner/systemd"
cat > "$R/runner/systemd/runner-weekly-cleanup.service" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
[Unit]
Description=Weekly cleanup of GitLab Runner host
After=docker.service

[Service]
Type=oneshot
ExecStart=/usr/local/bin/runner-weekly-cleanup.sh
Nice=10
IOSchedulingClass=idle
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 644 "$R/runner/systemd/runner-weekly-cleanup.service"

install -d "$R/runner/systemd"
cat > "$R/runner/systemd/runner-weekly-cleanup.timer" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
[Unit]
Description=Run runner cleanup every Monday

[Timer]
OnCalendar=Mon *-*-* 03:30:00
Persistent=true
RandomizedDelaySec=10m

[Install]
WantedBy=timers.target
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 644 "$R/runner/systemd/runner-weekly-cleanup.timer"

install -d "$R/runner/systemd"
cat > "$R/runner/systemd/runner-disk-guard.service" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
[Unit]
Description=Emergency disk cleanup when usage is high
After=docker.service

[Service]
Type=oneshot
ExecStart=/usr/local/bin/runner-disk-guard.sh
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 644 "$R/runner/systemd/runner-disk-guard.service"

install -d "$R/runner/systemd"
cat > "$R/runner/systemd/runner-disk-guard.timer" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
[Unit]
Description=Check disk usage every 30 minutes

[Timer]
OnBootSec=10m
OnUnitActiveSec=30m

[Install]
WantedBy=timers.target
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 644 "$R/runner/systemd/runner-disk-guard.timer"

install -d "$R/runner/scripts"
cat > "$R/runner/scripts/runner-engine-update.sh" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Weekly upgrade of Docker CE and GitLab Runner, only while no CI job runs (the distro's automatic updates skip these
# vendor repos). The runner is stopped gracefully (no new job, running jobs finish; KillSignal=SIGQUIT), the job list is
# checked again, the Docker packages are upgraded and Docker restarted, then gitlab-runner is upgraded last and started
# (separate transactions, so no Docker restart happens after the runner is back). Runner and jobs are checked again
# before the Docker restart. If a job is there, the runner is started again and nothing (more) is changed.
# The result is one line in /var/log/runner-engine-update.log, which health reads.
# Env: DRY_RUN=1 shows the plan only; STOP_WAIT seconds to wait for the graceful stop (default 1800).
set -Eeuo pipefail

LOG="${ENGINE_UPDATE_LOG:-/var/log/runner-engine-update.log}"   # overridable for tests
DRY_RUN="${DRY_RUN:-0}"
STOP_WAIT="${STOP_WAIT:-1800}"
DROPIN="${ENGINE_UPDATE_DROPIN:-/etc/systemd/system/gitlab-runner.service.d/graceful-stop.conf}"
PKGS=(docker-ce docker-ce-cli containerd.io docker-buildx-plugin docker-compose-plugin gitlab-runner)

note() { logger -t runner-engine-update -- "$*" 2>/dev/null || true; echo "$(date -u +%FT%TZ) $*" | tee -a "$LOG"; }

if [[ "${1:-}" == "-h" || "${1:-}" == "--help" ]]; then
  echo "Usage: $0 [--dry-run]   upgrades ${PKGS[*]} when no CI job runs"
  exit 0
fi
[[ "${1:-}" == "--dry-run" ]] && DRY_RUN=1
[[ $EUID -eq 0 ]] || { echo "run as root" >&2; exit 1; }
exec 9>/run/runner-engine-update.lock
flock -n 9 || { note "SKIP another update is running"; exit 0; }

# A docker error is not "no jobs": updating then could kill running jobs.
if ! jobs=$(docker ps -q --filter "label=com.gitlab.gitlab-runner.managed=true" 2>/dev/null); then
  note "SKIP docker does not answer, cannot tell whether jobs run"
  exit 0
fi
if [[ -n "$jobs" ]]; then
  note "SKIP $(wc -w <<<"$jobs") CI job(s) running, will retry next week"
  exit 0
fi

installed=()
if command -v dnf >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  for p in "${PKGS[@]}"; do rpm -q "$p" >/dev/null 2>&1 && installed+=("$p"); done
  upgrade() { dnf -y -q upgrade "$@"; }
  pending() { dnf -q check-update "${installed[@]}" >/dev/null 2>&1; [[ $? -eq 100 ]]; }
elif command -v apt-get >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  for p in "${PKGS[@]}"; do dpkg -s "$p" >/dev/null 2>&1 && installed+=("$p"); done
  upgrade() { DEBIAN_FRONTEND=noninteractive apt-get install -y -q --only-upgrade "$@"; }
  pending() { apt-get update -q >/dev/null 2>&1; apt-get -s install --only-upgrade "${installed[@]}" 2>/dev/null | grep -q '^Inst '; }
else
  note "FAIL no supported package manager"
  exit 1
fi
(( ${#installed[@]} )) || { note "SKIP none of the packages is installed"; exit 0; }

if ! pending; then
  note "OK nothing to upgrade (${installed[*]})"
  exit 0
fi
# Two transactions: Docker packages first (they restart Docker), gitlab-runner last (its scriptlet starts the runner).
docker_pkgs=(); runner_pkgs=()
for p in "${installed[@]}"; do if [[ "$p" == gitlab-runner ]]; then runner_pkgs+=("$p"); else docker_pkgs+=("$p"); fi; done
if [[ "$DRY_RUN" == 1 ]]; then
  note "DRY-RUN would upgrade ${installed[*]}"
  exit 0
fi

before=$(for p in "${installed[@]}"; do if command -v rpm >/dev/null 2>&1; then rpm -q --qf '%{NAME}-%{VERSION}-%{RELEASE} ' "$p"; else dpkg-query -W -f='${Package}-${Version} ' "$p"; fi; done)

# From here the runner may be stopped; whatever happens, leave it running again. --no-block: if the runner is still
# finishing a job, systemd runs this start after the stop completes, and this script does not wait for it.
runner_stopped=0
runner_back() { systemctl start --no-block gitlab-runner >/dev/null 2>&1 || true; }
trap '[[ $runner_stopped -eq 1 ]] && runner_back' EXIT

# `systemctl is-active` is false while a unit is deactivating, so the state is read with `show`.
runner_state() { systemctl show -p ActiveState --value gitlab-runner 2>/dev/null || echo unknown; }
# SIGQUIT is the runner's graceful stop: no new job, running jobs finish. Returns 1 when it is still running after STOP_WAIT.
stop_runner_wait() {
  local waited=0 st
  systemctl stop --no-block gitlab-runner
  while :; do
    st=$(runner_state)
    [[ "$st" == inactive || "$st" == failed ]] && return 0
    (( waited >= STOP_WAIT )) && return 1
    sleep 5
    waited=$((waited + 5))
  done
}
no_jobs() { local j; j=$(docker ps -q --filter "label=com.gitlab.gitlab-runner.managed=true" 2>/dev/null) && [[ -z "$j" ]]; }

# The default SIGTERM ends running jobs. TimeoutStopSec=infinity: systemd never SIGKILLs a runner that is still
# waiting for a job; this script gives up waiting on its own (STOP_WAIT) and leaves the stop to finish by itself.
if ! grep -qs '^KillSignal=SIGQUIT' "$DROPIN" 2>/dev/null || ! grep -qs '^TimeoutStopSec=infinity' "$DROPIN" 2>/dev/null; then
  mkdir -p "$(dirname "$DROPIN")"
  printf '[Service]\nKillSignal=SIGQUIT\nTimeoutStopSec=infinity\n' > "$DROPIN"
  systemctl daemon-reload
fi
runner_stopped=1
if ! stop_runner_wait; then
  # Starting a unit that is still stopping queues the start behind the stop: the runner comes back when the job ends.
  note "SKIP gitlab-runner did not stop within ${STOP_WAIT}s (jobs still running); it starts again once it has stopped, will retry next week"
  exit 0
fi
if ! no_jobs; then
  note "SKIP a CI job appeared or docker does not answer after the runner stopped; runner started again, will retry next week"
  exit 0
fi

# Docker packages first, runner stopped: their own Docker restart finds no job.
if (( ${#docker_pkgs[@]} )) && ! upgrade "${docker_pkgs[@]}" >>"$LOG" 2>&1; then
  note "FAIL Docker package upgrade failed, see ${LOG}"
  exit 1
fi
# A package scriptlet may have started the runner (a unit file in /etc cannot be masked from /run), and it may have
# taken a job. Restarting Docker under a running job would kill it, so stop the runner again and look once more.
if [[ "$(runner_state)" != inactive ]]; then
  if ! stop_runner_wait; then
    note "SKIP Docker packages upgraded, but gitlab-runner is busy again; Docker not restarted, gitlab-runner not upgraded (new version active after the next restart or reboot, retry next week)"
    exit 0
  fi
fi
if ! no_jobs; then
  note "SKIP Docker packages upgraded, but a CI job runs or docker does not answer; Docker not restarted, gitlab-runner not upgraded (retry next week)"
  exit 0
fi
bad=""
if (( ${#docker_pkgs[@]} )); then
  systemctl restart docker
  sleep 5
  # The isolation rules live in iptables; reapply them after the Docker restart.
  if systemctl cat ci-job-isolation.service >/dev/null 2>&1; then systemctl restart ci-job-isolation.service || bad+=" ci-job-isolation"; fi
fi
if (( ${#runner_pkgs[@]} )) && ! upgrade "${runner_pkgs[@]}" >>"$LOG" 2>&1; then
  note "FAIL gitlab-runner upgrade failed, see ${LOG}"
  exit 1
fi
systemctl start gitlab-runner || true
runner_stopped=0
sleep 5
for s in docker gitlab-runner; do systemctl is-active --quiet "$s" || bad+=" $s"; done
if [[ -n "$bad" ]]; then
  note "FAIL not active after the upgrade:${bad}; was: ${before}"
  exit 1
fi
note "OK upgraded ${installed[*]}; was: ${before}"
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 755 "$R/runner/scripts/runner-engine-update.sh"

install -d "$R/runner/scripts"
cat > "$R/runner/scripts/ci-job-isolation.sh" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Keep CI job containers away from the cloud metadata service and from services on the host.
# Run at boot and after every Docker start by ci-job-isolation.service; safe to rerun any time.
#
#   DOCKER-USER   drops traffic to 169.254.169.254 (Hetzner metadata: server data, user-data, tokens).
#   INPUT         traffic from docker0 and the br-* bridges to the host itself goes through the chain CI-JOBS-IN:
#                 only replies, ping and the npm cache (docker0 IP, port 4873) are let in, the rest is dropped.
#
# Not affected: internet access of jobs, the Docker Hub cache (Docker pulls through the mirror on 127.0.0.1:5000,
# jobs do not), traffic between containers of one job, and the host-network stacks (monitoring, logging, caches).
# Only IPv4 is handled. Remove the rules: systemctl disable --now ci-job-isolation; then iptables -F CI-JOBS-IN
# and delete the two "ci-job-isolation" rules shown by iptables -S.
set -Eeuo pipefail

META_IP="${META_IP:-169.254.169.254}"
NPM_PORT="${NPM_PORT:-4873}"
TAG=ci-job-isolation

log() { logger -t "$TAG" -- "$*" 2>/dev/null || true; echo "$*"; }

if [[ "${1:-}" == "-h" || "${1:-}" == "--help" ]]; then
  echo "Usage: $0   blocks CI jobs from ${META_IP} and from host services (except the npm cache on the docker0 IP, port ${NPM_PORT})"
  exit 0
fi
[[ $EUID -eq 0 ]] || { echo "run as root" >&2; exit 1; }
command -v iptables >/dev/null 2>&1 || { log "iptables not found, nothing applied"; exit 1; }

# Docker creates DOCKER-USER when it starts; wait for it after a boot.
for _ in $(seq 1 30); do
  if iptables -n -L DOCKER-USER >/dev/null 2>&1; then break; fi
  sleep 1
done
iptables -n -L DOCKER-USER >/dev/null 2>&1 || { log "chain DOCKER-USER does not exist (is Docker running?), nothing applied"; exit 1; }

# Insert a rule at the top of a chain unless it is there already.
ensure() {
  local chain="$1"
  shift
  iptables -C "$chain" "$@" 2>/dev/null || iptables -I "$chain" 1 "$@"
}

ensure DOCKER-USER -d "$META_IP" -m comment --comment "$TAG" -j DROP

BRIDGE_IP="${BRIDGE_IP:-}"
if [[ -z "$BRIDGE_IP" ]] && command -v ip >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  BRIDGE_IP=$(ip -4 -o addr show docker0 2>/dev/null | awk '{ print $4 }' | cut -d/ -f1 | head -1 || true)
fi

iptables -N CI-JOBS-IN 2>/dev/null || true
iptables -F CI-JOBS-IN
iptables -A CI-JOBS-IN -m conntrack --ctstate RELATED,ESTABLISHED -j ACCEPT
iptables -A CI-JOBS-IN -p icmp --icmp-type echo-request -j ACCEPT
if [[ -n "$BRIDGE_IP" ]]; then
  iptables -A CI-JOBS-IN -i docker0 -d "$BRIDGE_IP" -p tcp --dport "$NPM_PORT" -j ACCEPT
fi
iptables -A CI-JOBS-IN -j DROP
ensure INPUT -i docker0 -m comment --comment "$TAG" -j CI-JOBS-IN
ensure INPUT -i 'br-+' -m comment --comment "$TAG" -j CI-JOBS-IN

log "applied: ${META_IP} blocked for containers; host services closed to job networks (npm cache: ${BRIDGE_IP:-none}:${NPM_PORT})"
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 755 "$R/runner/scripts/ci-job-isolation.sh"

install -d "$R/runner/systemd"
cat > "$R/runner/systemd/runner-engine-update.service" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
[Unit]
Description=Weekly upgrade of Docker CE and GitLab Runner when no CI job runs
After=docker.service network-online.target

[Service]
Type=oneshot
ExecStart=/usr/local/bin/runner-engine-update.sh
Nice=10
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 644 "$R/runner/systemd/runner-engine-update.service"

install -d "$R/runner/systemd"
cat > "$R/runner/systemd/runner-engine-update.timer" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
[Unit]
Description=Upgrade Docker CE and GitLab Runner every Sunday

[Timer]
OnCalendar=Sun *-*-* 04:30:00
Persistent=true
RandomizedDelaySec=30m

[Install]
WantedBy=timers.target
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 644 "$R/runner/systemd/runner-engine-update.timer"

install -d "$R/runner/systemd"
cat > "$R/runner/systemd/ci-job-isolation.service" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
[Unit]
Description=Block CI jobs from the cloud metadata service and host services
After=docker.service network-online.target
Requires=docker.service
PartOf=docker.service

[Service]
Type=oneshot
RemainAfterExit=yes
ExecStart=/usr/local/bin/ci-job-isolation.sh
ExecReload=/usr/local/bin/ci-job-isolation.sh

[Install]
WantedBy=multi-user.target
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 644 "$R/runner/systemd/ci-job-isolation.service"

install -d "$R/common"
cat > "$R/common/sysctl-hardening.conf" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
# Kernel hardening for runner hosts. A leading "-" ignores a key the running kernel does not have.
-kernel.kptr_restrict = 2
-kernel.dmesg_restrict = 1
-kernel.yama.ptrace_scope = 1
-fs.protected_fifos = 2
-fs.protected_regular = 2
net.ipv4.conf.all.log_martians = 1
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 644 "$R/common/sysctl-hardening.conf"

echo "files written to $R: 15"
bash "$R/runner/install/host-tools.sh" </dev/null
INSTALL
````

<a id="firewall"></a>
## Host firewall and fail2ban

firewalld on Rocky, ufw on Debian, with SSH (22 and the port sshd listens on) allowed and everything else closed, plus the fail2ban sshd jail (5 tries in 10 minutes, 1 hour ban). The firewall starts only after SSH is in the saved rules and stops again if SSH is not allowed. Open the same ports in the Hetzner Cloud Firewall. If Docker was already running on Rocky, docker0 is moved into the firewalld docker zone, so Docker needs no restart.

````bash
sudo bash <<'INSTALL'
set -Eeuo pipefail
R=/root/server-init-config

install -d "$R/common"
cat > "$R/common/lib.sh" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Shared helpers for the installers (runner/install, server and common). Sourced, not executed.
# shellcheck shell=bash

REPO_ROOT="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")/.." && pwd)"
export REPO_ROOT

log() { echo "[$(date +%H:%M:%S)] $*"; }
die() { echo "ERROR: $*" >&2; exit 1; }

require_root() { [[ $EUID -eq 0 ]] || die "run as root"; }

selinux_on() { command -v selinuxenabled >/dev/null 2>&1 && selinuxenabled; }

pkg_install() {
  if command -v dnf >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    dnf -y -q install "$@"
  elif command -v apt-get >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    apt-get update -q && apt-get install -y -q "$@"
  else
    die "no supported package manager"
  fi
}

# Label a directory so containers may read files in it (no-op without SELinux).
selinux_label_dir() {
  local dir="$1"
  install -d -m 0755 "$dir"
  selinux_on || return 0
  command -v semanage >/dev/null 2>&1 || pkg_install policycoreutils-python-utils
  # Read the whole list first: grep -q exits at the first match and would SIGPIPE the producer under pipefail.
  if ! grep -q "^${dir}(/\.\*)?" <<<"$(semanage fcontext -l)"; then
    semanage fcontext -a -t container_file_t "${dir}(/.*)?"
  fi
  restorecon -R "$dir"
}

restorecon_if() { if command -v restorecon >/dev/null 2>&1; then restorecon "$@" || true; fi; }

# RHEL-family sudo resets PATH to secure_path, which can leave out /usr/local/bin (so `sudo health` fails).
# Adds a validated drop-in unless some sudoers file already sets a secure_path that includes it.
ensure_secure_path() {
  local f=/etc/sudoers.d/50-secure-path tmp
  command -v visudo >/dev/null 2>&1 || return 0
  if grep -rhsE '^[[:space:]]*Defaults[[:space:]]+secure_path[[:space:]]*=.*/usr/local/bin' /etc/sudoers /etc/sudoers.d 2>/dev/null | grep -q .; then
    return 0
  fi
  if ! grep -rhsE '^[[:space:]]*Defaults[[:space:]]+secure_path' /etc/sudoers /etc/sudoers.d 2>/dev/null | grep -q .; then
    return 0   # no secure_path at all (Debian family keeps it in sudoers; others use the caller PATH)
  fi
  tmp=$(mktemp)
  printf 'Defaults secure_path="/usr/local/sbin:/usr/local/bin:/usr/sbin:/usr/bin:/sbin:/bin"\n' > "$tmp"
  if visudo -cqf "$tmp"; then
    install -d -m 0750 /etc/sudoers.d
    install -m 0440 -o root -g root "$tmp" "$f"
    log "sudo secure_path now includes /usr/local/bin (${f})"
  else
    echo "WARN: generated sudoers drop-in is invalid, secure_path left as it was" >&2
  fi
  rm -f "$tmp"
}

ci_jobs_running() { docker ps -q --filter "label=com.gitlab.gitlab-runner.managed=true" 2>/dev/null | wc -l; }

require_runner_idle() {
  local n
  n=$(ci_jobs_running)
  (( n == 0 )) || die "${n} CI job container(s) running, retry when the runner is idle"
}

# Timestamped copy before a change. Skipped when an identical backup already exists, so reruns of an idempotent
# installer do not pile up copies of an unchanged file.
backup() {
  local f=$1 b
  for b in "${f}".bak.*; do
    [[ -f "$b" ]] && cmp -s "$f" "$b" && return 0
  done
  cp -a "$f" "${f}.bak.$(date +%Y%m%d-%H%M%S)"
}

# Prometheus scrape jobs for the logging stack, shared by runner/install/logging.sh and runner/install/update.sh.
prom_jobs_logging() {
  cat <<'YAML'
  - job_name: loki
    static_configs:
      - targets: ["127.0.0.1:3100"]
  - job_name: alloy
    static_configs:
      - targets: ["127.0.0.1:12345"]
YAML
}

# Merge KEY=VALUE entries into the single [[runners]] environment line of config.toml.
runner_env_merge() {
  local cfg=/etc/gitlab-runner/config.toml existing list key e line
  [[ $(grep -c '^\[\[runners\]\]' "$cfg") -eq 1 ]] || die "expected exactly one [[runners]] block in $cfg"
  existing=$(grep -E '^  environment = \[' "$cfg" | sed -E 's/^  environment = \[(.*)\]$/\1/' || true)
  list="$existing"
  for e in "$@"; do
    key="${e%%=*}"
    if grep -q "\"${key}=" <<<"$list"; then
      list=$(sed -E "s|\"${key}=[^\"]*\"|\"${e}\"|" <<<"$list")
    else
      list="${list:+${list}, }\"${e}\""
    fi
  done
  line="  environment = [${list}]"
  if grep -qE '^  environment = \[' "$cfg"; then
    sed -i -E "s|^  environment = \[.*\]$|${line}|" "$cfg"
  else
    sed -i -E "0,/^  executor = .*/s||${line}\n&|" "$cfg"
  fi
  chmod 600 "$cfg"
  runner_cfg_owner
}

# When the runner service runs as gitlab-runner (runner/install/users.sh), it must own its config after root edits it.
runner_cfg_owner() {
  local cfg=/etc/gitlab-runner/config.toml
  if [[ -f /etc/systemd/system/gitlab-runner.service.d/10-service-user.conf ]] && id gitlab-runner >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    chown -R gitlab-runner:gitlab-runner /etc/gitlab-runner
    chmod 600 "$cfg"
  fi
}

# --- service user for the monitoring and logging stacks (created by runner/install/users.sh) ---------------
MON_USER="${MON_USER:-monitoring}"

# Set MON_UID and MON_GID, or stop if the service user is missing.
mon_ids() {
  id "$MON_USER" >/dev/null 2>&1 || die "service user ${MON_USER} missing, run runner/install/users.sh first"
  MON_UID=$(id -u "$MON_USER")
  MON_GID=$(id -g "$MON_USER")
}

# env_set FILE KEY VALUE: set KEY=VALUE in a root-only env file.
env_set() {
  local f="$1" k="$2" v="$3"
  touch "$f"
  chmod 600 "$f"
  if grep -q "^${k}=" "$f"; then
    sed -i "s|^${k}=.*|${k}=${v}|" "$f"
  else
    printf '%s=%s\n' "$k" "$v" >> "$f"
  fi
}

# stack_ids DIR [logging]: write the ids the compose file needs into DIR/.env.
stack_ids() {
  local dir="$1" docker_gid journal_gid
  mon_ids
  env_set "${dir}/.env" MON_UID "$MON_UID"
  env_set "${dir}/.env" MON_GID "$MON_GID"
  if [[ "${2:-}" == logging ]]; then
    docker_gid=$(getent group docker | cut -d: -f3)
    [[ -n "$docker_gid" ]] || die "docker group missing"
    env_set "${dir}/.env" DOCKER_GID "$docker_gid"
    journal_gid=$(getent group systemd-journal | cut -d: -f3)
    [[ -n "$journal_gid" ]] || die "systemd-journal group missing"
    # compose rejects equal group_add items, so the journal group is only added when it differs from docker's.
    if [[ "$journal_gid" != "$docker_gid" ]]; then
      printf 'services:\n  alloy:\n    group_add:\n      - "%s"\n' "$journal_gid" > "${dir}/docker-compose.override.yml"
    else
      rm -f "${dir}/docker-compose.override.yml"
    fi
  fi
}

# Config readable by the service user, writable by root only; .env (secrets) stays root-only.
own_stack_dir() {
  local dir="$1"
  chown -R "root:${MON_USER}" "$dir"
  chmod -R u=rwX,g=rX,o= "$dir"
  if [[ -f "${dir}/.env" ]]; then
    chown root:root "${dir}/.env"
    chmod 600 "${dir}/.env"
  fi
}

# Create the stack's containers and volumes without starting them, then hand the volumes to the service user.
chown_volumes() {
  local dir="$1" project v
  mon_ids
  project=$(cd "$dir" && docker compose config --format json | jq -r .name)
  (cd "$dir" && docker compose up --no-start --quiet-pull >/dev/null 2>&1) || (cd "$dir" && docker compose up --no-start >/dev/null)
  for v in $(docker volume ls -q --filter "label=com.docker.compose.project=${project}"); do
    docker run --rm --network none -v "${v}:/v" alpine:3.20 chown -R "${MON_UID}:${MON_GID}" /v
  done
}
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 755 "$R/common/lib.sh"

install -d "$R/runner/install"
cat > "$R/runner/install/host-firewall.sh" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Host firewall and fail2ban for the runner host: firewalld on Rocky/Alma/CentOS, ufw on Debian/Ubuntu.
# Self-contained (no lib.sh) so cloud-init can embed it. Safe to rerun.
#
# Nothing is opened except SSH (22 and every port sshd listens on) and, when Grafana is published on a domain
# (override file exists), 80/443. Services of the host
# (monitoring, logging, caches) listen on 127.0.0.1 or the docker0 IP and need no rule. Lockout guard, as in
# server/server-init-*.sh: the firewall is only started or enabled when SSH is in the saved rules, and it is
# stopped again when SSH is not allowed in the running rules. Also open the same ports in the Hetzner Cloud Firewall.
#
# Docker keeps working: on Rocky firewalld puts docker0 into its own docker zone when Docker starts after firewalld;
# if Docker was already running, this script moves docker0 into that zone (no Docker restart). On Debian
# ufw does not touch the chains Docker manages. Jobs are kept away from the host by ci-job-isolation.sh.
# fail2ban: sshd jail on the journal, 5 tries in 10 minutes, 1 hour ban.
set -Eeuo pipefail

JAIL_FILE="${JAIL_FILE:-/etc/fail2ban/jail.d/50-sshd.local}"   # overridable for tests
GRAFANA_OVERRIDE="${GRAFANA_OVERRIDE:-/opt/monitoring/docker-compose.override.yml}"   # exists once grafana-domain.sh ran

log() { echo "[$(date +%H:%M:%S)] $*"; }
die() { echo "ERROR: $*" >&2; exit 1; }

if [[ "${1:-}" == "-h" || "${1:-}" == "--help" ]]; then
  echo "Usage: $0   enables firewalld (Rocky) or ufw (Debian) with SSH allowed, plus the fail2ban sshd jail"
  exit 0
fi
[[ $EUID -eq 0 ]] || die "run as root"

web_published() { [[ -f "$GRAFANA_OVERRIDE" ]]; }

ssh_ports() {
  { echo 22; sshd -T 2>/dev/null | awk '$1 == "port" { print $2 }'; } | sort -un
}

put_jail() {
  local banaction="$1" tmp ports
  ports=$(ssh_ports | paste -sd, -)   # the jail must watch every port sshd listens on, not only 22
  tmp=$(mktemp)
  {
    if [[ -n "$banaction" ]]; then
      printf '[DEFAULT]\nbanaction = %s\nbanaction_allports = %s[type=allports]\n\n' "$banaction" "$banaction"
    fi
    printf '[sshd]\nenabled  = true\nport     = %s\nbackend  = systemd\nmaxretry = 5\nfindtime = 10m\nbantime  = 1h\n' "$ports"
  } > "$tmp"
  install -D -m 0644 "$tmp" "$JAIL_FILE"
  rm -f "$tmp"
  if command -v restorecon >/dev/null 2>&1; then restorecon "$JAIL_FILE" || true; fi
}

firewalld_setup() {
  local running=0 p zone set_zone
  command -v firewall-cmd >/dev/null 2>&1 || dnf -y -q install firewalld || die "cannot install firewalld"
  systemctl is-active --quiet firewalld && running=1
  # SSH is allowed in the public zone, so the uplink must be in it: make it the default zone (as server-init does).
  if (( running )); then zone=$(firewall-cmd --get-default-zone) || die "cannot read the default zone"; else zone=$(firewall-offline-cmd --get-default-zone) || die "cannot read the default zone"; fi
  # Running: the default zone is switched after the reload, so SSH is never refused in between.
  set_zone=0
  if [[ "$zone" != public ]]; then
    log "firewalld: default zone was ${zone}, set to public"
    if (( running )); then set_zone=1; else firewall-offline-cmd --set-default-zone=public >/dev/null || die "cannot set the default zone"; fi
  fi
  # Permanent rules are written first (offline when firewalld is stopped), then the daemon is started or reloaded.
  fw() { if systemctl is-active --quiet firewalld; then firewall-cmd --permanent "$@"; else firewall-offline-cmd "$@"; fi; }
  fw --zone=public --query-service=ssh >/dev/null 2>&1 || fw --zone=public --add-service=ssh >/dev/null || die "cannot add ssh to the public zone"
  for p in $(ssh_ports); do
    [[ "$p" == 22 ]] && continue
    fw --zone=public --query-port="${p}/tcp" >/dev/null 2>&1 || fw --zone=public --add-port="${p}/tcp" >/dev/null || die "cannot add port ${p}"
  done
  if web_published; then
    for p in http https; do
      fw --zone=public --query-service="$p" >/dev/null 2>&1 || fw --zone=public --add-service="$p" >/dev/null || die "cannot add ${p} to the public zone"
    done
    log "firewalld: http and https kept open (Grafana is published on a domain)"
  fi
  # Lockout guard: the saved rules must allow SSH before the firewall runs.
  fw --zone=public --query-service=ssh >/dev/null 2>&1 || die "ssh missing from the saved public zone; firewall left as it was"
  for p in $(ssh_ports); do
    [[ "$p" == 22 ]] && continue
    fw --zone=public --query-port="${p}/tcp" >/dev/null 2>&1 || die "port ${p} missing from the saved public zone; firewall left as it was"
  done
  if (( running )); then firewall-cmd --reload >/dev/null || die "firewalld reload failed"; else systemctl enable --now firewalld || die "firewalld did not start"; fi
  systemctl enable firewalld >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
  if (( set_zone )); then firewall-cmd --set-default-zone=public >/dev/null || die "cannot set the default zone"; fi
  if ! firewall-cmd --zone=public --query-service=ssh >/dev/null; then
    systemctl stop firewalld
    die "ssh not allowed after start; firewalld stopped"
  fi
  for p in $(ssh_ports); do
    [[ "$p" == 22 ]] && continue
    if ! firewall-cmd --zone=public --query-port="${p}/tcp" >/dev/null; then
      systemctl stop firewalld
      die "ssh port ${p} not allowed after start; firewalld stopped"
    fi
  done
  log "firewalld: active, public zone: $(firewall-cmd --zone=public --list-services) $(firewall-cmd --zone=public --list-ports)"
  docker_zone
}

# Docker that was running before firewalld left docker0 in the default zone, where firewalld rejects job traffic to
# the host (the npm cache on the docker0 address). Put docker0 into the docker zone (target ACCEPT, as Docker makes it),
# permanently and now, without restarting Docker. iptables rules (ci-job-isolation.sh) still apply on top.
docker_zone() {
  command -v docker >/dev/null 2>&1 && systemctl is-active --quiet docker || return 0
  ip link show docker0 >/dev/null 2>&1 || return 0
  [[ "$(firewall-cmd --get-zone-of-interface=docker0 2>/dev/null)" == docker ]] && return 0
  if ! firewall-cmd --permanent --get-zones | tr ' ' '\n' | grep -qx docker; then
    firewall-cmd --permanent --new-zone=docker >/dev/null && firewall-cmd --permanent --zone=docker --set-target=ACCEPT >/dev/null \
      && firewall-cmd --reload >/dev/null || { log "WARN: cannot create the firewalld docker zone; restart Docker while no CI job runs: systemctl restart docker"; return 0; }
  fi
  if firewall-cmd --permanent --zone=docker --add-interface=docker0 >/dev/null && firewall-cmd --zone=docker --change-interface=docker0 >/dev/null; then
    log "firewalld: docker0 put into the docker zone"
  else
    log "WARN: docker0 is not in the docker zone; restart Docker while no CI job runs: systemctl restart docker"
  fi
}

ufw_setup() {
  local p o was_active=0
  command -v ufw >/dev/null 2>&1 || { DEBIAN_FRONTEND=noninteractive apt-get install -y -q ufw >/dev/null || die "cannot install ufw"; }
  o=$(ufw status 2>/dev/null) || true
  if grep -q '^Status: active' <<<"$o"; then was_active=1; fi
  for p in $(ssh_ports); do ufw allow "${p}/tcp" >/dev/null || die "cannot allow ${p}/tcp"; done
  if web_published; then
    ufw allow 80/tcp >/dev/null && ufw allow 443/tcp >/dev/null || die "cannot allow 80/443 in ufw"
    log "ufw: 80/tcp and 443/tcp kept open (Grafana is published on a domain)"
  fi
  # Lockout guard: every SSH port must be in the saved rules before ufw is enabled.
  o=$(ufw show added 2>/dev/null) || true
  for p in $(ssh_ports); do
    grep -qE "^ufw (allow|limit) (in )?${p}/tcp([[:space:]]|\$)" <<<"$o" || die "${p}/tcp missing from the saved ufw rules; ufw left as it was"
  done
  if (( ! was_active )); then
    ufw default deny incoming >/dev/null
    ufw default allow outgoing >/dev/null
    ufw --force enable >/dev/null
  fi
  systemctl enable ufw >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
  o=$(ufw status 2>/dev/null) || true
  for p in $(ssh_ports); do
    if ! grep -qE "^${p}/tcp([[:space:]]+\\(v6\\))?[[:space:]]+(ALLOW|LIMIT)" <<<"$o"; then
      if (( ! was_active )); then ufw disable >/dev/null 2>&1 || true; fi
      die "ssh port ${p} not allowed in the running ufw; ufw disabled again"
    fi
  done
  log "ufw: $(ufw status | head -1); allowed: $(ssh_ports | tr '\n' ' ')"
}

# FIREWALL_BACKEND=firewalld|ufw overrides the choice by package manager (used by the tests).
backend="${FIREWALL_BACKEND:-}"
if [[ -z "$backend" ]]; then
  if command -v dnf >/dev/null 2>&1; then backend=firewalld; elif command -v apt-get >/dev/null 2>&1; then backend=ufw; fi
fi

if [[ "$backend" == firewalld ]]; then
  dnf -y -q install epel-release >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
  dnf -y -q install fail2ban-server fail2ban-firewalld >/dev/null 2>&1 || log "WARN: fail2ban not available (EPEL?), sshd jail skipped"
  firewalld_setup
  if command -v fail2ban-client >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    put_jail ""
    systemctl enable --now fail2ban >/dev/null && systemctl restart fail2ban
  fi
elif [[ "$backend" == ufw ]]; then
  DEBIAN_FRONTEND=noninteractive apt-get install -y -q fail2ban >/dev/null 2>&1 || log "WARN: fail2ban not installed, sshd jail skipped"
  ufw_setup
  if command -v fail2ban-client >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    put_jail nftables
    systemctl enable --now fail2ban >/dev/null && systemctl restart fail2ban
  fi
else
  die "no supported package manager"
fi
if command -v fail2ban-client >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  log "fail2ban: $(fail2ban-client status 2>/dev/null | tr '\n' ' ' || true)"
fi
log "OK: host firewall on. If you lock yourself out, use the Hetzner web console: systemctl stop firewalld (or: ufw disable)"
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 755 "$R/runner/install/host-firewall.sh"

echo "files written to $R: 2"
bash "$R/runner/install/host-firewall.sh" </dev/null
INSTALL
````

<a id="security"></a>
## Security tools: audit, network, scan

Optional. Tools to test and audit your own server; use them only on systems you own or are authorized to test. Default: lynis, OpenSCAP, auditd (small ruleset), ssh-audit, testssl.sh, nmap and other network tools, and trivy, gitleaks, hadolint, cosign, osv-scanner, trufflehog, dockle as release binaries at pinned versions, checked against the checksums file of the same release. Options for the installer (`sudo bash /root/server-init-config/common/security-tools.sh --help`; `/root` is not readable by `ops`): `--aide`, `--clamav`, `--rkhunter`, `--chkrootkit`, `--grype`, `--syft`, `--k8s`, `--kali`, `--audit-timer`.

````bash
sudo bash <<'INSTALL'
set -Eeuo pipefail
R=/root/server-init-config

install -d "$R/common"
cat > "$R/common/lib.sh" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Shared helpers for the installers (runner/install, server and common). Sourced, not executed.
# shellcheck shell=bash

REPO_ROOT="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")/.." && pwd)"
export REPO_ROOT

log() { echo "[$(date +%H:%M:%S)] $*"; }
die() { echo "ERROR: $*" >&2; exit 1; }

require_root() { [[ $EUID -eq 0 ]] || die "run as root"; }

selinux_on() { command -v selinuxenabled >/dev/null 2>&1 && selinuxenabled; }

pkg_install() {
  if command -v dnf >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    dnf -y -q install "$@"
  elif command -v apt-get >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    apt-get update -q && apt-get install -y -q "$@"
  else
    die "no supported package manager"
  fi
}

# Label a directory so containers may read files in it (no-op without SELinux).
selinux_label_dir() {
  local dir="$1"
  install -d -m 0755 "$dir"
  selinux_on || return 0
  command -v semanage >/dev/null 2>&1 || pkg_install policycoreutils-python-utils
  # Read the whole list first: grep -q exits at the first match and would SIGPIPE the producer under pipefail.
  if ! grep -q "^${dir}(/\.\*)?" <<<"$(semanage fcontext -l)"; then
    semanage fcontext -a -t container_file_t "${dir}(/.*)?"
  fi
  restorecon -R "$dir"
}

restorecon_if() { if command -v restorecon >/dev/null 2>&1; then restorecon "$@" || true; fi; }

# RHEL-family sudo resets PATH to secure_path, which can leave out /usr/local/bin (so `sudo health` fails).
# Adds a validated drop-in unless some sudoers file already sets a secure_path that includes it.
ensure_secure_path() {
  local f=/etc/sudoers.d/50-secure-path tmp
  command -v visudo >/dev/null 2>&1 || return 0
  if grep -rhsE '^[[:space:]]*Defaults[[:space:]]+secure_path[[:space:]]*=.*/usr/local/bin' /etc/sudoers /etc/sudoers.d 2>/dev/null | grep -q .; then
    return 0
  fi
  if ! grep -rhsE '^[[:space:]]*Defaults[[:space:]]+secure_path' /etc/sudoers /etc/sudoers.d 2>/dev/null | grep -q .; then
    return 0   # no secure_path at all (Debian family keeps it in sudoers; others use the caller PATH)
  fi
  tmp=$(mktemp)
  printf 'Defaults secure_path="/usr/local/sbin:/usr/local/bin:/usr/sbin:/usr/bin:/sbin:/bin"\n' > "$tmp"
  if visudo -cqf "$tmp"; then
    install -d -m 0750 /etc/sudoers.d
    install -m 0440 -o root -g root "$tmp" "$f"
    log "sudo secure_path now includes /usr/local/bin (${f})"
  else
    echo "WARN: generated sudoers drop-in is invalid, secure_path left as it was" >&2
  fi
  rm -f "$tmp"
}

ci_jobs_running() { docker ps -q --filter "label=com.gitlab.gitlab-runner.managed=true" 2>/dev/null | wc -l; }

require_runner_idle() {
  local n
  n=$(ci_jobs_running)
  (( n == 0 )) || die "${n} CI job container(s) running, retry when the runner is idle"
}

# Timestamped copy before a change. Skipped when an identical backup already exists, so reruns of an idempotent
# installer do not pile up copies of an unchanged file.
backup() {
  local f=$1 b
  for b in "${f}".bak.*; do
    [[ -f "$b" ]] && cmp -s "$f" "$b" && return 0
  done
  cp -a "$f" "${f}.bak.$(date +%Y%m%d-%H%M%S)"
}

# Prometheus scrape jobs for the logging stack, shared by runner/install/logging.sh and runner/install/update.sh.
prom_jobs_logging() {
  cat <<'YAML'
  - job_name: loki
    static_configs:
      - targets: ["127.0.0.1:3100"]
  - job_name: alloy
    static_configs:
      - targets: ["127.0.0.1:12345"]
YAML
}

# Merge KEY=VALUE entries into the single [[runners]] environment line of config.toml.
runner_env_merge() {
  local cfg=/etc/gitlab-runner/config.toml existing list key e line
  [[ $(grep -c '^\[\[runners\]\]' "$cfg") -eq 1 ]] || die "expected exactly one [[runners]] block in $cfg"
  existing=$(grep -E '^  environment = \[' "$cfg" | sed -E 's/^  environment = \[(.*)\]$/\1/' || true)
  list="$existing"
  for e in "$@"; do
    key="${e%%=*}"
    if grep -q "\"${key}=" <<<"$list"; then
      list=$(sed -E "s|\"${key}=[^\"]*\"|\"${e}\"|" <<<"$list")
    else
      list="${list:+${list}, }\"${e}\""
    fi
  done
  line="  environment = [${list}]"
  if grep -qE '^  environment = \[' "$cfg"; then
    sed -i -E "s|^  environment = \[.*\]$|${line}|" "$cfg"
  else
    sed -i -E "0,/^  executor = .*/s||${line}\n&|" "$cfg"
  fi
  chmod 600 "$cfg"
  runner_cfg_owner
}

# When the runner service runs as gitlab-runner (runner/install/users.sh), it must own its config after root edits it.
runner_cfg_owner() {
  local cfg=/etc/gitlab-runner/config.toml
  if [[ -f /etc/systemd/system/gitlab-runner.service.d/10-service-user.conf ]] && id gitlab-runner >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    chown -R gitlab-runner:gitlab-runner /etc/gitlab-runner
    chmod 600 "$cfg"
  fi
}

# --- service user for the monitoring and logging stacks (created by runner/install/users.sh) ---------------
MON_USER="${MON_USER:-monitoring}"

# Set MON_UID and MON_GID, or stop if the service user is missing.
mon_ids() {
  id "$MON_USER" >/dev/null 2>&1 || die "service user ${MON_USER} missing, run runner/install/users.sh first"
  MON_UID=$(id -u "$MON_USER")
  MON_GID=$(id -g "$MON_USER")
}

# env_set FILE KEY VALUE: set KEY=VALUE in a root-only env file.
env_set() {
  local f="$1" k="$2" v="$3"
  touch "$f"
  chmod 600 "$f"
  if grep -q "^${k}=" "$f"; then
    sed -i "s|^${k}=.*|${k}=${v}|" "$f"
  else
    printf '%s=%s\n' "$k" "$v" >> "$f"
  fi
}

# stack_ids DIR [logging]: write the ids the compose file needs into DIR/.env.
stack_ids() {
  local dir="$1" docker_gid journal_gid
  mon_ids
  env_set "${dir}/.env" MON_UID "$MON_UID"
  env_set "${dir}/.env" MON_GID "$MON_GID"
  if [[ "${2:-}" == logging ]]; then
    docker_gid=$(getent group docker | cut -d: -f3)
    [[ -n "$docker_gid" ]] || die "docker group missing"
    env_set "${dir}/.env" DOCKER_GID "$docker_gid"
    journal_gid=$(getent group systemd-journal | cut -d: -f3)
    [[ -n "$journal_gid" ]] || die "systemd-journal group missing"
    # compose rejects equal group_add items, so the journal group is only added when it differs from docker's.
    if [[ "$journal_gid" != "$docker_gid" ]]; then
      printf 'services:\n  alloy:\n    group_add:\n      - "%s"\n' "$journal_gid" > "${dir}/docker-compose.override.yml"
    else
      rm -f "${dir}/docker-compose.override.yml"
    fi
  fi
}

# Config readable by the service user, writable by root only; .env (secrets) stays root-only.
own_stack_dir() {
  local dir="$1"
  chown -R "root:${MON_USER}" "$dir"
  chmod -R u=rwX,g=rX,o= "$dir"
  if [[ -f "${dir}/.env" ]]; then
    chown root:root "${dir}/.env"
    chmod 600 "${dir}/.env"
  fi
}
````
