  sed -i -E "0,/^  name = .*/s//&\n  request_concurrency = ${REQUEST_CONCURRENCY}/" "$CFG"
fi
chmod 600 "$CFG"
# Per-job CPU, memory and process limits (runner/install/job-limits.sh); the runner reloads config.toml by itself.
bash "$(dirname "$0")/job-limits.sh" >/dev/null || log "WARN: job limits not set; run runner/install/job-limits.sh"
runner_cfg_owner
systemctl restart gitlab-runner
sleep 3

gitlab-runner verify 2>&1 | grep -E 'is valid|is not valid' || true
grep -nE '^concurrent|^listen_address|request_concurrency|executor' "$CFG"
if grep -qi bottleneck <<<"$(journalctl -u gitlab-runner --since "-1min" --no-pager)"; then
  log "WARN: runner still reports a request bottleneck"
else
  log "OK: runner registered"
fi
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 755 "$R/runner/install/register-runner.sh"

install -d "$R/runner/install"
cat > "$R/runner/install/registry-cache.sh" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Docker Hub pull-through cache on 127.0.0.1:5000, wired into the Docker daemon as a mirror.
# Restarts Docker: refuses to run while CI jobs are running.
set -Eeuo pipefail
# shellcheck source=../../common/lib.sh
source "$(dirname "$0")/../../common/lib.sh"

DIR=/opt/registry-cache
MIRROR="http://127.0.0.1:5000"
DAEMON=/etc/docker/daemon.json
PROM_CFG=/opt/monitoring/prometheus/prometheus.yml

if [[ "${1:-}" == "-h" || "${1:-}" == "--help" ]]; then
  echo "Usage: $0  installs the Docker Hub pull-through cache into $DIR"
  exit 0
fi
require_root
command -v jq >/dev/null || pkg_install jq
require_runner_idle
if [[ -f "$DIR/docker-compose.yml" ]]; then
  log "${DIR} exists: re-applying the repo files and finishing the setup (safe to rerun)"
elif ss -ltn | awk '$4 ~ /:(5000|5001)$/' | grep -q .; then
  die "port 5000 or 5001 already in use"
fi

selinux_label_dir "$DIR"
cp -a "$REPO_ROOT/runner/stacks/registry-cache/config.yml" "$REPO_ROOT/runner/stacks/registry-cache/docker-compose.yml" "$DIR/"
restorecon_if -R "$DIR"

cd "$DIR"
docker compose config -q
docker compose pull -q
docker compose up -d
for _ in $(seq 1 15); do curl -fsS "$MIRROR/v2/" >/dev/null 2>&1 && break; sleep 2; done
curl -fsS "$MIRROR/v2/" >/dev/null || { docker compose logs --tail 30; die "registry cache did not start"; }

if ! jq -e --arg m "$MIRROR" '(.["registry-mirrors"] // []) | index($m)' "$DAEMON" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  backup "$DAEMON"
  tmp=$(mktemp)
  jq --arg m "$MIRROR" '.["registry-mirrors"] = [$m]' "$DAEMON" > "$tmp"
  dockerd --validate --config-file "$tmp" >/dev/null || { rm -f "$tmp"; die "new daemon.json is invalid"; }
  install -m 0644 "$tmp" "$DAEMON"
  rm -f "$tmp"
  restorecon_if "$DAEMON"
  log "restarting docker"
  systemctl restart docker
  sleep 5
  if systemctl cat gitlab-runner >/dev/null 2>&1; then systemctl restart gitlab-runner || true; fi
  for _ in $(seq 1 20); do curl -fsS "$MIRROR/v2/" >/dev/null 2>&1 && break; sleep 2; done
fi

if [[ -f "$PROM_CFG" ]] && ! grep -q 'job_name: registry-cache' "$PROM_CFG"; then
  backup "$PROM_CFG"
  cat >> "$PROM_CFG" <<'YAML'
  - job_name: registry-cache
    static_configs:
      - targets: ["127.0.0.1:5001"]
YAML
  curl -fsS -X POST http://127.0.0.1:9090/-/reload || log "WARN: prometheus reload failed"
fi

docker rmi -f busybox:1.36 >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
docker pull -q busybox:1.36 >/dev/null
docker info 2>/dev/null | grep -A1 'Registry Mirrors'
curl -fsS "$MIRROR/v2/_catalog" | jq -c .
ss -ltn | awk 'NR==1 || /:(5000|5001) /'
log "OK: Docker Hub pull-through cache at ${MIRROR}"
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 755 "$R/runner/install/registry-cache.sh"

install -d "$R/runner/install"
cat > "$R/runner/install/selinux-enforcing.sh" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Switch SELinux to enforcing now and after reboot (Rocky/RHEL). Rollback: setenforce 0.
# Restarts Docker and the runner: refuses to run while CI jobs are running.
set -Eeuo pipefail
# shellcheck source=../../common/lib.sh
source "$(dirname "$0")/../../common/lib.sh"

if [[ "${1:-}" == "-h" || "${1:-}" == "--help" ]]; then
  echo "Usage: $0  enables SELinux enforcing mode and verifies Docker and the runner still work"
  exit 0
fi
require_root
command -v getenforce >/dev/null || die "SELinux tools not present on this system"
require_runner_idle
systemctl is-active --quiet auditd || log "WARN: auditd not active, denials will not be logged"

setenforce 1
sed -i -E 's/^SELINUX=.*/SELINUX=enforcing/' /etc/selinux/config

getenforce
grep -E '^SELINUX=' /etc/selinux/config
hello=$(docker run --rm hello-world)
grep -m1 Hello <<<"$hello"
systemctl restart docker
systemctl restart gitlab-runner || true
systemctl is-active docker gitlab-runner || true
denials=$(ausearch --input-logs -m avc -ts recent </dev/null 2>/dev/null | grep -c 'avc:' || true)
log "recent SELinux denials: ${denials:-0}"
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 755 "$R/runner/install/selinux-enforcing.sh"

install -d "$R/runner/install"
cat > "$R/runner/install/shared-cache.sh" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Shared host cache /srv/ci-cache mounted into every job as /ci-cache (npm tarballs, Sonar plugins).
# The cache is shared across all projects on this runner: only use it for trusted projects.
set -Eeuo pipefail
# shellcheck source=../../common/lib.sh
source "$(dirname "$0")/../../common/lib.sh"

HOST_DIR=/srv/ci-cache
MOUNT=/ci-cache
CFG=/etc/gitlab-runner/config.toml

if [[ "${1:-}" == "-h" || "${1:-}" == "--help" ]]; then
  echo "Usage: $0  mounts $HOST_DIR into all jobs and sets npm_config_cache and SONAR_USER_HOME"
  exit 0
fi
require_root
if ! { [[ -f "$CFG" ]] && grep -q '^\[\[runners\]\]' "$CFG"; }; then die "runner not registered yet"; fi
require_runner_idle

install -d -m 1777 "$HOST_DIR" "$HOST_DIR/npm" "$HOST_DIR/sonar"
if selinux_on; then
  command -v semanage >/dev/null 2>&1 || pkg_install policycoreutils-python-utils
  grep -q "^${HOST_DIR}(/\.\*)?" <<<"$(semanage fcontext -l)" || semanage fcontext -a -t container_file_t "${HOST_DIR}(/.*)?"
  restorecon -R "$HOST_DIR"
fi

backup "$CFG"
if ! grep -q "${HOST_DIR}:${MOUNT}" "$CFG"; then
  grep -qE '^    volumes = \["/cache"\]' "$CFG" || die "unexpected volumes line in [runners.docker], edit manually"
  sed -i -E "s|^    volumes = \[\"/cache\"\]|    volumes = [\"/cache\", \"${HOST_DIR}:${MOUNT}:rw\"]|" "$CFG"
fi
runner_env_merge "npm_config_cache=${MOUNT}/npm" "SONAR_USER_HOME=${MOUNT}/sonar"
systemctl restart gitlab-runner
sleep 3

docker run --rm --user 1000:1000 -v "${HOST_DIR}:${MOUNT}:rw" alpine:3.20 \
  sh -c "touch ${MOUNT}/npm/.probe && rm ${MOUNT}/npm/.probe && echo 'non-root write OK'" \
  || die "containers cannot write to ${HOST_DIR}"

grep -nE 'volumes =|environment =' "$CFG"
gitlab-runner verify 2>&1 | grep -E 'is valid|is not valid' || true
log "OK: shared cache ${HOST_DIR} mounted as ${MOUNT} in all jobs"
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 755 "$R/runner/install/shared-cache.sh"

install -d "$R/runner/install"
cat > "$R/runner/install/update.sh" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Bring a server built from this repo up to the current repo state, for the installed components only.
# Syncs scripts, systemd units and stack configs. Never touches secrets (passwords, config.toml, tokens),
# the runner registration, daemon.json or SSH settings. In the stacks' .env it only sets MON_UID and MON_GID
# (logging also DOCKER_GID) and keeps the file root-only at mode 600. For logging it also creates or removes
# /opt/logging/docker-compose.override.yml (extra group_add for the journal gid, only when it differs from docker's).
set -Eeuo pipefail
# shellcheck source=../../common/lib.sh
source "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")/../../common/lib.sh"

BACKUP_ROOT=/var/backups/server-init-config
KEEP_BACKUPS=5
BACKUP_DIR="${BACKUP_ROOT}/$(date +%Y%m%d-%H%M%S)"
LOCK=/run/lock/server-init-update.lock
DRYRUN=0
DO_OS=0
DO_PULL=0
DO_ROLLBACK=0
CHANGED=()
SKIPPED=()
WORK=""

usage() {
  cat <<USAGE
Usage: $0 [--dry-run] [--pull] [--os] [--rollback]

Updates what is installed on this server to the state of this repo checkout:
  host tools     health, cleanup, disk guard (+ prewarm if configured), their systemd units, kernel hardening and core dump settings
  monitoring     /opt/monitoring (Prometheus, Grafana, node_exporter, Caddy override if present)
  logging        /opt/logging (Loki, Grafana Alloy)
  registry-cache /opt/registry-cache
  npm-cache      /opt/npm-cache
Components that are not installed are skipped.

  -n, --dry-run  show what would change, change nothing
      --pull     git pull --ff-only in this checkout first (needs a git clone, not an scp copy)
      --os       also upgrade OS packages (Docker, GitLab Runner included); never reboots
      --rollback restore the files from the newest backup in ${BACKUP_ROOT} and recreate the stacks

Steps that restart Docker-based caches or upgrade packages are skipped while CI jobs are running
and the script exits with code 3; rerun it when the runner is idle.
Every changed file is first copied to ${BACKUP_ROOT}/<timestamp>/ (last ${KEEP_BACKUPS} runs are kept).
USAGE
}

run() {
  if (( DRYRUN )); then
    log "[dry-run] $*"
  else
    "$@"
  fi
}

backup_to_run() {
  local dst="$1"
  [[ -e "$dst" ]] || return 0
  install -d -m 0700 "$BACKUP_ROOT"
  install -d "${BACKUP_DIR}$(dirname "$dst")"
  cp -a "$dst" "${BACKUP_DIR}${dst}"
}

# sync_file SRC DST MODE [replace|inplace]
# Returns 0 if DST was (or would be) changed, 1 if already identical.
# "inplace" keeps the inode: required for files that are single-file bind mounts in containers.
# "replace" writes a new file: required for scripts that may be running right now.
sync_file() {
  local src="$1" dst="$2" mode="$3" how="${4:-replace}" want_mode
  [[ -f "$src" ]] || die "missing source file $src"
  if [[ -f "$dst" ]] && cmp -s "$src" "$dst"; then
    if [[ "$how" == inplace ]]; then
      return 1
    fi
    want_mode=$(printf '%o' "$((8#${mode}))")
    if [[ "$(stat -c '%a' "$dst")" == "$want_mode" ]]; then
      return 1
    fi
  fi
  if (( DRYRUN )); then
    log "[dry-run] would update ${dst}"
    return 0
  fi
  backup_to_run "$dst"
  if [[ "$how" == inplace && -f "$dst" ]]; then
    cat "$src" > "$dst"
  else
    install -d "$(dirname "$dst")"
    install -m "$mode" "$src" "$dst"
  fi
  restorecon_if "$dst"
  log "updated ${dst}"
  return 0
}

# sync_tree SRC_DIR DST_DIR MODE [replace|inplace]: returns 0 if any file changed.
sync_tree() {
  local src="$1" dst="$2" mode="$3" how="${4:-replace}" f rel rc=1
  while IFS= read -r -d '' f; do
    rel="${f#"${src}"/}"
    if sync_file "$f" "${dst}/${rel}" "$mode" "$how"; then
      rc=0
    fi
  done < <(find "$src" -type f -print0)
  return "$rc"
}

restore_files() {
  local f
  [[ -d "$BACKUP_DIR" ]] || return 0
  log "restoring files from ${BACKUP_DIR}"
  while IFS= read -r -d '' f; do
    if (( DRYRUN )); then
      log "[dry-run] would restore ${f#"${BACKUP_DIR}"}"
    else
      cp -a "$f" "${f#"${BACKUP_DIR}"}"
    fi
  done < <(find "$BACKUP_DIR" -type f -print0)
  run systemctl daemon-reload
}

prune_backups() {
  [[ -d "$BACKUP_ROOT" ]] || return 0
  find "$BACKUP_ROOT" -mindepth 1 -maxdepth 1 -type d | sort | head -n "-${KEEP_BACKUPS}" | xargs -r rm -rf --
}

need_idle() {
  local n
  n=$(ci_jobs_running)
  if (( n == 0 )); then
    return 0
  fi
  SKIPPED+=("$1")
  log "WARN: ${n} CI job container(s) running, skipping $1 (rerun when idle)"
  return 1
}

wait_url() {
  local url="$1" tries="${2:-30}" i
  for ((i = 0; i < tries; i++)); do
    if curl -fsS --max-time 3 "$url" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
      return 0
    fi
    sleep 2
  done
  return 1
}

reboot_needed() {
  local running latest
  if [[ -f /var/run/reboot-required ]]; then
    return 0
  fi
  if command -v rpm >/dev/null 2>&1 && rpm -q kernel-core >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    running=$(uname -r)
    latest=$(rpm -q kernel-core --qf '%{VERSION}-%{RELEASE}.%{ARCH}\n' | sort -V | tail -n 1)
    if [[ "$running" != "$latest" ]]; then
      return 0
    fi
  fi
  return 1
}

update_os() {
  need_idle "os-packages" || return 0
  if (( DRYRUN )); then
    log "[dry-run] would upgrade OS packages"
    return 0
  fi
  log "upgrading OS packages"
  if command -v dnf >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    dnf -y upgrade --refresh
  elif command -v apt-get >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    apt-get update -q
    DEBIAN_FRONTEND=noninteractive apt-get -y -q -o Dpkg::Options::=--force-confold upgrade
  else
    die "no supported package manager"
  fi
  CHANGED+=("os-packages")
  local svc
  for svc in docker gitlab-runner; do
    if systemctl cat "$svc" >/dev/null 2>&1 && ! systemctl is-active --quiet "$svc"; then
      log "WARN: ${svc} is not active after the upgrade"
    fi
  done
  if reboot_needed; then
    log "REBOOT RECOMMENDED (new kernel or libraries). Do it when the runner is idle; this script never reboots."
  fi
}

script_path() { if [[ "$1" == health ]]; then echo common/health; else echo "runner/scripts/$1"; fi; }

update_host_tools() {
  local f u existed units_changed=0 cleanup_changed=0 isolation_changed=0
  local -a scripts=(health runner-weekly-cleanup.sh runner-disk-guard.sh runner-engine-update.sh ci-job-isolation.sh)
  local -a units=(runner-weekly-cleanup.service runner-weekly-cleanup.timer runner-disk-guard.service runner-disk-guard.timer
                  runner-engine-update.service runner-engine-update.timer ci-job-isolation.service)
  local -a new_timers=() changed_timers=()

  if [[ -f /etc/ci-prewarm.conf ]]; then
    scripts+=(ci-image-prewarm.sh)
    units+=(ci-image-prewarm.service ci-image-prewarm.timer)
  fi

  # health is shared with plain servers (common/); the other scripts belong to the runner host.
  for f in "${scripts[@]}"; do
    bash -n "${REPO_ROOT}/$(script_path "$f")" || die "syntax error in $(script_path "$f"), nothing changed"
  done

  for f in "${scripts[@]}"; do
    if sync_file "${REPO_ROOT}/$(script_path "$f")" "/usr/local/bin/${f}" 0755; then
      CHANGED+=("scripts/${f}")
      if [[ "$f" == runner-weekly-cleanup.sh ]]; then
        cleanup_changed=1
      fi
      if [[ "$f" == ci-job-isolation.sh ]]; then
        isolation_changed=1
      fi
    fi
  done

  for u in "${units[@]}"; do
    existed=0
    if [[ -f "/etc/systemd/system/${u}" ]]; then
      existed=1
    fi
    if sync_file "${REPO_ROOT}/runner/systemd/${u}" "/etc/systemd/system/${u}" 0644; then
      CHANGED+=("systemd/${u}")
      units_changed=1
      if [[ "$u" == *.timer ]]; then
        if (( existed )); then
          changed_timers+=("$u")
        else
          new_timers+=("$u")
        fi
      fi
    fi
  done

  # Kernel hardening and no core dumps (also written by the cloud-init files and by host-tools.sh).
  local ktmp kernel_changed=0
  ktmp=$(mktemp -d)
  printf '* hard core 0\n' > "${ktmp}/50-nocore.conf"
  printf '[Coredump]\nStorage=none\nProcessSizeMax=0\n' > "${ktmp}/50-off.conf"
  if sync_file "${REPO_ROOT}/common/sysctl-hardening.conf" /etc/sysctl.d/90-ci-hardening.conf 0644; then
    CHANGED+=("sysctl/90-ci-hardening.conf"); kernel_changed=1
  fi
  if sync_file "${ktmp}/50-nocore.conf" /etc/security/limits.d/50-nocore.conf 0644; then CHANGED+=("limits.d/50-nocore.conf"); fi
  if sync_file "${ktmp}/50-off.conf" /etc/systemd/coredump.conf.d/50-off.conf 0644; then CHANGED+=("coredump.conf.d/50-off.conf"); fi
  rm -rf "$ktmp"
  if (( kernel_changed )); then run sysctl --system >/dev/null; fi

  if (( units_changed )); then
    run systemctl daemon-reload
    for u in "${new_timers[@]}"; do
      run systemctl enable --now "$u"
    done
    for u in "${changed_timers[@]}"; do
      if (( DRYRUN )) || systemctl is-active --quiet "$u"; then
        run systemctl restart "$u"
      fi
    done
  fi

  # The isolation rules live in iptables: apply them when the script is new or changed and Docker is up.
  if command -v docker >/dev/null 2>&1 && systemctl is-active --quiet docker 2>/dev/null; then
    if ! systemctl is-enabled --quiet ci-job-isolation.service 2>/dev/null; then
      run systemctl enable --now ci-job-isolation.service
    elif (( isolation_changed )); then
      run systemctl restart ci-job-isolation.service
    fi
  fi

  if (( cleanup_changed && ! DRYRUN )); then
    if ! DRY_RUN=1 /usr/local/bin/runner-weekly-cleanup.sh >/dev/null; then
      restore_files
      die "new runner-weekly-cleanup.sh fails its dry run, files restored"
    fi
  fi
  log "host tools: done"
}

update_monitoring() {
  local dir=/opt/monitoring stage="${WORK}/monitoring" prom_image zopt="" prom=0 graf=0 comp=0
  if [[ ! -f "${dir}/docker-compose.yml" ]]; then
    log "monitoring: not installed, skipping"
    return 0
  fi

  install -d -m 0755 "$stage"
  cp -a "${REPO_ROOT}/runner/stacks/monitoring/prometheus/." "${stage}/"
  if [[ -f /opt/registry-cache/docker-compose.yml ]] && ! grep -q 'job_name: registry-cache' "${stage}/prometheus.yml"; then
    cat >> "${stage}/prometheus.yml" <<'YAML'
  - job_name: registry-cache
    static_configs:
      - targets: ["127.0.0.1:5001"]
YAML
  fi
  if [[ -f /opt/logging/docker-compose.yml ]] && ! grep -q 'job_name: loki' "${stage}/prometheus.yml"; then
    prom_jobs_logging >> "${stage}/prometheus.yml"
  fi
  chmod -R a+rX "$stage"

  if command -v jq >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    jq empty "${REPO_ROOT}/runner/stacks/monitoring/grafana/dashboards/gitlab-runner.json" || die "invalid dashboard JSON, nothing changed"
  fi
  if selinux_on; then
    zopt=",z"
  fi
  prom_image=$(awk '/image: prom\/prometheus/ { print $2 }' "${REPO_ROOT}/runner/stacks/monitoring/docker-compose.yml")
  docker run --rm --entrypoint promtool -v "${stage}:/etc/prometheus:ro${zopt}" "$prom_image" \
    check config /etc/prometheus/prometheus.yml || die "promtool rejected the new Prometheus config, nothing changed"

  if sync_file "${stage}/prometheus.yml" "${dir}/prometheus/prometheus.yml" 0644 inplace; then
    prom=1
    CHANGED+=("monitoring/prometheus.yml")
  fi
  if sync_file "${stage}/alerts.yml" "${dir}/prometheus/alerts.yml" 0644 inplace; then
    prom=1
    CHANGED+=("monitoring/alerts.yml")
  fi
  if sync_tree "${REPO_ROOT}/runner/stacks/monitoring/grafana" "${dir}/grafana" 0644 inplace; then
    graf=1
    CHANGED+=("monitoring/grafana")
  fi
  if sync_file "${REPO_ROOT}/runner/stacks/monitoring/docker-compose.yml" "${dir}/docker-compose.yml" 0644 inplace; then
    comp=1
    CHANGED+=("monitoring/docker-compose.yml")
  fi
  if (( DRYRUN )); then
    id "$MON_USER" >/dev/null 2>&1 || log "WARN: service user ${MON_USER} missing: run runner/install/users.sh before applying"
    return 0
  fi

  stack_ids "$dir"
  own_stack_dir "$dir"
  if ! (cd "$dir" && docker compose config -q); then
    restore_files
    die "monitoring: compose config invalid, files restored"
  fi
  if (( comp )); then
    (cd "$dir" && docker compose pull -q)
  fi
  chown_volumes "$dir"
  (cd "$dir" && docker compose up -d)
  if ! wait_url http://127.0.0.1:9090/-/ready 30; then
    (cd "$dir" && docker compose logs --tail 30 prometheus) || true
    restore_files
    (cd "$dir" && docker compose up -d --force-recreate) || true
    die "monitoring: prometheus not ready, files restored"
  fi
  if (( prom )); then
    curl -fsS -X POST http://127.0.0.1:9090/-/reload || log "WARN: prometheus reload failed"
  fi
  if (( graf )); then
    (cd "$dir" && docker compose restart grafana)
  fi
  wait_url http://127.0.0.1:3000/api/health 30 || log "WARN: grafana did not report healthy yet"
  log "monitoring: done"
}

# stack_sync NAME DIR CONFIG_SRC CONFIG_NAME COMPOSE_SRC HEALTH_URL
# Single-config stacks (registry, verdaccio). A change recreates the container, so it waits for an idle runner.
stack_sync() {
  local name="$1" dir="$2" cfg_src="$3" cfg_name="$4" compose_src="$5" url="$6" pending=0
  if ! cmp -s "$cfg_src" "${dir}/${cfg_name}" || ! cmp -s "$compose_src" "${dir}/docker-compose.yml"; then
    pending=1
  fi

  if (( DRYRUN )); then
    if (( pending )); then
      sync_file "$cfg_src" "${dir}/${cfg_name}" 0644 inplace || true
      sync_file "$compose_src" "${dir}/docker-compose.yml" 0644 inplace || true
      CHANGED+=("$name")
    else
      log "${name}: up to date"
    fi
    return 0
  fi

  if (( pending )); then
    need_idle "$name" || return 0
    sync_file "$cfg_src" "${dir}/${cfg_name}" 0644 inplace || true
    sync_file "$compose_src" "${dir}/docker-compose.yml" 0644 inplace || true
    CHANGED+=("$name")
    if ! (cd "$dir" && docker compose config -q); then
      restore_files
      die "${name}: compose config invalid, files restored"
    fi
    (cd "$dir" && docker compose pull -q && docker compose up -d --force-recreate)
  else
    (cd "$dir" && docker compose up -d)
  fi

  if ! wait_url "$url" 20; then
    (cd "$dir" && docker compose logs --tail 30) || true
    if (( pending )); then
      restore_files
      (cd "$dir" && docker compose up -d --force-recreate) || true
    fi
    die "${name}: not healthy at ${url}"
  fi
  log "${name}: done"
}

# Loki + Alloy. Restarting them does not affect CI jobs, so no idle check.
update_logging() {
  local dir=/opt/logging ds=/opt/monitoring/grafana/provisioning/datasources/loki.yml f changed=0 ds_changed=0 ok=0
  if [[ ! -f "${dir}/docker-compose.yml" ]]; then
    log "logging: not installed, skipping"
    return 0
  fi
  for f in loki.yaml config.alloy docker-compose.yml; do
    if sync_file "${REPO_ROOT}/runner/stacks/logging/${f}" "${dir}/${f}" 0644 inplace; then
      changed=1
      CHANGED+=("logging/${f}")
    fi
  done
  if [[ -f /opt/monitoring/docker-compose.yml ]] && sync_file "${REPO_ROOT}/runner/stacks/logging/grafana-datasource.yml" "$ds" 0644 inplace; then
    ds_changed=1
    CHANGED+=("logging/grafana-datasource.yml")
  fi
  if (( DRYRUN )); then
    id "$MON_USER" >/dev/null 2>&1 || log "WARN: service user ${MON_USER} missing: run runner/install/users.sh before applying"
    return 0
  fi

  stack_ids "$dir" logging
  own_stack_dir "$dir"
  if ! (cd "$dir" && docker compose config -q); then
    restore_files
    die "logging: compose config invalid, files restored"
  fi
  if (( changed )); then
    (cd "$dir" && docker compose pull -q)
  fi
  chown_volumes "$dir"
  if (( changed )); then
    (cd "$dir" && docker compose up -d --force-recreate)
  else
    (cd "$dir" && docker compose up -d)
  fi
  for _ in $(seq 1 45); do
    if wait_url http://127.0.0.1:3100/ready 1 && wait_url http://127.0.0.1:12345/-/ready 1; then
      ok=1
      break
    fi
  done
  if (( ! ok )); then
    (cd "$dir" && docker compose logs --tail 30) || true
    if (( changed )); then
      restore_files
      (cd "$dir" && docker compose up -d --force-recreate) || true
    fi
    die "logging: Loki or Alloy not ready"
  fi
  if (( ds_changed )); then
    (cd /opt/monitoring && docker compose restart grafana >/dev/null)
  fi
  log "logging: done"
}

update_registry_cache() {
  local dir=/opt/registry-cache
  if [[ ! -f "${dir}/docker-compose.yml" ]]; then
    log "registry-cache: not installed, skipping"
    return 0
  fi
  stack_sync registry-cache "$dir" "${REPO_ROOT}/runner/stacks/registry-cache/config.yml" config.yml \
    "${REPO_ROOT}/runner/stacks/registry-cache/docker-compose.yml" http://127.0.0.1:5000/v2/
}

update_npm_cache() {
  local dir=/opt/npm-cache bridge cfg="${WORK}/npm-config.yaml" compose="${WORK}/npm-compose.yml"
  if [[ ! -f "${dir}/docker-compose.yml" ]]; then
    log "npm-cache: not installed, skipping"
    return 0
  fi
  bridge=$(ip -4 -o addr show docker0 2>/dev/null | awk '{ print $4 }' | cut -d/ -f1 || true)
  if [[ -z "$bridge" ]]; then
    SKIPPED+=("npm-cache")
    log "WARN: docker0 bridge not found, skipping npm-cache"
    return 0
  fi
  sed "s/172\.17\.0\.1:4873/${bridge}:4873/" "${REPO_ROOT}/runner/stacks/npm-cache/config.yaml" > "$cfg"
  sed "s/172\.17\.0\.1:4873/${bridge}:4873/" "${REPO_ROOT}/runner/stacks/npm-cache/docker-compose.yml" > "$compose"
  stack_sync npm-cache "$dir" "$cfg" config.yaml "$compose" "http://${bridge}:4873/-/ping"
}

do_rollback() {
  local latest
  latest=$(find "$BACKUP_ROOT" -mindepth 1 -maxdepth 1 -type d 2>/dev/null | sort | tail -n 1 || true)
  [[ -n "$latest" ]] || die "no backups in ${BACKUP_ROOT}"
  BACKUP_DIR="$latest"
  restore_files
  if (( DRYRUN )); then
    return 0
  fi
  if command -v docker >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    if [[ -f /opt/monitoring/docker-compose.yml ]]; then
      (cd /opt/monitoring && docker compose up -d --force-recreate)
    fi
    if [[ -f /opt/logging/docker-compose.yml ]]; then
      (cd /opt/logging && docker compose up -d --force-recreate)
    fi
    if need_idle "stack-recreate"; then
      if [[ -f /opt/registry-cache/docker-compose.yml ]]; then
        (cd /opt/registry-cache && docker compose up -d --force-recreate)
      fi
      if [[ -f /opt/npm-cache/docker-compose.yml ]]; then
        (cd /opt/npm-cache && docker compose up -d --force-recreate)
      fi
    fi
  fi
  log "rollback done. Running update.sh again re-applies the repo state."
}

summary() {
  local verb="updated" item
  if (( DRYRUN )); then
    verb="would update"
  fi
  if (( ${#CHANGED[@]} )); then
    log "${verb}:"
    for item in "${CHANGED[@]}"; do
      printf '  - %s\n' "$item"
    done
  else
    log "everything is up to date"
  fi
  if (( ${#SKIPPED[@]} )); then
    log "SKIPPED (rerun when idle): ${SKIPPED[*]}"
  fi
}

main() {
  while (( $# )); do
    case "$1" in
      -n|--dry-run) DRYRUN=1 ;;
      --pull) DO_PULL=1 ;;
      --os) DO_OS=1 ;;
      --rollback) DO_ROLLBACK=1 ;;
      -h|--help) usage; exit 0 ;;
      *) usage >&2; die "unknown option: $1" ;;
    esac
    shift
  done

  require_root
  exec 9>"$LOCK"
  flock -n 9 || die "another update is running"
  WORK=$(mktemp -d)
  trap 'rm -rf "$WORK"' EXIT
  trap 'log "ERROR at line ${LINENO}: update aborted. Changed files are backed up in ${BACKUP_DIR} (runner/install/update.sh --rollback)"' ERR

  if (( DO_ROLLBACK )); then
    do_rollback
    if (( ${#SKIPPED[@]} )); then
      exit 3
    fi
    return 0
  fi

  if (( DO_PULL )); then
    [[ -d "${REPO_ROOT}/.git" ]] || die "--pull needs a git clone, this copy has no .git"
    run git -C "$REPO_ROOT" pull --ff-only
  fi

  if (( DO_OS )); then
    update_os
  fi
  update_host_tools
  if command -v docker >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    update_monitoring
    update_logging
    update_registry_cache
    update_npm_cache
  else
    log "docker not installed, skipping container stacks"
  fi

  if (( ! DRYRUN )); then
    prune_backups
  fi
  summary

  if (( ! DRYRUN )) && [[ -x /usr/local/bin/health ]]; then
    log "health check"
    /usr/local/bin/health -q --no-color || log "health exit code $? (1 = warnings, 2 = failures)"
  fi
  if (( ${#SKIPPED[@]} )); then
    exit 3
  fi
}

if [[ "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}" == "$0" ]]; then
  main "$@"
fi
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 755 "$R/runner/install/update.sh"

install -d "$R/runner/install"
cat > "$R/runner/install/users.sh" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Users and SSH access for the runner host. Self-contained (no lib.sh) so cloud-init can embed it.
#
#   admin          ops (ADMIN_USER): SSH by key, sudo without password, not in the docker group
#   gitlab-runner  service account: no shell, no password, no SSH; the runner service runs as this user
#                  with access to Docker through the docker group (root-equivalent, needed by the executor)
#   monitoring     service account: no shell, no password, no SSH; owns the monitoring and logging containers
#
# SSH: keys only, only members of ssh-users, port SSH_PORT (default 22; remembered from the existing drop-in), no agent or X11 forwarding,
# local port forwarding kept for tunnels.
# Root keeps SSH by key until --lock-root, which refuses to run while the admin has no key. With another
# SSH_PORT, sshd listens on 22 and SSH_PORT until --lock-root, which then closes 22. A locked server stays
# locked on rerun unless --unlock-root is given. Safe to rerun.
set -Eeuo pipefail

ADMIN_USER="${ADMIN_USER:-ops}"
MON_USER="${MON_USER:-monitoring}"
RUNNER_USER=gitlab-runner
SSH_GROUP="${SSH_GROUP:-ssh-users}"
DROPIN=/etc/ssh/sshd_config.d/00-ci-hardening.conf
# SSH_PORT from the environment wins; otherwise the port written by the previous run is kept, so a rerun,
# --lock-root and --unlock-root never fall back to 22 on a host that listens elsewhere.
if [[ -z "${SSH_PORT:-}" ]]; then
  SSH_PORT=$(awk 'tolower($1) == "port" && $2 != 22 { p = $2 } END { print p }' "$DROPIN" 2>/dev/null || true)
fi
SSH_PORT="${SSH_PORT:-22}"
RUNNER_DROPIN=/etc/systemd/system/gitlab-runner.service.d/10-service-user.conf
RUNNER_CFG=/etc/gitlab-runner/config.toml
LOCK_ROOT=0
UNLOCK_ROOT=0
RELOAD=1

log() { echo "[$(date +%H:%M:%S)] $*"; }
die() { echo "ERROR: $*" >&2; exit 1; }

usage() {
  cat <<USAGE
Usage: $0 [--lock-root | --unlock-root] [--no-reload]

Creates the admin user (ADMIN_USER, default ops), the service
accounts (${MON_USER}, ${RUNNER_USER} if installed), the ${SSH_GROUP} group and the SSH drop-in ${DROPIN}.
The admin gets the keys from /root/.ssh/authorized_keys.

  --lock-root    close root SSH (and port 22 when SSH_PORT is another port). Run it only after
                 "ssh ADMIN@server sudo -n true" works from your laptop.
  --unlock-root  undo --lock-root (root by key again)
  --no-reload    write the config but do not reload sshd or restart gitlab-runner

Env: ADMIN_USER, MON_USER (default monitoring), SSH_GROUP (default ssh-users),
     SSH_PORT (default 22, or the port already in ${DROPIN}; give it once, reruns keep it)
Rollback: delete ${DROPIN} and ${RUNNER_DROPIN}, then: systemctl reload sshd; systemctl daemon-reload; systemctl restart gitlab-runner
USAGE
}

while (( $# )); do
  case "$1" in
    --lock-root) LOCK_ROOT=1 ;;
    --unlock-root) UNLOCK_ROOT=1 ;;
    --no-reload) RELOAD=0 ;;
    -h|--help) usage; exit 0 ;;
    *) usage >&2; die "unknown option: $1" ;;
  esac
  shift
done

[[ $EUID -eq 0 ]] || die "run as root (sudo $0)"
(( LOCK_ROOT && UNLOCK_ROOT )) && die "--lock-root and --unlock-root exclude each other"
if ! [[ "$SSH_PORT" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]] || (( SSH_PORT < 1 || SSH_PORT > 65535 )); then die "invalid SSH_PORT: ${SSH_PORT}"; fi
[[ "$ADMIN_USER" =~ ^[a-z_][a-z0-9_-]{0,31}$ ]] || die "invalid ADMIN_USER: '${ADMIN_USER}'"
[[ "$ADMIN_USER" != root && "$ADMIN_USER" != "$MON_USER" && "$ADMIN_USER" != "$RUNNER_USER" ]] || die "ADMIN_USER must be a separate user"

NOLOGIN=$(command -v nologin || true)
[[ -n "$NOLOGIN" ]] || NOLOGIN=/sbin/nologin
if getent group wheel >/dev/null; then
  SUDO_GROUP=wheel
elif getent group sudo >/dev/null; then
  SUDO_GROUP=sudo
else
  die "neither wheel nor sudo group exists"
fi
command -v visudo >/dev/null || die "sudo is not installed"

# --- admin -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
getent group "$SSH_GROUP" >/dev/null || groupadd -r "$SSH_GROUP"
if id "$ADMIN_USER" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  usermod -aG "${SUDO_GROUP},${SSH_GROUP}" "$ADMIN_USER"
else
  useradd -m -s /bin/bash -G "${SUDO_GROUP},${SSH_GROUP}" -c "server admin" "$ADMIN_USER"
  log "created admin user ${ADMIN_USER}"
fi
# "!" (locked) can block key logins when sshd runs without PAM; "*" means no password, key login allowed.
if [[ "$(getent shadow "$ADMIN_USER" | cut -d: -f2)" == !* ]]; then
  usermod -p '*' "$ADMIN_USER"
fi
if id -nG "$ADMIN_USER" | tr ' ' '\n' | grep -qx docker; then
  gpasswd -d "$ADMIN_USER" docker >/dev/null
  log "removed ${ADMIN_USER} from the docker group (root-equivalent); use sudo docker"
fi

sudoers="/etc/sudoers.d/90-${ADMIN_USER}"
tmp=$(mktemp)
# secure_path: the Rocky default can leave out /usr/local/bin, where health and the cleanup scripts live.
printf '%s ALL=(ALL) NOPASSWD:ALL\nDefaults:%s secure_path="/usr/local/sbin:/usr/local/bin:/usr/sbin:/usr/bin:/sbin:/bin"\n' \
  "$ADMIN_USER" "$ADMIN_USER" > "$tmp"
visudo -cqf "$tmp" || { rm -f "$tmp"; die "generated sudoers file is invalid"; }
install -m 0440 -o root -g root "$tmp" "$sudoers"
rm -f "$tmp"

home=$(getent passwd "$ADMIN_USER" | cut -d: -f6)
group=$(id -gn "$ADMIN_USER")
ak="${home}/.ssh/authorized_keys"
install -d -m 0700 -o "$ADMIN_USER" -g "$group" "${home}/.ssh"
touch "$ak"
if [[ -f /root/.ssh/authorized_keys ]]; then
  # Plain key lines only: lines with options (for example cloud-init's "Please login as" command=) are skipped.
  while IFS= read -r line; do
    grep -qxF -- "$line" "$ak" || printf '%s\n' "$line" >> "$ak"
  done < <(grep -E '^(ssh-(ed25519|rsa)|ecdsa-sha2-|sk-)' /root/.ssh/authorized_keys || true)
fi
chown "$ADMIN_USER:$group" "$ak"
chmod 0600 "$ak"
if [[ -f /root/.tmux.conf && ! -f "${home}/.tmux.conf" ]]; then
  install -m 0644 -o "$ADMIN_USER" -g "$group" /root/.tmux.conf "${home}/.tmux.conf"
fi
if command -v restorecon >/dev/null 2>&1; then restorecon -R "${home}/.ssh" || true; fi
keys=$(grep -cE '^(ssh-|ecdsa-|sk-)' "$ak" || true)
log "admin ${ADMIN_USER}: groups $(id -nG "$ADMIN_USER" | tr ' ' ','), ${keys:-0} SSH key(s), sudo without password"

# --- service accounts --------------------------------------------------------------------------
if ! id "$MON_USER" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  useradd -r -U -M -d /nonexistent -s "$NOLOGIN" -c "monitoring and logging containers" "$MON_USER"
  log "created service user ${MON_USER}"
fi
usermod -s "$NOLOGIN" "$MON_USER" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
log "service ${MON_USER}: uid $(id -u "$MON_USER"), gid $(id -g "$MON_USER"), shell ${NOLOGIN}"

if id "$RUNNER_USER" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  usermod -s "$NOLOGIN" "$RUNNER_USER" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
  usermod -L "$RUNNER_USER" 2>/dev/null || true
  getent group docker >/dev/null && usermod -aG docker "$RUNNER_USER"
  install -d -m 0755 "$(dirname "$RUNNER_DROPIN")"
  cat > "${RUNNER_DROPIN}.new" <<'EOF'
# Run the runner service as its own user instead of root. Docker access comes from the docker group.
[Service]
User=gitlab-runner
Group=gitlab-runner
SupplementaryGroups=docker
ExecStart=
ExecStart=/usr/bin/gitlab-runner run --working-directory /home/gitlab-runner --config /etc/gitlab-runner/config.toml --service gitlab-runner
EOF
  changed=0
  if ! cmp -s "${RUNNER_DROPIN}.new" "$RUNNER_DROPIN" 2>/dev/null; then
    mv "${RUNNER_DROPIN}.new" "$RUNNER_DROPIN"
    chmod 0644 "$RUNNER_DROPIN"
    changed=1
  else
    rm -f "${RUNNER_DROPIN}.new"
  fi
  # The service reads and rewrites config.toml (token, system id), so it owns /etc/gitlab-runner and its home.
  install -d -m 0700 -o "$RUNNER_USER" -g "$RUNNER_USER" /etc/gitlab-runner
  chown -R "$RUNNER_USER:$RUNNER_USER" /etc/gitlab-runner
  if [[ -f "$RUNNER_CFG" ]]; then chmod 0600 "$RUNNER_CFG"; fi
  install -d -m 0750 -o "$RUNNER_USER" -g "$RUNNER_USER" "/home/${RUNNER_USER}"
  chown -R "$RUNNER_USER:$RUNNER_USER" "/home/${RUNNER_USER}"
  if command -v restorecon >/dev/null 2>&1; then restorecon -R /etc/gitlab-runner "$(dirname "$RUNNER_DROPIN")" || true; fi
  if (( RELOAD )); then
    systemctl daemon-reload
    restart=0
    if (( changed )) || [[ "$(ps -o user= -p "$(systemctl show -p MainPID --value gitlab-runner)" 2>/dev/null | tr -d ' ')" != "$RUNNER_USER" ]]; then
      restart=1
    fi
    if (( restart )); then
      main_pid=$(systemctl show -p MainPID --value gitlab-runner)
      if [[ "${main_pid:-0}" == 0 ]]; then
        # Stopped service: nothing to interrupt, and the idle gate would only block the start.
        systemctl start gitlab-runner
        sleep 3
      elif ! job_ids=$(docker ps -q --filter "label=com.gitlab.gitlab-runner.managed=true" 2>/dev/null); then
        # A docker failure is not "0 jobs": restarting could kill running CI jobs.
        log "WARN: cannot confirm that no CI job is running (docker does not answer), gitlab-runner not restarted; check with: docker ps --filter label=com.gitlab.gitlab-runner.managed=true, then: systemctl restart gitlab-runner"
      elif [[ -n "$job_ids" ]]; then
        log "WARN: $(wc -w <<<"$job_ids") CI job(s) running, gitlab-runner not restarted; run: systemctl restart gitlab-runner"
      else
        systemctl restart gitlab-runner
        sleep 3
      fi
    fi
    pid=$(systemctl show -p MainPID --value gitlab-runner)
    if [[ "${pid:-0}" != 0 ]]; then
      log "service ${RUNNER_USER}: runner process runs as $(ps -o user= -p "$pid" | tr -d ' ') (groups: $(ps -o supgrp= -p "$pid" | tr -d ' '))"
    else
      log "WARN: gitlab-runner is not running: journalctl -u gitlab-runner -n 30"
    fi
  fi
else
  log "${RUNNER_USER} not installed yet, rerun this script after installing GitLab Runner"
fi

# --- SSH ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# A server that is already locked stays locked, so a rerun never reopens root or port 22 by accident.
if (( ! LOCK_ROOT && ! UNLOCK_ROOT )) && grep -qsx 'PermitRootLogin no' "$DROPIN"; then
  LOCK_ROOT=1
  log "keeping the existing root lock (use --unlock-root to undo it)"
fi
if (( LOCK_ROOT )) && (( ${keys:-0} == 0 )); then
  die "--lock-root refused: ${ADMIN_USER} has no SSH key, root would be the only way in"
fi
if (( LOCK_ROOT )); then
  root_login=no
  allow="AllowGroups ${SSH_GROUP}"
  ports="Port ${SSH_PORT}"
else
  root_login=prohibit-password
  allow="AllowGroups ${SSH_GROUP} root"
  ports="Port 22"
  if [[ "$SSH_PORT" != 22 ]]; then ports+=$'\n'"Port ${SSH_PORT}"; fi
fi

# SELinux only lets sshd bind ports labeled ssh_port_t; firewalld (if active) must allow the port too.
if [[ "$SSH_PORT" != 22 ]] && command -v selinuxenabled >/dev/null 2>&1 && selinuxenabled; then
  command -v semanage >/dev/null 2>&1 || die "semanage missing: install policycoreutils-python-utils"
  if ! semanage port -l | awk '$1 == "ssh_port_t" { for (i = 3; i <= NF; i++) print $i }' | tr -d ',' | grep -qx "$SSH_PORT"; then
    semanage port -a -t ssh_port_t -p tcp "$SSH_PORT" 2>/dev/null || semanage port -m -t ssh_port_t -p tcp "$SSH_PORT"
    log "SELinux: port ${SSH_PORT} labeled ssh_port_t"
  fi
fi
if systemctl is-active --quiet firewalld 2>/dev/null; then
  firewall-cmd -q --permanent --add-port="${SSH_PORT}/tcp" && firewall-cmd -q --reload
  log "firewalld: port ${SSH_PORT}/tcp allowed"
fi
# Debian runner hosts get ufw from host-firewall.sh: the new port must be open there too, or sshd listens on a port the
# firewall drops (and --lock-root, which closes port 22, would then lock you out).
if command -v ufw >/dev/null 2>&1 && ufw status 2>/dev/null | grep -q '^Status: active'; then
  ufw allow "${SSH_PORT}/tcp" >/dev/null || die "cannot allow ${SSH_PORT}/tcp in ufw"
  log "ufw: port ${SSH_PORT}/tcp allowed"
fi
# Add this port to the fail2ban sshd jail written by host-firewall.sh.
JAIL_FILE="${JAIL_FILE:-/etc/fail2ban/jail.d/50-sshd.local}"
if [[ -f "$JAIL_FILE" ]] && grep -q '^port ' "$JAIL_FILE" && ! grep -E '^port ' "$JAIL_FILE" | tr -c '0-9\n' ' ' | tr ' ' '\n' | grep -qx "$SSH_PORT"; then
  sed -i -E "s/^(port[[:space:]]*=[[:space:]]*.*)\$/\1,${SSH_PORT}/" "$JAIL_FILE"
  systemctl reload fail2ban >/dev/null 2>&1 || systemctl restart fail2ban >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
  log "fail2ban: sshd jail now also watches port ${SSH_PORT}"
fi
# Never write a config that leaves SSH only on a port the running host firewall drops.
if [[ "$SSH_PORT" != 22 ]]; then
  if systemctl is-active --quiet firewalld 2>/dev/null && ! firewall-cmd --query-port="${SSH_PORT}/tcp" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    die "firewalld does not allow ${SSH_PORT}/tcp in the running rules; sshd config left unchanged"
  fi
  if command -v ufw >/dev/null 2>&1 && ufw status 2>/dev/null | grep -q '^Status: active' \
     && ! ufw status 2>/dev/null | grep -qE "^${SSH_PORT}/tcp([[:space:]]+\(v6\))?[[:space:]]+(ALLOW|LIMIT)"; then
    die "ufw does not allow ${SSH_PORT}/tcp in the running rules; sshd config left unchanged"
  fi
fi
[[ -d /etc/ssh/sshd_config.d ]] || die "/etc/ssh/sshd_config.d missing (OpenSSH too old for drop-ins?)"
grep -qiE '^\s*Include\s+/etc/ssh/sshd_config.d/' /etc/ssh/sshd_config || die "/etc/ssh/sshd_config does not include sshd_config.d"

new="${DROPIN}.new"
cat > "$new" <<EOF
# Managed by runner/install/users.sh. First match wins in sshd, so this file is named 00- to load first.
${ports}
PasswordAuthentication no
KbdInteractiveAuthentication no
PubkeyAuthentication yes
PermitRootLogin ${root_login}
${allow}
MaxAuthTries 3
LoginGraceTime 30
ClientAliveInterval 300
ClientAliveCountMax 2
X11Forwarding no
AllowAgentForwarding no
AllowTcpForwarding local
PermitTunnel no
EOF
chmod 0600 "$new"
old_backup=""
if [[ -f "$DROPIN" ]] && cmp -s "$new" "$DROPIN"; then
  rm -f "$new"   # same content: no backup, no rewrite, a rerun leaves the managed state untouched
else
  if [[ -f "$DROPIN" ]]; then
    old_backup="${DROPIN}.bak.$(date +%Y%m%d-%H%M%S)"
    cp -a "$DROPIN" "$old_backup"
  fi
  mv "$new" "$DROPIN"
fi
legacy=/etc/ssh/sshd_config.d/60-ci-hardening.conf
[[ -f "$legacy" ]] && mv "$legacy" "${legacy}.disabled"
if command -v restorecon >/dev/null 2>&1; then restorecon /etc/ssh/sshd_config.d/* 2>/dev/null || true; fi

sshd_bin=$(command -v sshd || echo /usr/sbin/sshd)
if ! "$sshd_bin" -t; then
  if [[ -n "$old_backup" ]]; then mv "$old_backup" "$DROPIN"; else rm -f "$DROPIN"; fi
  [[ -f "${legacy}.disabled" ]] && mv "${legacy}.disabled" "$legacy"
  die "sshd rejected the new config, previous config restored"
fi
if (( RELOAD )); then
  if systemctl is-enabled --quiet ssh.socket 2>/dev/null; then
    # Socket activation (Ubuntu, some Debian setups): the socket generator reads Port from sshd_config.
    systemctl daemon-reload
    systemctl restart ssh.socket
  elif systemctl cat sshd >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    systemctl reload sshd
  else
    systemctl reload ssh
  fi
fi
effective=$("$sshd_bin" -T 2>/dev/null | grep -Ei '^(port|permitrootlogin|passwordauthentication|allowgroups|allowtcpforwarding) ' | tr '\n' ';' || true)
log "sshd: ${effective}"

port_opt=""
if [[ "$SSH_PORT" != 22 ]]; then port_opt="-p ${SSH_PORT} "; fi
if (( LOCK_ROOT )); then
  log "OK: root SSH closed. Log in as: ssh ${port_opt}${ADMIN_USER}@<server>"
else
  if [[ -n "$port_opt" ]]; then log "OK: allow TCP ${SSH_PORT} in the Hetzner Firewall first"; fi
  log "OK: test from your laptop:  ssh ${port_opt}${ADMIN_USER}@<server> 'sudo -n true && echo sudo works'"
  log "    then lock root:         sudo $0 --lock-root"
fi
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 755 "$R/runner/install/users.sh"

install -d "$R/runner/scripts"
cat > "$R/runner/scripts/ci-image-prewarm.sh" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Pre-pull every tag of the CI images listed in /etc/ci-prewarm.conf so jobs start from local images.
set -Eeuo pipefail

CONF="${CONF:-/etc/ci-prewarm.conf}"
log() { logger -t ci-image-prewarm -- "$*"; echo "$(date +%FT%T) $*"; }

if [[ "${1:-}" == "-h" || "${1:-}" == "--help" ]]; then
  echo "Usage: $0  (pulls all tags of REPOS under BASE from $CONF)"
  exit 0
fi
[[ $EUID -eq 0 ]] || { echo "run as root" >&2; exit 1; }
command -v crane >/dev/null 2>&1 || { log "ERROR: crane not installed"; exit 1; }
[[ -f "$CONF" ]] || { log "ERROR: $CONF not found"; exit 1; }

BASE=""
REPOS=()
# shellcheck disable=SC1090
source "$CONF"
[[ -n "$BASE" && ${#REPOS[@]} -gt 0 ]] || { log "ERROR: BASE or REPOS empty in $CONF"; exit 1; }

exec 9>/run/ci-image-prewarm.lock
flock -n 9 || { log "another prewarm is running, exit"; exit 0; }

ok=0; noarm=0; fail=0
for repo in "${REPOS[@]}"; do
  if ! tags=$(crane ls "${BASE}/${repo}" 2>&1); then
    log "FAIL    ${BASE}/${repo}: cannot list tags: ${tags}"
    fail=$((fail + 1))
    continue
  fi
  for tag in $tags; do
    ref="${BASE}/${repo}:${tag}"
    if out=$(docker pull -q "$ref" 2>&1); then
      log "OK      $ref"
      ok=$((ok + 1))
    elif grep -qiE 'no matching manifest|does not match the specified platform' <<<"$out"; then
      log "NO ARM  $ref"
      noarm=$((noarm + 1))
    else
      log "FAIL    $ref: $out"
      fail=$((fail + 1))
    fi
  done
done

log "summary: ok=${ok} no_arm=${noarm} failed=${fail}, disk: $(df -h --output=pcent / | tail -1 | xargs)"
(( fail == 0 ))
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 755 "$R/runner/scripts/ci-image-prewarm.sh"

install -d "$R/runner/scripts"
cat > "$R/runner/scripts/ci-job-isolation.sh" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Keep CI job containers away from the cloud metadata service and from services on the host.
# Run at boot and after every Docker start by ci-job-isolation.service; safe to rerun any time.
#
#   DOCKER-USER   drops traffic to 169.254.169.254 (Hetzner metadata: server data, user-data, tokens).
#   INPUT         traffic from docker0 and the br-* bridges to the host itself goes through the chain CI-JOBS-IN:
#                 only replies, ping and the npm cache (docker0 IP, port 4873) are let in, the rest is dropped.
#
# Not affected: internet access of jobs, the Docker Hub cache (Docker pulls through the mirror on 127.0.0.1:5000,
# jobs do not), traffic between containers of one job, and the host-network stacks (monitoring, logging, caches).
# Only IPv4 is handled. Remove the rules: systemctl disable --now ci-job-isolation; then iptables -F CI-JOBS-IN
# and delete the two "ci-job-isolation" rules shown by iptables -S.
set -Eeuo pipefail

META_IP="${META_IP:-169.254.169.254}"
NPM_PORT="${NPM_PORT:-4873}"
TAG=ci-job-isolation

log() { logger -t "$TAG" -- "$*" 2>/dev/null || true; echo "$*"; }

if [[ "${1:-}" == "-h" || "${1:-}" == "--help" ]]; then
  echo "Usage: $0   blocks CI jobs from ${META_IP} and from host services (except the npm cache on the docker0 IP, port ${NPM_PORT})"
  exit 0
fi
[[ $EUID -eq 0 ]] || { echo "run as root" >&2; exit 1; }
command -v iptables >/dev/null 2>&1 || { log "iptables not found, nothing applied"; exit 1; }

# Docker creates DOCKER-USER when it starts; wait for it after a boot.
for _ in $(seq 1 30); do
  if iptables -n -L DOCKER-USER >/dev/null 2>&1; then break; fi
  sleep 1
done
iptables -n -L DOCKER-USER >/dev/null 2>&1 || { log "chain DOCKER-USER does not exist (is Docker running?), nothing applied"; exit 1; }

# Insert a rule at the top of a chain unless it is there already.
ensure() {
  local chain="$1"
  shift
  iptables -C "$chain" "$@" 2>/dev/null || iptables -I "$chain" 1 "$@"
}

ensure DOCKER-USER -d "$META_IP" -m comment --comment "$TAG" -j DROP

BRIDGE_IP="${BRIDGE_IP:-}"
if [[ -z "$BRIDGE_IP" ]] && command -v ip >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  BRIDGE_IP=$(ip -4 -o addr show docker0 2>/dev/null | awk '{ print $4 }' | cut -d/ -f1 | head -1 || true)
fi

iptables -N CI-JOBS-IN 2>/dev/null || true
iptables -F CI-JOBS-IN
iptables -A CI-JOBS-IN -m conntrack --ctstate RELATED,ESTABLISHED -j ACCEPT
iptables -A CI-JOBS-IN -p icmp --icmp-type echo-request -j ACCEPT
if [[ -n "$BRIDGE_IP" ]]; then
  iptables -A CI-JOBS-IN -i docker0 -d "$BRIDGE_IP" -p tcp --dport "$NPM_PORT" -j ACCEPT
fi
iptables -A CI-JOBS-IN -j DROP
ensure INPUT -i docker0 -m comment --comment "$TAG" -j CI-JOBS-IN
ensure INPUT -i 'br-+' -m comment --comment "$TAG" -j CI-JOBS-IN

log "applied: ${META_IP} blocked for containers; host services closed to job networks (npm cache: ${BRIDGE_IP:-none}:${NPM_PORT})"
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 755 "$R/runner/scripts/ci-job-isolation.sh"

install -d "$R/runner/scripts"
cat > "$R/runner/scripts/runner-disk-guard.sh" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Emergency cleanup when the root filesystem crosses HARD_LIMIT percent.
set -Eeuo pipefail

HARD_LIMIT="${HARD_LIMIT:-85}"

if [[ "${1:-}" == "-h" || "${1:-}" == "--help" ]]; then
  echo "Usage: $0  (env: HARD_LIMIT=85) runs runner-weekly-cleanup.sh with RETENTION_DAYS=1 when disk usage >= HARD_LIMIT%"
  exit 0
fi

used=$(df --output=pcent / | tail -1 | tr -dc '0-9')
if (( used >= HARD_LIMIT )); then
  logger -t runner-disk-guard -- "disk ${used}% >= ${HARD_LIMIT}%, running emergency cleanup"
  RETENTION_DAYS=1 /usr/local/bin/runner-weekly-cleanup.sh
fi
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 755 "$R/runner/scripts/runner-disk-guard.sh"

install -d "$R/runner/scripts"
cat > "$R/runner/scripts/runner-engine-update.sh" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Weekly upgrade of Docker CE and GitLab Runner, only while no CI job runs (the distro's automatic updates skip these
# vendor repos). The runner is stopped gracefully (no new job, running jobs finish; KillSignal=SIGQUIT), the job list is
# checked again, the Docker packages are upgraded and Docker restarted, then gitlab-runner is upgraded last and started
# (separate transactions, so no Docker restart happens after the runner is back). Runner and jobs are checked again
# before the Docker restart. If a job is there, the runner is started again and nothing (more) is changed.
# The result is one line in /var/log/runner-engine-update.log, which health reads.
# Env: DRY_RUN=1 shows the plan only; STOP_WAIT seconds to wait for the graceful stop (default 1800).
set -Eeuo pipefail

LOG="${ENGINE_UPDATE_LOG:-/var/log/runner-engine-update.log}"   # overridable for tests
DRY_RUN="${DRY_RUN:-0}"
STOP_WAIT="${STOP_WAIT:-1800}"
DROPIN="${ENGINE_UPDATE_DROPIN:-/etc/systemd/system/gitlab-runner.service.d/graceful-stop.conf}"
PKGS=(docker-ce docker-ce-cli containerd.io docker-buildx-plugin docker-compose-plugin gitlab-runner)

note() { logger -t runner-engine-update -- "$*" 2>/dev/null || true; echo "$(date -u +%FT%TZ) $*" | tee -a "$LOG"; }

if [[ "${1:-}" == "-h" || "${1:-}" == "--help" ]]; then
  echo "Usage: $0 [--dry-run]   upgrades ${PKGS[*]} when no CI job runs"
  exit 0
fi
[[ "${1:-}" == "--dry-run" ]] && DRY_RUN=1
[[ $EUID -eq 0 ]] || { echo "run as root" >&2; exit 1; }
exec 9>/run/runner-engine-update.lock
flock -n 9 || { note "SKIP another update is running"; exit 0; }

# A docker error is not "no jobs": updating then could kill running jobs.
if ! jobs=$(docker ps -q --filter "label=com.gitlab.gitlab-runner.managed=true" 2>/dev/null); then
  note "SKIP docker does not answer, cannot tell whether jobs run"
  exit 0
fi
if [[ -n "$jobs" ]]; then
  note "SKIP $(wc -w <<<"$jobs") CI job(s) running, will retry next week"
  exit 0
fi

installed=()
if command -v dnf >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  for p in "${PKGS[@]}"; do rpm -q "$p" >/dev/null 2>&1 && installed+=("$p"); done
  upgrade() { dnf -y -q upgrade "$@"; }
  pending() { dnf -q check-update "${installed[@]}" >/dev/null 2>&1; [[ $? -eq 100 ]]; }
elif command -v apt-get >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  for p in "${PKGS[@]}"; do dpkg -s "$p" >/dev/null 2>&1 && installed+=("$p"); done
  upgrade() { DEBIAN_FRONTEND=noninteractive apt-get install -y -q --only-upgrade "$@"; }
  pending() { apt-get update -q >/dev/null 2>&1; apt-get -s install --only-upgrade "${installed[@]}" 2>/dev/null | grep -q '^Inst '; }
else
  note "FAIL no supported package manager"
  exit 1
fi
(( ${#installed[@]} )) || { note "SKIP none of the packages is installed"; exit 0; }

if ! pending; then
  note "OK nothing to upgrade (${installed[*]})"
  exit 0
fi
# Two transactions: Docker packages first (they restart Docker), gitlab-runner last (its scriptlet starts the runner).
docker_pkgs=(); runner_pkgs=()
for p in "${installed[@]}"; do if [[ "$p" == gitlab-runner ]]; then runner_pkgs+=("$p"); else docker_pkgs+=("$p"); fi; done
if [[ "$DRY_RUN" == 1 ]]; then
  note "DRY-RUN would upgrade ${installed[*]}"
  exit 0
fi

before=$(for p in "${installed[@]}"; do if command -v rpm >/dev/null 2>&1; then rpm -q --qf '%{NAME}-%{VERSION}-%{RELEASE} ' "$p"; else dpkg-query -W -f='${Package}-${Version} ' "$p"; fi; done)

# From here the runner may be stopped; whatever happens, leave it running again. --no-block: if the runner is still
# finishing a job, systemd runs this start after the stop completes, and this script does not wait for it.
runner_stopped=0
runner_back() { systemctl start --no-block gitlab-runner >/dev/null 2>&1 || true; }
trap '[[ $runner_stopped -eq 1 ]] && runner_back' EXIT

# `systemctl is-active` is false while a unit is deactivating, so the state is read with `show`.
runner_state() { systemctl show -p ActiveState --value gitlab-runner 2>/dev/null || echo unknown; }
# SIGQUIT is the runner's graceful stop: no new job, running jobs finish. Returns 1 when it is still running after STOP_WAIT.
stop_runner_wait() {
  local waited=0 st
  systemctl stop --no-block gitlab-runner
  while :; do
    st=$(runner_state)
    [[ "$st" == inactive || "$st" == failed ]] && return 0
    (( waited >= STOP_WAIT )) && return 1
    sleep 5
    waited=$((waited + 5))
  done
}
no_jobs() { local j; j=$(docker ps -q --filter "label=com.gitlab.gitlab-runner.managed=true" 2>/dev/null) && [[ -z "$j" ]]; }

# The default SIGTERM ends running jobs. TimeoutStopSec=infinity: systemd never SIGKILLs a runner that is still
# waiting for a job; this script gives up waiting on its own (STOP_WAIT) and leaves the stop to finish by itself.
if ! grep -qs '^KillSignal=SIGQUIT' "$DROPIN" 2>/dev/null || ! grep -qs '^TimeoutStopSec=infinity' "$DROPIN" 2>/dev/null; then
  mkdir -p "$(dirname "$DROPIN")"
  printf '[Service]\nKillSignal=SIGQUIT\nTimeoutStopSec=infinity\n' > "$DROPIN"
  systemctl daemon-reload
fi
runner_stopped=1
if ! stop_runner_wait; then
  # Starting a unit that is still stopping queues the start behind the stop: the runner comes back when the job ends.
  note "SKIP gitlab-runner did not stop within ${STOP_WAIT}s (jobs still running); it starts again once it has stopped, will retry next week"
  exit 0
fi
if ! no_jobs; then
  note "SKIP a CI job appeared or docker does not answer after the runner stopped; runner started again, will retry next week"
  exit 0
fi

# Docker packages first, runner stopped: their own Docker restart finds no job.
if (( ${#docker_pkgs[@]} )) && ! upgrade "${docker_pkgs[@]}" >>"$LOG" 2>&1; then
  note "FAIL Docker package upgrade failed, see ${LOG}"
  exit 1
fi
# A package scriptlet may have started the runner (a unit file in /etc cannot be masked from /run), and it may have
# taken a job. Restarting Docker under a running job would kill it, so stop the runner again and look once more.
if [[ "$(runner_state)" != inactive ]]; then
  if ! stop_runner_wait; then
    note "SKIP Docker packages upgraded, but gitlab-runner is busy again; Docker not restarted, gitlab-runner not upgraded (new version active after the next restart or reboot, retry next week)"
    exit 0
  fi
fi
if ! no_jobs; then
  note "SKIP Docker packages upgraded, but a CI job runs or docker does not answer; Docker not restarted, gitlab-runner not upgraded (retry next week)"
  exit 0
fi
bad=""
if (( ${#docker_pkgs[@]} )); then
  systemctl restart docker
  sleep 5
  # The isolation rules live in iptables; reapply them after the Docker restart.
  if systemctl cat ci-job-isolation.service >/dev/null 2>&1; then systemctl restart ci-job-isolation.service || bad+=" ci-job-isolation"; fi
fi
if (( ${#runner_pkgs[@]} )) && ! upgrade "${runner_pkgs[@]}" >>"$LOG" 2>&1; then
  note "FAIL gitlab-runner upgrade failed, see ${LOG}"
  exit 1
fi
systemctl start gitlab-runner || true
runner_stopped=0
sleep 5
for s in docker gitlab-runner; do systemctl is-active --quiet "$s" || bad+=" $s"; done
if [[ -n "$bad" ]]; then
  note "FAIL not active after the upgrade:${bad}; was: ${before}"
  exit 1
fi
note "OK upgraded ${installed[*]}; was: ${before}"
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 755 "$R/runner/scripts/runner-engine-update.sh"

install -d "$R/runner/scripts"
cat > "$R/runner/scripts/runner-weekly-cleanup.sh" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Weekly cleanup of the GitLab Runner host: Docker, runner caches, packages, logs, temp files.
set -Eeuo pipefail

RETENTION_DAYS="${RETENTION_DAYS:-7}"
JOURNAL_MAX="${JOURNAL_MAX:-500M}"
SHARED_CACHE_DIR="${SHARED_CACHE_DIR:-/srv/ci-cache}"
SHARED_CACHE_MAX_AGE_DAYS="${SHARED_CACHE_MAX_AGE_DAYS:-14}"
DRY_RUN="${DRY_RUN:-0}"

CACHE_DIRS=(/home/gitlab-runner/cache /home/gitlab-runner/.cache /var/lib/gitlab-runner/cache /root/.cache)
BUILD_DIRS=(/home/gitlab-runner/builds /var/lib/gitlab-runner/builds)

log()  { logger -t runner-weekly-cleanup -- "$*"; echo "$(date -u +%FT%TZ) $*"; }
disk() { df --output=used,avail,pcent -h / | tail -1 | xargs; }

run() {
  log "-> $*"
  [[ "$DRY_RUN" == "1" ]] && return 0
  "$@" || log "WARN: failed: $*"
}

wipe_dir() {
  local dir="$1" age="${2:-}"
  [[ -d "$dir" ]] || return 0
  if [[ -n "$age" ]]; then
    log "-> cleaning $dir older than ${age}d"
    [[ "$DRY_RUN" == "1" ]] && return 0
    find "$dir" -mindepth 1 -mtime +"$age" -delete 2>/dev/null || log "WARN: partial cleanup of $dir"
  else
    log "-> wiping $dir"
    [[ "$DRY_RUN" == "1" ]] && return 0
    find "$dir" -mindepth 1 -delete 2>/dev/null || log "WARN: partial wipe of $dir"
  fi
}

usage() {
  cat <<USAGE
Usage: $0 [--dry-run] [--help]
  --dry-run                   same as DRY_RUN=1: show what would be cleaned without deleting anything
Env:
  RETENTION_DAYS              keep images, builds and temp files newer than N days (default: 7)
  JOURNAL_MAX                 max systemd journal size (default: 500M)
  SHARED_CACHE_DIR            shared CI cache directory (default: /srv/ci-cache)
  SHARED_CACHE_MAX_AGE_DAYS   prune shared cache files not accessed for N days (default: 14)
  DRY_RUN=1                   show what would be cleaned without deleting anything
USAGE
}

while (( $# )); do
  case "$1" in
    -h|--help) usage; exit 0 ;;
    -n|--dry-run) DRY_RUN=1 ;;
    *) usage >&2; echo "unknown option: $1" >&2; exit 2 ;;
  esac
  shift
done
[[ $EUID -eq 0 ]] || { echo "run as root" >&2; exit 1; }

exec 9>/run/runner-weekly-cleanup.lock
flock -n 9 || { log "another cleanup is running, exit"; exit 0; }

hours=$(( RETENTION_DAYS * 24 ))
log "start (dry_run=${DRY_RUN}), disk before: $(disk)"

if command -v docker >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  # Never guess the job count: if docker does not answer, a default of 0 would wipe the caches under running jobs.
  # stderr goes to a temp file so a docker WARNING is never counted as a running job.
  ps_err=$(mktemp)
  trap 'rm -f "$ps_err"' EXIT
  if ! job_ids=$(docker ps -q --filter "label=com.gitlab.gitlab-runner.managed=true" 2>"$ps_err"); then
    log "ERROR: docker is not responding, cleanup skipped: $(cat "$ps_err")"
    exit 1
  fi
  running=$(wc -w <<<"$job_ids")
  if (( running > 0 )); then
    log "WARN: ${running} job container(s) running, skipping cache wipe"
  else
    run /usr/share/gitlab-runner/clear-docker-cache prune-volumes
    if [[ "$DRY_RUN" != "1" ]]; then
      docker volume ls -q --filter name=runner- | xargs -r docker volume rm >/dev/null 2>&1 || log "WARN: some runner volumes in use"
    fi
    for dir in "${CACHE_DIRS[@]}"; do wipe_dir "$dir"; done
  fi
  run docker container prune -f --filter "until=${hours}h"
  run docker image prune -af --filter "until=${hours}h"
  run docker builder prune -af --filter "until=${hours}h"
  run docker network prune -f --filter "until=${hours}h"
fi

for dir in "${BUILD_DIRS[@]}"; do wipe_dir "$dir" "$RETENTION_DAYS"; done

if command -v apt-get >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  run apt-get -o DPkg::Lock::Timeout=600 autoremove --purge -y -q
  run apt-get -o DPkg::Lock::Timeout=600 autoclean -q
  run apt-get -o DPkg::Lock::Timeout=600 clean -q
elif command -v dnf >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  run dnf -y -q autoremove
  run dnf -q clean all
fi

run journalctl --vacuum-time="$(( RETENTION_DAYS * 2 ))d" --vacuum-size="$JOURNAL_MAX"

if [[ "$DRY_RUN" != "1" ]]; then
  log "-> removing rotated logs older than 30d"
  find /var/log -type f \( -name '*.gz' -o -name '*-[0-9]*' -o -name '*.[0-9]' -o -name '*.old' \) -mtime +30 -delete 2>/dev/null || true
  find /tmp /var/tmp -xdev -mindepth 1 -type f -atime +"$RETENTION_DAYS" -delete 2>/dev/null || true
fi
run systemd-tmpfiles --clean

if [[ -d "$SHARED_CACHE_DIR" && "$DRY_RUN" != "1" ]]; then
  log "-> pruning ${SHARED_CACHE_DIR} files not used for ${SHARED_CACHE_MAX_AGE_DAYS}d"
  find "$SHARED_CACHE_DIR" -xdev -type f -atime +"$SHARED_CACHE_MAX_AGE_DAYS" -delete 2>/dev/null || true
  find "$SHARED_CACHE_DIR" -xdev -mindepth 2 -type d -empty -delete 2>/dev/null || true
fi

log "done, disk after: $(disk)"
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 755 "$R/runner/scripts/runner-weekly-cleanup.sh"

install -d "$R/runner/systemd"
cat > "$R/runner/systemd/ci-image-prewarm.service" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
[Unit]
Description=Pre-pull CI images from GitLab Registry
After=docker.service network-online.target
Wants=network-online.target

[Service]
Type=oneshot
ExecStart=/usr/local/bin/ci-image-prewarm.sh
Nice=10
IOSchedulingClass=idle
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 644 "$R/runner/systemd/ci-image-prewarm.service"

install -d "$R/runner/systemd"
cat > "$R/runner/systemd/ci-image-prewarm.timer" <<'__SIC_EOF__'

Description=Pre-pull CI images daily and after boot

[Timer]
OnBootSec=5min
OnCalendar=*-*-* 04:15:00
Persistent=true

[Install]
WantedBy=timers.target
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 644 "$R/runner/systemd/ci-image-prewarm.timer"

install -d "$R/runner/systemd"
cat > "$R/runner/systemd/ci-job-isolation.service" <<'__SIC_EOF__'

Description=Block CI jobs from the cloud metadata service and host services
After=docker.service network-online.target
Requires=docker.service
PartOf=docker.service

[Service]
Type=oneshot
RemainAfterExit=yes
ExecStart=/usr/local/bin/ci-job-isolation.sh
ExecReload=/usr/local/bin/ci-job-isolation.sh

[Install]
WantedBy=multi-user.target
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 644 "$R/runner/systemd/ci-job-isolation.service"

install -d "$R/runner/systemd"
cat > "$R/runner/systemd/runner-disk-guard.service" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
[Unit]
Description=Emergency disk cleanup when usage is high
After=docker.service

[Service]
Type=oneshot
ExecStart=/usr/local/bin/runner-disk-guard.sh
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 644 "$R/runner/systemd/runner-disk-guard.service"

install -d "$R/runner/systemd"
cat > "$R/runner/systemd/runner-disk-guard.timer" <<'__SIC_EOF__'

Description=Check disk usage every 30 minutes

[Timer]
OnBootSec=10m
OnUnitActiveSec=30m

[Install]
WantedBy=timers.target
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 644 "$R/runner/systemd/runner-disk-guard.timer"

install -d "$R/runner/systemd"
cat > "$R/runner/systemd/runner-engine-update.service" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
[Unit]
Description=Weekly upgrade of Docker CE and GitLab Runner when no CI job runs
After=docker.service network-online.target

[Service]
Type=oneshot
ExecStart=/usr/local/bin/runner-engine-update.sh
Nice=10
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 644 "$R/runner/systemd/runner-engine-update.service"

install -d "$R/runner/systemd"
cat > "$R/runner/systemd/runner-engine-update.timer" <<'__SIC_EOF__'

Description=Upgrade Docker CE and GitLab Runner every Sunday

[Timer]
OnCalendar=Sun *-*-* 04:30:00
Persistent=true
RandomizedDelaySec=30m

[Install]
WantedBy=timers.target
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 644 "$R/runner/systemd/runner-engine-update.timer"

install -d "$R/runner/systemd"
cat > "$R/runner/systemd/runner-weekly-cleanup.service" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
[Unit]
Description=Weekly cleanup of GitLab Runner host
After=docker.service

[Service]
Type=oneshot
ExecStart=/usr/local/bin/runner-weekly-cleanup.sh
Nice=10
IOSchedulingClass=idle
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 644 "$R/runner/systemd/runner-weekly-cleanup.service"

install -d "$R/runner/systemd"
cat > "$R/runner/systemd/runner-weekly-cleanup.timer" <<'__SIC_EOF__'

Description=Run runner cleanup every Monday

[Timer]

Persistent=true
RandomizedDelaySec=10m

[Install]
WantedBy=timers.target
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 644 "$R/runner/systemd/runner-weekly-cleanup.timer"

install -d "$R/runner/stacks/logging"
cat > "$R/runner/stacks/logging/config.alloy" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
// Container logs and the systemd journal to the local Loki.
// CI job containers are skipped: their output is already the job log in GitLab.

logging {
  level = "warn"
}

loki.write "local" {
  endpoint {
    url = "http://127.0.0.1:3100/loki/api/v1/push"
  }
}

discovery.docker "containers" {
  host             = "unix:///var/run/docker.sock"
  refresh_interval = "15s"
}

discovery.relabel "containers" {
  targets = []

  rule {
    source_labels = ["__meta_docker_container_label_com_gitlab_gitlab_runner_managed"]
    regex         = "true"
    action        = "drop"
  }

  rule {
    source_labels = ["__meta_docker_container_name"]
    regex         = "/(.*)"
    target_label  = "container"
  }

  rule {
    source_labels = ["__meta_docker_container_label_com_docker_compose_project"]
    target_label  = "compose_project"
  }

  rule {
    source_labels = ["__meta_docker_container_label_com_docker_compose_service"]
    target_label  = "compose_service"
  }
}

loki.source.docker "containers" {
  host          = "unix:///var/run/docker.sock"
  targets       = discovery.docker.containers.targets
  labels        = {source = "docker"}
  relabel_rules = discovery.relabel.containers.rules
  forward_to    = [loki.write.local.receiver]
}

loki.relabel "journal" {
  forward_to = []

  rule {
    source_labels = ["__journal__systemd_unit"]
    target_label  = "unit"
  }

  rule {
    source_labels = ["__journal_priority_keyword"]
    target_label  = "level"
  }

  rule {
    source_labels = ["__journal_syslog_identifier"]
    target_label  = "identifier"
  }
}

loki.source.journal "system" {
  path          = "/var/log/journal"
  max_age       = "12h"
  labels        = {source = "journal"}
  relabel_rules = loki.relabel.journal.rules
  forward_to    = [loki.write.local.receiver]
}
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 644 "$R/runner/stacks/logging/config.alloy"

install -d "$R/runner/stacks/logging"
cat > "$R/runner/stacks/logging/docker-compose.yml" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
name: logging

services:
  loki:
    image: grafana/loki:3.7.7
    user: "${MON_UID:?run runner/install/users.sh first}:${MON_GID:?run runner/install/users.sh first}"
    restart: unless-stopped
    network_mode: host
    mem_limit: 512m
    command: ["-config.file=/etc/loki/config.yaml"]
    volumes:
      - ./loki.yaml:/etc/loki/config.yaml:ro
      - loki-data:/loki

  # Runs as the monitoring service user. Docker socket and journal access come from group_add
  # (docker and systemd-journal); note that reading the Docker socket is root-equivalent on the host.
  # The journal group is added by docker-compose.override.yml, which stack_ids (common/lib.sh) writes
  # only when its gid differs from the docker gid: compose rejects a group_add list with equal items.
  alloy:
    image: grafana/alloy:v1.19.2
    restart: unless-stopped
    network_mode: host
    mem_limit: 256m
    user: "${MON_UID:?run runner/install/users.sh first}:${MON_GID:?run runner/install/users.sh first}"
    group_add:
      - "${DOCKER_GID:?run runner/install/logging.sh}"
    read_only: true
    cap_drop: [ALL]
    security_opt:
      - label=disable
      - no-new-privileges:true
    tmpfs:
      - /tmp
    command:
      - run
      - --server.http.listen-addr=127.0.0.1:12345
      - --storage.path=/data
      - --disable-reporting
      - /config/config.alloy
    volumes:
      - ./config.alloy:/config/config.alloy:ro
      - alloy-data:/data
      - /var/run/docker.sock:/var/run/docker.sock:ro
      - /var/log/journal:/var/log/journal:ro
      - /etc/machine-id:/etc/machine-id:ro
    depends_on: [loki]

volumes:
  loki-data:
  alloy-data:
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 644 "$R/runner/stacks/logging/docker-compose.yml"

install -d "$R/runner/stacks/logging"
cat > "$R/runner/stacks/logging/grafana-datasource.yml" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
apiVersion: 1
datasources:
  - name: Loki
    uid: loki
    type: loki
    access: proxy
    url: http://127.0.0.1:3100
    editable: false
    jsonData:
      maxLines: 1000
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 644 "$R/runner/stacks/logging/grafana-datasource.yml"

install -d "$R/runner/stacks/logging"
cat > "$R/runner/stacks/logging/loki.yaml" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
auth_enabled: false

server:
  http_listen_address: 127.0.0.1
  http_listen_port: 3100
  grpc_listen_address: 127.0.0.1
  grpc_listen_port: 9096
  log_level: warn

common:
  instance_addr: 127.0.0.1
  path_prefix: /loki
  replication_factor: 1
  storage:
    filesystem:
      chunks_directory: /loki/chunks
      rules_directory: /loki/rules
  ring:
    kvstore:
      store: inmemory

schema_config:
  configs:
    - from: 2024-04-01
      store: tsdb
      object_store: filesystem
      schema: v13
      index:
        prefix: index_
        period: 24h

limits_config:
  retention_period: 168h
  ingestion_rate_mb: 4
  ingestion_burst_size_mb: 8
  max_query_series: 5000
  allow_structured_metadata: true
  volume_enabled: true

compactor:
  working_directory: /loki/compactor
  compaction_interval: 10m
  retention_enabled: true
  retention_delete_delay: 2h
  delete_request_store: filesystem

analytics:
  reporting_enabled: false
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 644 "$R/runner/stacks/logging/loki.yaml"

install -d "$R/runner/stacks/monitoring/caddy"
cat > "$R/runner/stacks/monitoring/caddy/Caddyfile.tmpl" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
{
	email __ACME_EMAIL__
	admin off
}

__DOMAIN__ {
	encode zstd gzip

	header {
		Strict-Transport-Security "max-age=31536000"
		X-Content-Type-Options "nosniff"
		X-Frame-Options "DENY"
		Referrer-Policy "strict-origin-when-cross-origin"
		-Server
	}

	reverse_proxy 127.0.0.1:3000

	log {
		output stdout
		format console
	}
}
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 644 "$R/runner/stacks/monitoring/caddy/Caddyfile.tmpl"

install -d "$R/runner/stacks/monitoring"
cat > "$R/runner/stacks/monitoring/docker-compose.yml" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
name: monitoring

services:
  prometheus:
    image: prom/prometheus:v3.4.0
    user: "${MON_UID:?run runner/install/users.sh first}:${MON_GID:?run runner/install/users.sh first}"
    restart: unless-stopped
    network_mode: host
    mem_limit: 512m
    command:
      - --config.file=/etc/prometheus/prometheus.yml
      - --storage.tsdb.path=/prometheus
      - --storage.tsdb.retention.time=15d
      - --storage.tsdb.retention.size=2GB
      - --web.listen-address=127.0.0.1:9090
      - --web.enable-lifecycle
    volumes:
      - ./prometheus/prometheus.yml:/etc/prometheus/prometheus.yml:ro
      - ./prometheus/alerts.yml:/etc/prometheus/alerts.yml:ro
      - prometheus-data:/prometheus

  node-exporter:
    image: prom/node-exporter:v1.9.1
    user: "${MON_UID:?run runner/install/users.sh first}:${MON_GID:?run runner/install/users.sh first}"
    restart: unless-stopped
    network_mode: host
    pid: host
    security_opt:
      - label=disable
    mem_limit: 64m
    command:
      - --path.rootfs=/host
      - --web.listen-address=127.0.0.1:9100
    volumes:
      - /:/host:ro,rslave

  grafana:
    image: grafana/grafana:12.0.0
    user: "${MON_UID:?run runner/install/users.sh first}:${MON_GID:?run runner/install/users.sh first}"
    restart: unless-stopped
    network_mode: host
    mem_limit: 256m
    env_file: .env
    depends_on: [prometheus]
    volumes:
      - ./grafana/provisioning:/etc/grafana/provisioning:ro
      - ./grafana/dashboards:/var/lib/grafana/dashboards:ro
      - grafana-data:/var/lib/grafana

volumes:
  prometheus-data:
  grafana-data:
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 644 "$R/runner/stacks/monitoring/docker-compose.yml"

install -d "$R/runner/stacks/monitoring"
cat > "$R/runner/stacks/monitoring/env.example" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
# Copied to /opt/monitoring/.env by runner/install/monitoring.sh; the password is generated there.
GF_SECURITY_ADMIN_USER=admin
GF_SECURITY_ADMIN_PASSWORD=generated-on-install
GF_SERVER_HTTP_ADDR=127.0.0.1
GF_SERVER_HTTP_PORT=3000
GF_USERS_ALLOW_SIGN_UP=false
GF_AUTH_ANONYMOUS_ENABLED=false
GF_ANALYTICS_REPORTING_ENABLED=false
GF_ANALYTICS_CHECK_FOR_UPDATES=false
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 644 "$R/runner/stacks/monitoring/env.example"

install -d "$R/runner/stacks/monitoring/grafana/dashboards"
cat > "$R/runner/stacks/monitoring/grafana/dashboards/gitlab-runner.json" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
{
  "uid": "gitlab-runner-host",
  "title": "GitLab Runner Host",
  "schemaVersion": 39,
  "version": 1,
  "refresh": "30s",
  "time": { "from": "now-6h", "to": "now" },
  "tags": ["gitlab", "runner"],
  "panels": [
    {
      "id": 1, "type": "stat", "title": "Runner up",
      "gridPos": { "h": 4, "w": 6, "x": 0, "y": 0 },
      "datasource": { "type": "prometheus", "uid": "prometheus" },
      "fieldConfig": { "defaults": { "mappings": [ { "type": "value", "options": { "0": { "text": "DOWN", "color": "red" }, "1": { "text": "UP", "color": "green" } } } ], "color": { "mode": "thresholds" }, "thresholds": { "mode": "absolute", "steps": [ { "color": "red", "value": null }, { "color": "green", "value": 1 } ] } } },
      "targets": [ { "refId": "A", "expr": "up{job=\"gitlab-runner\"}" } ]
    },
    {
      "id": 2, "type": "stat", "title": "Running jobs",
      "gridPos": { "h": 4, "w": 6, "x": 6, "y": 0 },
      "datasource": { "type": "prometheus", "uid": "prometheus" },
      "targets": [ { "refId": "A", "expr": "sum(gitlab_runner_jobs) or vector(0)" } ]
    },
    {
      "id": 3, "type": "stat", "title": "Concurrent limit",
      "gridPos": { "h": 4, "w": 6, "x": 12, "y": 0 },
      "datasource": { "type": "prometheus", "uid": "prometheus" },
      "targets": [ { "refId": "A", "expr": "gitlab_runner_concurrent" } ]
    },
    {
      "id": 4, "type": "gauge", "title": "Disk used",
      "gridPos": { "h": 4, "w": 6, "x": 18, "y": 0 },
      "datasource": { "type": "prometheus", "uid": "prometheus" },
      "fieldConfig": { "defaults": { "unit": "percent", "min": 0, "max": 100, "thresholds": { "mode": "absolute", "steps": [ { "color": "green", "value": null }, { "color": "orange", "value": 70 }, { "color": "red", "value": 85 } ] } } },
      "targets": [ { "refId": "A", "expr": "(1 - node_filesystem_avail_bytes{mountpoint=\"/\",fstype!=\"rootfs\"} / node_filesystem_size_bytes{mountpoint=\"/\",fstype!=\"rootfs\"}) * 100" } ]
    },
    {
      "id": 5, "type": "timeseries", "title": "Jobs by stage",
      "gridPos": { "h": 8, "w": 12, "x": 0, "y": 4 },
      "datasource": { "type": "prometheus", "uid": "prometheus" },
      "targets": [ { "refId": "A", "expr": "sum by (executor_stage) (gitlab_runner_jobs)", "legendFormat": "{{executor_stage}}" } ]
    },
    {
      "id": 6, "type": "timeseries", "title": "Job duration p95",
      "gridPos": { "h": 8, "w": 12, "x": 12, "y": 4 },
      "datasource": { "type": "prometheus", "uid": "prometheus" },
      "fieldConfig": { "defaults": { "unit": "s" } },
      "targets": [ { "refId": "A", "expr": "histogram_quantile(0.95, sum by (le) (rate(gitlab_runner_job_duration_seconds_bucket[1h])))", "legendFormat": "p95" } ]
    },
    {
      "id": 7, "type": "timeseries", "title": "CPU usage",
      "gridPos": { "h": 8, "w": 8, "x": 0, "y": 12 },
      "datasource": { "type": "prometheus", "uid": "prometheus" },
      "fieldConfig": { "defaults": { "unit": "percent", "min": 0, "max": 100 } },
      "targets": [ { "refId": "A", "expr": "100 - avg(rate(node_cpu_seconds_total{mode=\"idle\"}[5m])) * 100", "legendFormat": "cpu" } ]
    },
    {
      "id": 8, "type": "timeseries", "title": "Memory usage",
      "gridPos": { "h": 8, "w": 8, "x": 8, "y": 12 },
      "datasource": { "type": "prometheus", "uid": "prometheus" },
      "fieldConfig": { "defaults": { "unit": "percent", "min": 0, "max": 100 } },
      "targets": [ { "refId": "A", "expr": "(1 - node_memory_MemAvailable_bytes / node_memory_MemTotal_bytes) * 100", "legendFormat": "memory" } ]
    },
    {
      "id": 9, "type": "timeseries", "title": "Failed jobs (1h)",
      "gridPos": { "h": 8, "w": 8, "x": 16, "y": 12 },
      "datasource": { "type": "prometheus", "uid": "prometheus" },
      "targets": [ { "refId": "A", "expr": "sum by (failure_reason) (increase(gitlab_runner_failed_jobs_total[1h]))", "legendFormat": "{{failure_reason}}" } ]
    }
  ]
}
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 644 "$R/runner/stacks/monitoring/grafana/dashboards/gitlab-runner.json"

install -d "$R/runner/stacks/monitoring/grafana/provisioning/dashboards"
cat > "$R/runner/stacks/monitoring/grafana/provisioning/dashboards/default.yml" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
apiVersion: 1
providers:
  - name: default
    folder: GitLab Runner
    type: file
    disableDeletion: true
    allowUiUpdates: false
    options:
      path: /var/lib/grafana/dashboards
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 644 "$R/runner/stacks/monitoring/grafana/provisioning/dashboards/default.yml"

install -d "$R/runner/stacks/monitoring/grafana/provisioning/datasources"
cat > "$R/runner/stacks/monitoring/grafana/provisioning/datasources/prometheus.yml" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
apiVersion: 1
datasources:
  - name: Prometheus
    uid: prometheus
    type: prometheus
    access: proxy
    url: http://127.0.0.1:9090
    isDefault: true
    editable: false
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 644 "$R/runner/stacks/monitoring/grafana/provisioning/datasources/prometheus.yml"

install -d "$R/runner/stacks/monitoring/prometheus"
cat > "$R/runner/stacks/monitoring/prometheus/alerts.yml" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
groups:
  - name: runner-host
    rules:
      - alert: GitLabRunnerDown
        expr: up{job="gitlab-runner"} == 0
        for: 2m
        labels:
          severity: critical
        annotations:
          summary: GitLab Runner metrics endpoint is down
      - alert: DiskSpaceHigh
        expr: (1 - node_filesystem_avail_bytes{mountpoint="/",fstype!="rootfs"} / node_filesystem_size_bytes{mountpoint="/",fstype!="rootfs"}) * 100 > 80
        for: 10m
        labels:
          severity: warning
        annotations:
          summary: Root filesystem usage above 80%
      - alert: MemoryHigh
        expr: (1 - node_memory_MemAvailable_bytes / node_memory_MemTotal_bytes) * 100 > 90
        for: 10m
        labels:
          severity: warning
        annotations:
          summary: Memory usage above 90%
      - alert: RunnerJobFailures
        expr: sum(increase(gitlab_runner_failed_jobs_total[30m])) > 3
        for: 0m
        labels:
          severity: warning
        annotations:
          summary: More than 3 runner job failures in 30 minutes
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 644 "$R/runner/stacks/monitoring/prometheus/alerts.yml"

install -d "$R/runner/stacks/monitoring/prometheus"
cat > "$R/runner/stacks/monitoring/prometheus/prometheus.yml" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
global:
  scrape_interval: 15s
  evaluation_interval: 15s

rule_files:
  - /etc/prometheus/alerts.yml

scrape_configs:
  - job_name: prometheus
    static_configs:
      - targets: ["127.0.0.1:9090"]
  - job_name: node
    static_configs:
      - targets: ["127.0.0.1:9100"]
  - job_name: gitlab-runner
    static_configs:
      - targets: ["127.0.0.1:9252"]
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 644 "$R/runner/stacks/monitoring/prometheus/prometheus.yml"

install -d "$R/runner/stacks/npm-cache"
cat > "$R/runner/stacks/npm-cache/config.yaml" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
storage: /verdaccio/storage/data
plugins: /verdaccio/plugins

listen:
  - 172.17.0.1:4873

auth:
  htpasswd:
    file: /verdaccio/storage/htpasswd
    max_users: -1

uplinks:
  npmjs:
    url: https://registry.npmjs.org/
    timeout: 60s
    maxage: 30m
    cache: true

packages:
  '@*/*':
    access: $all
    publish: $authenticated
    proxy: npmjs
  '**':
    access: $all
    publish: $authenticated
    proxy: npmjs

server:
  keepAliveTimeout: 60

web:
  enable: false

log:
  type: stdout
  format: pretty
  level: warn
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 644 "$R/runner/stacks/npm-cache/config.yaml"

install -d "$R/runner/stacks/npm-cache"
cat > "$R/runner/stacks/npm-cache/docker-compose.yml" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
name: npm-cache

services:
  verdaccio:
    image: verdaccio/verdaccio:6.1.2
    restart: unless-stopped
    network_mode: host
    mem_limit: 256m
    # The image's default command passes --listen 0.0.0.0:4873, which overrides "listen:" in config.yaml
    # and exposes the cache (access: $all) on every interface. runner/install/npm-cache.sh and update.sh
    # replace 172.17.0.1 below with the docker0 IP, as they do in config.yaml.
    command: ["verdaccio", "--config", "/verdaccio/conf/config.yaml", "--listen", "http://172.17.0.1:4873"]
    volumes:
      - ./config.yaml:/verdaccio/conf/config.yaml:ro
      - verdaccio-storage:/verdaccio/storage

volumes:
  verdaccio-storage:
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 644 "$R/runner/stacks/npm-cache/docker-compose.yml"

install -d "$R/runner/stacks/registry-cache"
cat > "$R/runner/stacks/registry-cache/config.yml" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
version: 0.1
log:
  level: info
  fields:
    service: registry-cache
storage:
  filesystem:
    rootdirectory: /var/lib/registry
  delete:
    enabled: true
  cache:
    blobdescriptor: inmemory
http:
  addr: 127.0.0.1:5000
  headers:
    X-Content-Type-Options: [nosniff]
  debug:
    addr: 127.0.0.1:5001
    prometheus:
      enabled: true
      path: /metrics
proxy:
  remoteurl: https://registry-1.docker.io
health:
  storagedriver:
