install -d "$R/runner/stacks/monitoring/grafana/dashboards"
cat > "$R/runner/stacks/monitoring/grafana/dashboards/gitlab-runner.json" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
{
  "uid": "gitlab-runner-host",
  "title": "GitLab Runner Host",
  "schemaVersion": 39,
  "version": 1,
  "refresh": "30s",
  "time": { "from": "now-6h", "to": "now" },
  "tags": ["gitlab", "runner"],
  "panels": [
    {
      "id": 1, "type": "stat", "title": "Runner up",
      "gridPos": { "h": 4, "w": 6, "x": 0, "y": 0 },
      "datasource": { "type": "prometheus", "uid": "prometheus" },
      "fieldConfig": { "defaults": { "mappings": [ { "type": "value", "options": { "0": { "text": "DOWN", "color": "red" }, "1": { "text": "UP", "color": "green" } } } ], "color": { "mode": "thresholds" }, "thresholds": { "mode": "absolute", "steps": [ { "color": "red", "value": null }, { "color": "green", "value": 1 } ] } } },
      "targets": [ { "refId": "A", "expr": "up{job=\"gitlab-runner\"}" } ]
    },
    {
      "id": 2, "type": "stat", "title": "Running jobs",
      "gridPos": { "h": 4, "w": 6, "x": 6, "y": 0 },
      "datasource": { "type": "prometheus", "uid": "prometheus" },
      "targets": [ { "refId": "A", "expr": "sum(gitlab_runner_jobs) or vector(0)" } ]
    },
    {
      "id": 3, "type": "stat", "title": "Concurrent limit",
      "gridPos": { "h": 4, "w": 6, "x": 12, "y": 0 },
      "datasource": { "type": "prometheus", "uid": "prometheus" },
      "targets": [ { "refId": "A", "expr": "gitlab_runner_concurrent" } ]
    },
    {
      "id": 4, "type": "gauge", "title": "Disk used",
      "gridPos": { "h": 4, "w": 6, "x": 18, "y": 0 },
      "datasource": { "type": "prometheus", "uid": "prometheus" },
      "fieldConfig": { "defaults": { "unit": "percent", "min": 0, "max": 100, "thresholds": { "mode": "absolute", "steps": [ { "color": "green", "value": null }, { "color": "orange", "value": 70 }, { "color": "red", "value": 85 } ] } } },
      "targets": [ { "refId": "A", "expr": "(1 - node_filesystem_avail_bytes{mountpoint=\"/\",fstype!=\"rootfs\"} / node_filesystem_size_bytes{mountpoint=\"/\",fstype!=\"rootfs\"}) * 100" } ]
    },
    {
      "id": 5, "type": "timeseries", "title": "Jobs by stage",
      "gridPos": { "h": 8, "w": 12, "x": 0, "y": 4 },
      "datasource": { "type": "prometheus", "uid": "prometheus" },
      "targets": [ { "refId": "A", "expr": "sum by (executor_stage) (gitlab_runner_jobs)", "legendFormat": "{{executor_stage}}" } ]
    },
    {
      "id": 6, "type": "timeseries", "title": "Job duration p95",
      "gridPos": { "h": 8, "w": 12, "x": 12, "y": 4 },
      "datasource": { "type": "prometheus", "uid": "prometheus" },
      "fieldConfig": { "defaults": { "unit": "s" } },
      "targets": [ { "refId": "A", "expr": "histogram_quantile(0.95, sum by (le) (rate(gitlab_runner_job_duration_seconds_bucket[1h])))", "legendFormat": "p95" } ]
    },
    {
      "id": 7, "type": "timeseries", "title": "CPU usage",
      "gridPos": { "h": 8, "w": 8, "x": 0, "y": 12 },
      "datasource": { "type": "prometheus", "uid": "prometheus" },
      "fieldConfig": { "defaults": { "unit": "percent", "min": 0, "max": 100 } },
      "targets": [ { "refId": "A", "expr": "100 - avg(rate(node_cpu_seconds_total{mode=\"idle\"}[5m])) * 100", "legendFormat": "cpu" } ]
    },
    {
      "id": 8, "type": "timeseries", "title": "Memory usage",
      "gridPos": { "h": 8, "w": 8, "x": 8, "y": 12 },
      "datasource": { "type": "prometheus", "uid": "prometheus" },
      "fieldConfig": { "defaults": { "unit": "percent", "min": 0, "max": 100 } },
      "targets": [ { "refId": "A", "expr": "(1 - node_memory_MemAvailable_bytes / node_memory_MemTotal_bytes) * 100", "legendFormat": "memory" } ]
    },
    {
      "id": 9, "type": "timeseries", "title": "Failed jobs (1h)",
      "gridPos": { "h": 8, "w": 8, "x": 16, "y": 12 },
      "datasource": { "type": "prometheus", "uid": "prometheus" },
      "targets": [ { "refId": "A", "expr": "sum by (failure_reason) (increase(gitlab_runner_failed_jobs_total[1h]))", "legendFormat": "{{failure_reason}}" } ]
    }
  ]
}
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 644 "$R/runner/stacks/monitoring/grafana/dashboards/gitlab-runner.json"

install -d "$R/runner/stacks/monitoring/grafana/provisioning/dashboards"
cat > "$R/runner/stacks/monitoring/grafana/provisioning/dashboards/default.yml" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
apiVersion: 1
providers:
  - name: default
    folder: GitLab Runner
    type: file
    disableDeletion: true
    allowUiUpdates: false
    options:
      path: /var/lib/grafana/dashboards
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 644 "$R/runner/stacks/monitoring/grafana/provisioning/dashboards/default.yml"

install -d "$R/runner/stacks/monitoring/grafana/provisioning/datasources"
cat > "$R/runner/stacks/monitoring/grafana/provisioning/datasources/prometheus.yml" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
apiVersion: 1
datasources:
  - name: Prometheus
    uid: prometheus
    type: prometheus
    access: proxy
    url: http://127.0.0.1:9090
    isDefault: true
    editable: false
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 644 "$R/runner/stacks/monitoring/grafana/provisioning/datasources/prometheus.yml"

install -d "$R/runner/stacks/monitoring/prometheus"
cat > "$R/runner/stacks/monitoring/prometheus/alerts.yml" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
groups:
  - name: runner-host
    rules:
      - alert: GitLabRunnerDown
        expr: up{job="gitlab-runner"} == 0
        for: 2m
        labels:
          severity: critical
        annotations:
          summary: GitLab Runner metrics endpoint is down
      - alert: DiskSpaceHigh
        expr: (1 - node_filesystem_avail_bytes{mountpoint="/",fstype!="rootfs"} / node_filesystem_size_bytes{mountpoint="/",fstype!="rootfs"}) * 100 > 80
        for: 10m
        labels:
          severity: warning
        annotations:
          summary: Root filesystem usage above 80%
      - alert: MemoryHigh
        expr: (1 - node_memory_MemAvailable_bytes / node_memory_MemTotal_bytes) * 100 > 90
        for: 10m
        labels:
          severity: warning
        annotations:
          summary: Memory usage above 90%
      - alert: RunnerJobFailures
        expr: sum(increase(gitlab_runner_failed_jobs_total[30m])) > 3
        for: 0m
        labels:
          severity: warning
        annotations:
          summary: More than 3 runner job failures in 30 minutes
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 644 "$R/runner/stacks/monitoring/prometheus/alerts.yml"

install -d "$R/runner/stacks/monitoring/prometheus"
cat > "$R/runner/stacks/monitoring/prometheus/prometheus.yml" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
global:
  scrape_interval: 15s
  evaluation_interval: 15s

rule_files:
  - /etc/prometheus/alerts.yml

scrape_configs:
  - job_name: prometheus
    static_configs:
      - targets: ["127.0.0.1:9090"]
  - job_name: node
    static_configs:
      - targets: ["127.0.0.1:9100"]
  - job_name: gitlab-runner
    static_configs:
      - targets: ["127.0.0.1:9252"]
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 644 "$R/runner/stacks/monitoring/prometheus/prometheus.yml"

install -d "$R/runner/stacks/npm-cache"
cat > "$R/runner/stacks/npm-cache/config.yaml" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
storage: /verdaccio/storage/data
plugins: /verdaccio/plugins

listen:
  - 172.17.0.1:4873

auth:
  htpasswd:
    file: /verdaccio/storage/htpasswd
    max_users: -1

uplinks:
  npmjs:
    url: https://registry.npmjs.org/
    timeout: 60s
    maxage: 30m
    cache: true

packages:
  '@*/*':
    access: $all
    publish: $authenticated
    proxy: npmjs
  '**':
    access: $all
    publish: $authenticated
    proxy: npmjs

server:
  keepAliveTimeout: 60

web:
  enable: false

log:
  type: stdout
  format: pretty
  level: warn
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 644 "$R/runner/stacks/npm-cache/config.yaml"

install -d "$R/runner/stacks/npm-cache"
cat > "$R/runner/stacks/npm-cache/docker-compose.yml" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
name: npm-cache

services:
  verdaccio:
    image: verdaccio/verdaccio:6.1.2
    restart: unless-stopped
    network_mode: host
    mem_limit: 256m
    # The image's default command passes --listen 0.0.0.0:4873, which overrides "listen:" in config.yaml
    # and exposes the cache (access: $all) on every interface. runner/install/npm-cache.sh and update.sh
    # replace 172.17.0.1 below with the docker0 IP, as they do in config.yaml.
    command: ["verdaccio", "--config", "/verdaccio/conf/config.yaml", "--listen", "http://172.17.0.1:4873"]
    volumes:
      - ./config.yaml:/verdaccio/conf/config.yaml:ro
      - verdaccio-storage:/verdaccio/storage

volumes:
  verdaccio-storage:
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 644 "$R/runner/stacks/npm-cache/docker-compose.yml"

install -d "$R/runner/stacks/registry-cache"
cat > "$R/runner/stacks/registry-cache/config.yml" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
version: 0.1
log:
  level: info
  fields:
    service: registry-cache
storage:
  filesystem:
    rootdirectory: /var/lib/registry
  delete:
    enabled: true
  cache:
    blobdescriptor: inmemory
http:
  addr: 127.0.0.1:5000
  headers:
    X-Content-Type-Options: [nosniff]
  debug:
    addr: 127.0.0.1:5001
    prometheus:
      enabled: true
      path: /metrics
proxy:
  remoteurl: https://registry-1.docker.io
health:
  storagedriver:
    enabled: true
    interval: 10s
    threshold: 3
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 644 "$R/runner/stacks/registry-cache/config.yml"

install -d "$R/runner/stacks/registry-cache"
cat > "$R/runner/stacks/registry-cache/docker-compose.yml" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
name: registry-cache

services:
  registry:
    image: registry:2.8.3
    restart: unless-stopped
    network_mode: host
    mem_limit: 128m
    volumes:
      - ./config.yml:/etc/docker/registry/config.yml:ro
      - registry-data:/var/lib/registry

volumes:
  registry-data:
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 644 "$R/runner/stacks/registry-cache/docker-compose.yml"

install -d "$R/common"
cat > "$R/common/admin-tools.sh" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Admin tools for the runner host: htop, btop, ncdu, iotop, mtr, network and disk tools, editors, skopeo, mc, fd, rg, fzf.
# Each package is installed on its own, so one that is missing on this release doesn't stop the rest.
# Also installs the shell aliases, shell history and fzf key bindings (Ctrl+R, Ctrl+T) and, if there is none yet, the tmux config from the init. Safe to rerun.
set -Eeuo pipefail
# shellcheck source=lib.sh
source "$(dirname "$0")/lib.sh"

if [[ "${1:-}" == "-h" || "${1:-}" == "--help" ]]; then
  echo "Usage: $0  installs admin tools (htop, btop, ncdu, iotop, mtr, dig, tcpdump, skopeo, mc, fd, rg, fzf, ...), aliases, shell history and tmux config"
  exit 0
fi
require_root

if command -v dnf >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  dnf -y -q install epel-release >/dev/null 2>&1 || log "WARN: epel-release not available, EPEL tools will be missing"
  PKGS=(htop btop ncdu iotop mtr nano vim-enhanced rsync wget unzip tar bash-completion skopeo dnf-utils
        bind-utils lsof sysstat tcpdump traceroute nmap-ncat jq git tmux curl mc fd-find ripgrep fzf)
  pkg_one() { dnf -y -q install "$1" >/dev/null 2>&1; }
elif command -v apt-get >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  apt-get update -q >/dev/null
  PKGS=(htop btop ncdu iotop mtr-tiny nano vim rsync wget unzip tar bash-completion skopeo
        dnsutils lsof sysstat tcpdump traceroute netcat-openbsd jq git tmux curl mc fd-find ripgrep fzf)
  pkg_one() { DEBIAN_FRONTEND=noninteractive apt-get install -y -q "$1" >/dev/null 2>&1; }
else
  die "no supported package manager"
fi

installed=()
missing=()
for p in "${PKGS[@]}"; do
  if pkg_one "$p"; then
    installed+=("$p")
  else
    missing+=("$p")
  fi
done

if [[ -f /etc/default/sysstat ]]; then
  sed -i 's/^ENABLED=.*/ENABLED="true"/' /etc/default/sysstat
fi
if systemctl cat sysstat >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  systemctl enable --now sysstat >/dev/null 2>&1 || log "WARN: could not enable sysstat collection"
fi

cat > /etc/profile.d/ci-runner.sh <<'EOF'
alias health='sudo /usr/local/bin/health'
alias runner-logs='sudo journalctl -u gitlab-runner -f'
alias runner-errors='sudo journalctl -u gitlab-runner --since "-30min" --no-pager | grep -Ei "error|warn|bottleneck"'
alias runner-jobs='sudo docker ps --filter label=com.gitlab.gitlab-runner.managed=true --format "{{.Names}}  {{.Status}}"'
alias runner-metrics='curl -s 127.0.0.1:9252/metrics | grep "^gitlab_runner_"'
alias dfd='df -h / ; sudo docker system df'
alias dstats='sudo docker stats --no-stream'
EOF
chmod 644 /etc/profile.d/ci-runner.sh
restorecon_if /etc/profile.d/ci-runner.sh

# Shell history, Ctrl+R / Ctrl+T with fzf: one source file, also embedded in server/server-init-*.sh
install -m 0644 "$REPO_ROOT/common/shell-history.sh" /etc/profile.d/90-shell-history.sh
restorecon_if /etc/profile.d/90-shell-history.sh
# Debian and Ubuntu ship HISTSIZE=1000/HISTFILESIZE=2000 in the skeleton ~/.bashrc; bash applies them before the
# first prompt and truncates the history file, so the larger limits in profile.d never take effect. Comment out
# exactly those default lines (other values are a deliberate choice and are kept).
for rc in /etc/skel/.bashrc /root/.bashrc /home/*/.bashrc; do
  [[ -f "$rc" ]] && sed -i -E 's/^(HISTSIZE=1000|HISTFILESIZE=2000)$/# &  (raised in \/etc\/profile.d\/90-shell-history.sh)/' "$rc"
done
# Debian and Ubuntu name the fd binary fdfind
if command -v fdfind >/dev/null 2>&1 && ! command -v fd >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  ln -sfn "$(command -v fdfind)" /usr/local/bin/fd
fi

# root and every admin in the ssh-users group (runner/install/users.sh) get the tmux config if they have none
homes=(/root)
while IFS= read -r u; do
  [[ -n "$u" ]] && homes+=("$(getent passwd "$u" | cut -d: -f6)")
done < <(getent group ssh-users | cut -d: -f4 | tr ',' '\n')
for home in "${homes[@]}"; do
  [[ -d "$home" ]] || continue
  if [[ -f "${home}/.tmux.conf" ]]; then
    log "kept existing ${home}/.tmux.conf"
    continue
  fi
  cat > "${home}/.tmux.conf" <<'EOF'
set -g mouse on
set -g history-limit 50000
set -g base-index 1
set -g status-right '#H  %H:%M'
EOF
  chown "$(stat -c '%U:%G' "$home")" "${home}/.tmux.conf"
  chmod 644 "${home}/.tmux.conf"
done

log "installed or already present (${#installed[@]}): ${installed[*]}"
if (( ${#missing[@]} )); then
  log "WARN: not available on this release (${#missing[@]}): ${missing[*]}"
fi
for c in htop btop ncdu iotop mtr dig tcpdump skopeo sar mc fd rg fzf; do
  if command -v "$c" >/dev/null 2>&1; then printf '  %-8s ok\n' "$c"; else printf '  %-8s missing\n' "$c"; fi
done
log "OK: aliases work in new shells (or run: source /etc/profile.d/ci-runner.sh); Docker and journal aliases use sudo"
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 755 "$R/common/admin-tools.sh"

install -d "$R/common"
cat > "$R/common/health" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# health: one-command health and status report for this host (GitLab Runner host or general server)
set -Eeuo pipefail

DISK_WARN=70
DISK_FAIL=85
INODE_WARN=80
MEM_WARN=15
MEM_FAIL=5
LOAD_WARN=1.5
LOAD_FAIL=3
CLAM_SIG_WARN_DAYS=7
QUICK=0
BRIEF=0
WRITE=0
ONLY_CONTAINERS=0
COLOR=1
PROFILE=auto
RUNNER_UNREGISTERED=0
CFG=/etc/gitlab-runner/config.toml
PROM_URL="${PROM_URL:-http://127.0.0.1:9090}"
FORECAST_MIN_HISTORY_H="${FORECAST_MIN_HISTORY_H:-6}"

usage() {
  cat <<'USAGE'
Usage: health [options]

One-command health and status report for this host. Works on a GitLab Runner
host and on a general server.

Run it as a normal user: it re-runs itself through sudo when that needs no password.
Without root and without passwordless sudo it still runs, but skips the root-only
checks (firewall rules, fail2ban, sshd settings, journal, docker, runner).

Options:
  -q, --quick       skip slow checks (runner verify, network, pending updates, needs-restarting)
  -c, --containers  only show health of running containers
  --profile P       server|runner (default: auto = server; runner only when a runner is registered)
  --brief           compact summary (7 lines) of the state the server timers cached, plus live disk, memory,
                    firewall and fail2ban; this is what the admin sees on login
  --write           with --brief, as root: save it to /var/lib/server-health/brief.txt (done by server-health-brief.timer;
                    also runs a full report at most every 6 hours and notifies when it has failures)
  --no-color        plain output without colors
  -h, --help        show this help

Exit codes: 0 all OK, 1 warnings only, 2 at least one failure
USAGE
}

ORIG_ARGS=("$@")
while [[ $# -gt 0 ]]; do
  case "$1" in
    -q|--quick) QUICK=1 ;;
    -c|--containers) ONLY_CONTAINERS=1 ;;
    --profile) [[ $# -ge 2 ]] || { echo "--profile needs a value: runner or server" >&2; exit 2; }; shift; PROFILE="$1" ;;
    --profile=*) PROFILE="${1#--profile=}" ;;
    --no-color) COLOR=0 ;;
    --brief) BRIEF=1 ;;
    --write) WRITE=1 ;;
    -h|--help) usage; exit 0 ;;
    *) echo "unknown option: $1" >&2; usage >&2; exit 2 ;;
  esac
  shift
done

if (( WRITE == 1 && BRIEF == 0 )); then echo "--write needs --brief" >&2; exit 2; fi
case "$PROFILE" in
  auto|runner|server) ;;
  *) echo "invalid --profile '${PROFILE}': use runner or server" >&2; exit 2 ;;
esac

LIMITED=0
# Not root: re-run through sudo when that works without a password (HEALTH_REEXEC stops loops);
# otherwise run in limited mode and skip the checks that need root.
if [[ $EUID -ne 0 ]]; then
  if [[ -z "${HEALTH_REEXEC:-}" ]] && command -v sudo >/dev/null 2>&1 && sudo -n true >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    exec sudo -n -- env HEALTH_REEXEC=1 "$(readlink -f "$0")" "${ORIG_ARGS[@]}"
  fi
  LIMITED=1
fi

if [[ -t 1 && "$COLOR" == "1" && -z "${NO_COLOR:-}" ]]; then
  C_OK=$'\e[32m'; C_WARN=$'\e[33m'; C_FAIL=$'\e[31m'; C_B=$'\e[1m'; C_D=$'\e[2m'; C_N=$'\e[0m'
else
  C_OK=""; C_WARN=""; C_FAIL=""; C_B=""; C_D=""; C_N=""
fi

N_OK=0; N_WARN=0; N_FAIL=0
FW_STATE=unknown
ok()      { N_OK=$((N_OK + 1));     printf '  %s%-5s%s %s\n' "$C_OK" "OK" "$C_N" "$*"; }
warn()    { N_WARN=$((N_WARN + 1)); printf '  %s%-5s%s %s\n' "$C_WARN" "WARN" "$C_N" "$*"; }
fail()    { N_FAIL=$((N_FAIL + 1)); printf '  %s%-5s%s %s\n' "$C_FAIL" "FAIL" "$C_N" "$*"; }
skip()    { printf '  %s%-5s %s%s\n' "$C_D" "--" "$*" "$C_N"; }
info()    { printf '  %s%-5s %s%s\n' "$C_D" "" "$*" "$C_N"; }
section() { printf '\n%s%s%s\n' "$C_B" "$*" "$C_N"; }
have()    { command -v "$1" >/dev/null 2>&1; }
gt()      { awk -v a="$1" -v b="$2" 'BEGIN { exit !(a > b) }'; }
isnum()   { [[ "${1:-}" =~ ^-?[0-9]+([.][0-9]+)?([eE][-+]?[0-9]+)?$ ]]; }
num()     { awk -v x="$1" -v d="${2:-1}" 'BEGIN { printf "%.*f", d, x }'; }
unit_exists() { systemctl list-unit-files "$1" --no-legend 2>/dev/null | grep -q .; }
is_runner()   { [[ "$PROFILE" == "runner" ]]; }
detect_profile() {
  [[ "$PROFILE" == "auto" ]] || return 0
  # Default is a plain server. Only a host with a registered runner gets the runner checks;
  # gitlab-runner installed but never registered is still a plain server.
  if [[ -r "$CFG" ]] && grep -q '^[[:space:]]*\[\[runners\]\]' "$CFG" 2>/dev/null; then
    PROFILE=runner
  else
    PROFILE=server
    # Without root the 0600 config.toml can't be read, so "not registered" would be a guess.
    if [[ -e "$CFG" && ! -r "$CFG" ]]; then RUNNER_UNREGISTERED=2
    elif have gitlab-runner || unit_exists gitlab-runner.service; then RUNNER_UNREGISTERED=1; fi
  fi
}
http_code()   { curl -s -o /dev/null -m "${2:-5}" -w '%{http_code}' "$1" 2>/dev/null || true; }

human_age() {
  local s="$1"
  if (( s < 3600 )); then echo "$(( s / 60 ))m"
  elif (( s < 86400 )); then echo "$(( s / 3600 ))h$(( s % 3600 / 60 ))m"
  else echo "$(( s / 86400 ))d$(( s % 86400 / 3600 ))h"; fi
}

prom_q() {
  curl -fsS -m 10 -G "${PROM_URL}/api/v1/query" --data-urlencode "query=$1" 2>/dev/null \
    | jq -r '.data.result[0].value[1] // empty' 2>/dev/null || true
}

prom_qv() {
  curl -fsS -m 10 -G "${PROM_URL}/api/v1/query" --data-urlencode "query=$1" 2>/dev/null \
    | jq -r '.data.result[] | ((.metric | to_entries | map(.value) | join(",")) as $k | "\($k)=\(.value[1])")' 2>/dev/null || true
}

print_summary_and_exit() {
  section "Summary"
  printf '  %s%d OK%s  %s%d WARN%s  %s%d FAIL%s\n' \
    "$C_OK" "$N_OK" "$C_N" "$C_WARN" "$N_WARN" "$C_N" "$C_FAIL" "$N_FAIL" "$C_N"
  if (( N_FAIL > 0 )); then exit 2; fi
  if (( N_WARN > 0 )); then exit 1; fi
  exit 0
}

check_system() {
  section "System"
  local pretty="unknown"
  if [[ -r /etc/os-release ]]; then
    pretty=$(awk -F= '/^PRETTY_NAME=/ { gsub(/"/, "", $2); print $2 }' /etc/os-release)
  fi
  info "$(uname -n) | ${pretty} | $(uname -m) | kernel $(uname -r)"
  info "uptime $(uptime -p 2>/dev/null | sed 's/^up //') | $(nproc) vCPU"

  local cores load5 per_core
  cores=$(nproc)
  read -r _ load5 _ _ _ < /proc/loadavg
  per_core=$(awk -v l="$load5" -v c="$cores" 'BEGIN { printf "%.2f", l / c }')
  if gt "$per_core" "$LOAD_FAIL"; then
    fail "load ${load5} (5 min), ${per_core} per core"
  elif gt "$per_core" "$LOAD_WARN"; then
    warn "load ${load5} (5 min), ${per_core} per core"
  else
    ok "load ${load5} (5 min), ${per_core} per core"
  fi

  local mem_total mem_avail mem_pct msg
  mem_total=$(awk '/^MemTotal:/ { print $2 }' /proc/meminfo)
  mem_avail=$(awk '/^MemAvailable:/ { print $2 }' /proc/meminfo)
  mem_pct=$(( mem_avail * 100 / mem_total ))
  msg="memory $(( (mem_total - mem_avail) / 1024 ))/$(( mem_total / 1024 )) MB used, ${mem_pct}% available"
  if (( mem_pct < MEM_FAIL )); then fail "$msg"
  elif (( mem_pct < MEM_WARN )); then warn "$msg"
  else ok "$msg"; fi

  local swap_total swap_free
  swap_total=$(awk '/^SwapTotal:/ { print $2 }' /proc/meminfo)
  swap_free=$(awk '/^SwapFree:/ { print $2 }' /proc/meminfo)
  if (( swap_total > 0 )); then
    local swap_pct=$(( (swap_total - swap_free) * 100 / swap_total ))
    msg="swap $(( (swap_total - swap_free) / 1024 ))/$(( swap_total / 1024 )) MB used (${swap_pct}%)"
    if is_runner; then info "$msg"
    elif (( swap_pct >= 80 )); then warn "$msg, memory pressure"
    else ok "$msg"; fi
  else
    info "swap: none"
  fi

  local kills ooms victims
  # Count kills, not every related kernel line. One kill emits several matches.
  if (( LIMITED )); then
    kills=""
    skip "OOM kills: needs root (kernel log)"
  else
    kills=$(journalctl -k -b --no-pager -o cat 2>/dev/null | grep 'Out of memory: Killed process' || true)
  fi
  if [[ -n "$kills" ]]; then
    ooms=$(grep -c . <<<"$kills")
    victims=$(sed -nE 's/.*Killed process [0-9]+ \(([^)]+)\).*/\1/p' <<<"$kills" | sort | uniq -c | awk '{printf "%s x%s, ", $2, $1}' | sed 's/, $//')
    warn "${ooms} OOM kill(s) since boot${victims:+ (${victims})} (journalctl -k -b | grep 'Killed process')"
  elif (( ! LIMITED )); then
    ok "no OOM kills since boot"
  fi

  local disk_pct disk_info inode_pct
  disk_pct=$(df --output=pcent / | tail -1 | tr -dc '0-9')
  disk_info=$(df -h --output=used,size / | tail -1 | awk '{ print $1 "/" $2 }')
  msg="disk / ${disk_pct}% used (${disk_info})"
  if (( disk_pct >= DISK_FAIL )); then fail "$msg"
  elif (( disk_pct >= DISK_WARN )); then warn "$msg"
  else ok "$msg"; fi

  inode_pct=$(df --output=ipcent / 2>/dev/null | tail -1 | tr -dc '0-9' || true)
  if (( ${inode_pct:-0} >= INODE_WARN )); then
    warn "inodes / ${inode_pct}% used"
  fi

  local failed
  failed=$(systemctl --failed --no-legend --plain 2>/dev/null | awk '{ print $1 }' | tr '\n' ' ' || true)
  if [[ -n "${failed// /}" ]]; then
    fail "failed units: ${failed}"
  else
    ok "no failed systemd units"
  fi

  if have timedatectl; then
    local sync
    sync=$(timedatectl show -p NTPSynchronized --value 2>/dev/null || true)
    if [[ "$sync" == "yes" ]]; then ok "clock synchronized (NTP)"
    else warn "clock not synchronized (timedatectl)"; fi
  fi

  if have cloud-init; then
    local ci
    ci=$(cloud-init status 2>/dev/null | awk -F': ' '/^status:/ { print $2 }' || true)
    case "$ci" in
      done) ok "cloud-init done" ;;
      running) warn "cloud-init still running" ;;
      "") skip "cloud-init status unknown" ;;
      *) warn "cloud-init status: ${ci}" ;;
    esac
  fi
  return 0
}

check_security() {
  section "Security"
  if have getenforce; then
    local mode denials raw rc=0
    mode=$(getenforce 2>/dev/null || true)
    case "$mode" in
      Enforcing) ok "SELinux enforcing" ;;
      Permissive) warn "SELinux permissive (logs only, does not block)" ;;
      *) warn "SELinux ${mode:-unknown}" ;;
    esac
    # ausearch treats a pipe on stdin as the audit log and blocks until it closes.
    # health is often started over ssh, so force the on-disk logs and bound the query.
    if (( LIMITED )); then
      skip "SELinux denials: needs root"
    elif ! have ausearch; then
      skip "ausearch not installed"
    else
      raw=$(timeout 10 ausearch --input-logs -m avc -ts today </dev/null 2>/dev/null) || rc=$?
      if (( rc == 124 )); then
        skip "SELinux denial query timed out"
      elif (( rc != 0 && rc != 1 )); then
        skip "could not query SELinux denials (ausearch exit ${rc})"
      else
        denials=$(grep -c 'avc:' <<<"$raw" || true)
        if (( ${denials:-0} > 0 )); then
          warn "${denials} SELinux denials today (ausearch --input-logs -m avc -ts today)"
        else
          ok "no SELinux denials today"
        fi
      fi
    fi
  fi

  if (( LIMITED )); then
    skip "sshd settings, listening ports, pending updates: need root (run as root for the full report)"
  elif have sshd; then
    local sshd_t root_login
    sshd_t=$(sshd -T 2>/dev/null || true)
    if grep -qx 'passwordauthentication no' <<<"$sshd_t"; then
      ok "SSH password login disabled"
    elif is_runner; then
      fail "SSH password login enabled"
    else
      warn "SSH password login enabled (set PasswordAuthentication no)"
    fi
    root_login=$(awk '/^permitrootlogin / { print $2 }' <<<"$sshd_t")
    if is_runner; then
      case "$root_login" in
        no)  ok "SSH root login disabled" ;;
        yes) fail "SSH root login with password allowed" ;;
        *)   warn "SSH root login by key still allowed (${root_login:-unknown}); disable: sudo bash -c \"cd /root/server-init-config && runner/setup.sh --lock-root\"" ;;
      esac
      if grep -q '^allowgroups ' <<<"$sshd_t"; then
        ok "SSH limited to groups: $(awk '/^allowgroups / { printf "%s ", $2 }' <<<"$sshd_t")"
      else
        warn "SSH not limited by AllowGroups (runner/install/users.sh sets it)"
      fi
    else
      case "$root_login" in
        yes) warn "SSH root login with password allowed (PermitRootLogin yes)" ;;
        *)   info "SSH root login: ${root_login:-unknown}" ;;
      esac
    fi
  fi

  local members u shell pid ru
  members=$(getent group docker 2>/dev/null | cut -d: -f4 | tr ',' '\n' | grep -vx 'gitlab-runner' | grep . | tr '\n' ' ' || true)
  if [[ -n "${members// /}" ]]; then
    warn "users in the docker group (root-equivalent): ${members}; use sudo docker instead"
  else
    if is_runner; then ok "docker group: only gitlab-runner"; else ok "docker group: no members"; fi
  fi
  for u in gitlab-runner monitoring; do
    id "$u" >/dev/null 2>&1 || continue
    shell=$(getent passwd "$u" | cut -d: -f7)
    case "$shell" in
      */nologin|*/false) ok "service user ${u}: no login shell" ;;
      *) warn "service user ${u} has login shell ${shell}" ;;
    esac
  done
  if have systemctl && systemctl is-active --quiet gitlab-runner 2>/dev/null; then
    pid=$(systemctl show -p MainPID --value gitlab-runner 2>/dev/null || true)
    ru=$(ps -o user= -p "${pid:-0}" 2>/dev/null | tr -d ' ' || true)
    if [[ "$ru" == root ]]; then
      warn "gitlab-runner service runs as root (runner/install/users.sh switches it to gitlab-runner)"
    elif [[ -n "$ru" ]]; then
      ok "gitlab-runner service runs as ${ru}"
    fi
  fi

  local exposed ssh_ports allowed
  if (( LIMITED == 0 )); then
    ssh_ports=$(sshd -T 2>/dev/null | awk '/^port / { print $2 }' | sort -un | tr '\n' ' ' || true)
    ssh_ports="${ssh_ports:-22 }"
    allowed="$(tr ' ' '|' <<<"${ssh_ports% }")|80|443"
    if ! is_runner; then
      info "SSH port: ${ssh_ports% }"
    elif [[ " ${ssh_ports}" == *" 22 "* ]]; then
      info "SSH listens on: ${ssh_ports}(port 22 closes with the lock-root step)"
    else
      ok "SSH port: ${ssh_ports% }"
    fi
    exposed=$(ss -ltnH 2>/dev/null | awk '{ print $4 }' \
      | grep -vE '^(127\.|\[::1\]|172\.17\.)' \
      | sed -E 's/.*:([0-9]+)$/\1/' | sort -un | grep -vxE "$allowed" | tr '\n' ' ' || true)
    if [[ -n "${exposed// /}" ]]; then
      if is_runner; then
        warn "ports listening on public interfaces: ${exposed}(make sure the Hetzner firewall blocks them)"
      elif [[ "$FW_STATE" == "active" ]]; then
        info "ports listening on public interfaces: ${exposed}(the host firewall decides what is reachable)"
      else
        warn "ports listening on public interfaces: ${exposed}(no active host firewall)"
      fi
    else
      ok "only SSH/HTTP/HTTPS listen on public interfaces"
    fi

  fi

  if (( QUICK == 0 && LIMITED == 0 )); then
    local out n
    if have dnf; then
      if out=$(timeout 90 dnf -q updateinfo list --security 2>/dev/null); then
        n=$(grep -c . <<<"$out" || true)
        if (( ${n:-0} > 0 )); then
          warn "${n} pending security updates (dnf updateinfo list --security)"
        else
          ok "no pending security updates"
        fi
      else
        skip "could not query security updates"
      fi
    elif have apt-get; then
      n=$(apt-get -s upgrade 2>/dev/null | grep -c '^Inst.*security' || true)
      if (( ${n:-0} > 0 )); then
        warn "${n} pending security updates"
      else
        ok "no pending security updates"
      fi
    fi
  fi
  return 0
}

# Sets FW_KIND (firewalld|ufw|none) and FW_STATE (active|inactive|none) without printing.
detect_firewall() {
  FW_KIND=none; FW_STATE=none
  (( LIMITED == 0 )) || return 0
  if have firewall-cmd || unit_exists firewalld.service; then
    FW_KIND=firewalld; FW_STATE=inactive
    if have firewall-cmd && [[ "$(firewall-cmd --state 2>/dev/null || true)" == "running" ]]; then FW_STATE=active; fi
  elif have ufw; then
    FW_KIND=ufw; FW_STATE=inactive
    if ufw status 2>/dev/null | grep -qi '^Status: active'; then FW_STATE=active; fi
  fi
  return 0
}

check_firewall() {
  section "Firewall and brute-force protection"
  if (( LIMITED )); then
    skip "firewall and fail2ban: need root (run as root for the full report)"
    return 0
  fi
  local ports p ssh_ok=0 zone svc open ufw_out
  ports=$(sshd -T 2>/dev/null | awk '/^port / { print $2 }' | sort -un | tr '\n' ' ' || true)
  ports="${ports:-22}"

  case "$FW_KIND:$FW_STATE" in
    firewalld:active)
      zone=$(firewall-cmd --get-default-zone 2>/dev/null || true)
      svc=$(firewall-cmd --zone="$zone" --list-services 2>/dev/null || true)
      open=$(firewall-cmd --zone="$zone" --list-ports 2>/dev/null || true)
      for p in $ports; do
        if [[ " ${open} " == *" ${p}/tcp "* ]]; then ssh_ok=1; fi
        if [[ "$p" == "22" && " ${svc} " == *" ssh "* ]]; then ssh_ok=1; fi
      done
      if (( ssh_ok )); then
        ok "firewalld active, default zone ${zone:-?}, SSH allowed"
      else
        fail "firewalld active but SSH (${ports% }) is not allowed in zone ${zone:-?}; the next reload can lock you out"
      fi
      info "open in ${zone:-?}: services ${svc:-none} | ports ${open:-none}"
      ;;
    firewalld:*)
      if is_runner; then info "firewalld installed but not running (cloud firewall assumed)"
      else warn "firewalld installed but not running (systemctl enable --now firewalld)"; fi
      ;;
    ufw:active)
      ufw_out=$(ufw status 2>/dev/null || true)
      for p in $ports; do
        if grep -Eq "^(${p}(/tcp)?|OpenSSH|SSH)( \\(v6\\))?( on [^[:space:]]+)?[[:space:]]+(ALLOW|LIMIT)" <<<"$ufw_out"; then ssh_ok=1; fi
      done
      if (( ssh_ok )); then
        ok "ufw active, SSH allowed"
      else
        fail "ufw active but SSH (${ports% }) is not allowed"
      fi
      open=$(awk '/ALLOW/ && !/\(v6\)/ { print $1 }' <<<"$ufw_out" | sort -u | tr '\n' ' ')
      info "allowed in ufw: ${open:-none}"
      ;;
    ufw:*)
      if is_runner; then info "ufw installed but inactive (cloud firewall assumed)"
      else warn "ufw installed but inactive (ufw enable)"; fi
      ;;
    *)
      if is_runner; then info "no host firewall (cloud firewall assumed)"
      else warn "no host firewall found (firewalld or ufw)"; fi
      ;;
  esac

  if ! have fail2ban-client && ! unit_exists fail2ban.service; then
    if is_runner; then skip "fail2ban not installed"; else warn "fail2ban not installed (SSH brute-force protection)"; fi
    return 0
  fi
  if ! systemctl is-active --quiet fail2ban 2>/dev/null && ! { have fail2ban-client && fail2ban-client ping >/dev/null 2>&1; }; then
    warn "fail2ban not active"
    return 0
  fi
  local st banned total
  if st=$(fail2ban-client status sshd 2>/dev/null); then
    banned=$(sed -nE 's/.*Currently banned:[[:space:]]*([0-9]+).*/\1/p' <<<"$st" | head -1)
    total=$(sed -nE 's/.*Total banned:[[:space:]]*([0-9]+).*/\1/p' <<<"$st" | head -1)
    ok "fail2ban active, sshd jail: ${banned:-0} banned now (${total:-0} total)"
  else
    warn "fail2ban active but no sshd jail (fail2ban-client status)"
  fi
  return 0
}

check_updates() {
  section "Updates and reboot"
  local t found="" n pending="" applies=0 conf
  for t in server-update.timer dnf5-automatic.timer dnf-automatic-install.timer dnf-automatic.timer apt-daily-upgrade.timer; do
    unit_exists "$t" || continue
    systemctl is-enabled --quiet "$t" 2>/dev/null || systemctl is-active --quiet "$t" 2>/dev/null || continue
    found="$t"; break
  done
  if [[ -z "$found" ]]; then
    # openSUSE and others: any enabled timer that looks like an update job
    found=$(systemctl list-unit-files '*.timer' --state=enabled --no-legend --plain 2>/dev/null \
      | awk '{ print $1 }' | grep -E 'zypper|yum-cron|unattended|transactional-update|packagekit-offline' | head -1 || true)
  fi

  if [[ -z "$found" ]]; then
    warn "no automatic update timer found (dnf-automatic, unattended-upgrades or zypper)"
  elif [[ "$found" == server-update.timer ]]; then
    local dup=""
    for t in dnf5-automatic.timer dnf-automatic-install.timer dnf-automatic.timer; do
      if unit_exists "$t" && systemctl is-enabled --quiet "$t" 2>/dev/null; then dup="$t"; fi
    done
    if [[ -n "$dup" ]]; then warn "server-update.timer and ${dup} are both enabled: two updaters race on the dnf lock (rerun server/install-tools.sh)"
    else ok "automatic updates: server-update.timer ($(systemctl show "$found" -p NextElapseUSecRealtime --value 2>/dev/null || true))"; fi
  elif [[ "$found" == apt-daily-upgrade.timer ]]; then
    if grep -qsE '^[^/]*APT::Periodic::Unattended-Upgrade[[:space:]]+"1"' /etc/apt/apt.conf.d/20auto-upgrades; then
      ok "unattended-upgrades enabled (${found})"
    else
      warn "${found} enabled but APT::Periodic::Unattended-Upgrade is not 1 (/etc/apt/apt.conf.d/20auto-upgrades)"
    fi
  elif [[ "$found" == dnf*automatic* ]]; then
    [[ "$found" == *install* ]] && applies=1
    for conf in /etc/dnf/automatic.conf /etc/dnf/dnf5-plugins/automatic.conf; do
      [[ -f "$conf" ]] || continue
      if grep -qE '^[[:space:]]*apply_updates[[:space:]]*=[[:space:]]*(yes|true|1)' "$conf"; then applies=1; fi
    done
    if (( applies )); then
      ok "automatic updates: ${found} (installs updates)"
    else
      warn "${found} enabled but apply_updates is not yes (updates are only downloaded or reported)"
    fi
  else
    ok "automatic updates: ${found}"
  fi

  local reboot=0 latest running
  running=$(uname -r)
  if [[ -f /var/run/reboot-required ]]; then
    reboot=1
    pending=$(tr '\n' ' ' < /var/run/reboot-required.pkgs 2>/dev/null || true)
    warn "reboot required after package updates${pending:+ (${pending% })}"
  fi
  if (( QUICK == 0 && LIMITED == 0 && reboot == 0 )); then
    local out rc=0 cmd=""
    if have needs-restarting; then cmd="needs-restarting"
    elif have dnf; then cmd="dnf needs-restarting"; fi
    if [[ -n "$cmd" ]]; then
      # shellcheck disable=SC2086
      out=$(timeout 60 $cmd -r 2>&1 </dev/null) || rc=$?
      if (( rc == 0 )); then
        ok "no reboot required (${cmd} -r)"
      elif (( rc == 1 )) && grep -qi 'reboot is required' <<<"$out"; then
        reboot=1
        warn "reboot required (${cmd} -r): core libraries or the kernel were updated since boot"
      else
        skip "${cmd} -r not usable (exit ${rc}), reboot state unknown"
      fi
    fi
  fi
  if have rpm; then
    latest=$(rpm -q kernel-core --qf '%{VERSION}-%{RELEASE}.%{ARCH}\n' 2>/dev/null | grep -v 'not installed' | sort -V | tail -1 || true)
    if [[ -z "$latest" ]]; then
      info "kernel ${running} (kernel-core not found in rpm)"
    elif [[ "$latest" != "$running" ]]; then
      if (( reboot == 0 )); then warn "newer kernel installed (${latest}), running ${running}; reboot to apply"
      else info "kernel running ${running}, newest installed ${latest}"; fi
    else
      info "kernel ${running} is the newest installed"
    fi
  elif have dpkg-query && ls /boot/vmlinuz-* >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    latest=$(find /boot -maxdepth 1 -name 'vmlinuz-*' -printf '%f\n' | sed 's/^vmlinuz-//' | sort -V | tail -1)
    if [[ "$latest" != "$running" ]]; then info "kernel running ${running}, newest installed ${latest}"
    else info "kernel ${running} is the newest installed"; fi
  fi
  if (( LIMITED == 1 && reboot == 0 )); then
    info "needs-restarting skipped (needs root)"
  elif (( QUICK == 1 && reboot == 0 )); then
    info "needs-restarting skipped (quick mode)"
  fi
  return 0
}

check_serverinit() {
  local conf=/etc/server-init.conf failed=/var/log/server-init-failed.log
  [[ -r "$conf" || -r "$failed" ]] || return 0
  [[ -f "$conf" || -s "$failed" ]] || return 0
  section "Server setup"
  if [[ -f "$conf" && -r "$conf" ]]; then
    local vals admin
    vals=$(grep -E '^(ADMIN_USER|EXTRA_PORTS|INSTALL_DOCKER)=' "$conf" | tr '\n' ' ' || true)
    info "${conf}: ${vals:-no known settings}"
    admin=$(sed -nE 's/^ADMIN_USER=["]?([A-Za-z0-9_-]+).*/\1/p' "$conf" | tail -1 || true)
    if [[ -n "$admin" ]]; then
      if id "$admin" >/dev/null 2>&1; then ok "admin user ${admin} exists"
      else warn "admin user ${admin} from ${conf} does not exist"; fi
    fi
    if [[ -f /etc/ssh/sshd_config.d/00-hardening.conf ]]; then
      info "sshd hardening drop-in present (sshd_config.d/00-hardening.conf)"
    fi
  fi
  local ct=server-cleanup.timer res last
  if unit_exists "$ct"; then
    if systemctl is-active --quiet "$ct" 2>/dev/null; then
      last=$(systemctl show server-cleanup.service -p ExecMainExitTimestamp --value 2>/dev/null || true)
      res=$(systemctl show server-cleanup.service -p Result --value 2>/dev/null || true)
      if [[ -n "$res" && "$res" != success ]]; then warn "${ct} active, last run ${last:-unknown}: ${res} (journalctl -u server-cleanup.service)"
      else ok "${ct} active, last run ${last:-not yet}"; fi
    else
      warn "${ct} installed but not active"
    fi
  fi
  if [[ -s "$failed" && ! -r "$failed" ]]; then
    skip "${failed}: not readable without root"
  elif [[ -s "$failed" ]]; then
    local n last
    n=$(grep -c . "$failed" || true)
    last=$(tail -1 "$failed" | cut -c1-110 || true)
    warn "${n} failed setup step(s) in ${failed}; last: ${last}"
  elif [[ -f "$failed" ]]; then
    ok "no failed setup steps (${failed} is empty)"
  fi
  return 0
}

# Optional section: only for security tools that are installed (common/security-tools.sh). Reads existing reports and
# files only, runs no scan, so it is cheap and -q does not change it. Prints nothing when none is installed.
check_securitytools() {
  local shown=0 f idx n age newest sig
  hdr() { if (( shown == 0 )); then section "Security tools"; shown=1; fi; }

  if have lynis && [[ -r /var/log/lynis-report.dat ]]; then
    idx=$(sed -n 's/^hardening_index=//p' /var/log/lynis-report.dat 2>/dev/null | tail -1 || true)
    if isnum "$idx"; then
      hdr
      f=$(stat -c %Y /var/log/lynis-report.dat 2>/dev/null || echo 0)
      info "lynis hardening index ${idx} (last audit $(human_age $(( $(date +%s) - f ))) ago)"
    fi
  fi

  if have rkhunter; then
    f=$(ls -1t /var/log/security-audit/rkhunter-*.log 2>/dev/null | head -1 || true)
    if [[ -n "$f" && -r "$f" ]]; then
      hdr
      n=$(grep -c '^Warning' "$f" 2>/dev/null || true)
      age=$(human_age $(( $(date +%s) - $(stat -c %Y "$f" 2>/dev/null || echo 0) )))
      if [[ "${n:-0}" -gt 0 ]]; then warn "rkhunter: ${n} warning(s) in the last run (${age} ago), see ${f}"
      else ok "rkhunter: no warnings in the last run (${age} ago)"; fi
    fi
  fi

  if have aide; then
    f=/var/lib/security-tools/aide/aide.db.gz
    if [[ -e "$f" ]]; then
      hdr
      info "aide database from $(date -d "@$(stat -c %Y "$f" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)" +%F)"
    elif [[ -f /etc/security-tools/aide.conf ]]; then
      hdr
      warn "aide installed but its database is missing (aide --config /etc/security-tools/aide.conf --init)"
    fi
  fi

  if have freshclam || have clamscan; then
    newest=0
    for f in /var/lib/clamav/*.cvd /var/lib/clamav/*.cld; do
      [[ -e "$f" ]] || continue
      sig=$(stat -c %Y "$f" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)
      if (( sig > newest )); then newest=$sig; fi
    done
    hdr
    if (( newest == 0 )); then
      warn "clamav: no signature database (run freshclam)"
    else
      age=$(( ( $(date +%s) - newest ) / 86400 ))
      if (( age > CLAM_SIG_WARN_DAYS )); then warn "clamav signatures are ${age} days old (freshclam)"
      else ok "clamav signatures ${age} day(s) old"; fi
    fi
  fi

  if have auditctl && (( ! LIMITED )); then
    hdr
    if auditctl -s 2>/dev/null | grep -q '^enabled [12]'; then ok "auditd: audit enabled"
    else warn "auditd installed but audit is not enabled"; fi
  fi
  return 0
}

check_services() {
  section "Services"
  local u
  for u in sshd.service docker.service gitlab-runner.service; do
    if [[ "$u" == "gitlab-runner.service" ]] && ! is_runner; then continue; fi
    if ! unit_exists "$u"; then
      if [[ "$u" == "sshd.service" ]] && unit_exists ssh.service; then
        u=ssh.service
      elif is_runner; then
        skip "${u} not installed"
        continue
      else
        continue
      fi
    fi
    if systemctl is-active --quiet "$u" 2>/dev/null; then
      ok "${u} active"
    else
      fail "${u} $(systemctl is-active "$u" 2>/dev/null || true)"
    fi
  done

  local t next
  for t in runner-weekly-cleanup.timer runner-disk-guard.timer runner-engine-update.timer ci-image-prewarm.timer; do
    is_runner || break
    unit_exists "$t" || continue
    if systemctl is-active --quiet "$t" 2>/dev/null; then
      next=$(systemctl show "$t" -p NextElapseUSecRealtime --value 2>/dev/null || true)
      if [[ -n "$next" && "$next" != "n/a" ]]; then
        ok "${t} active, next run ${next}"
      else
        ok "${t} active"
      fi
    else
      warn "${t} not active"
    fi
  done

  local s started result
  for s in runner-weekly-cleanup.service runner-disk-guard.service ci-image-prewarm.service; do
    is_runner || break
    unit_exists "$s" || continue
    started=$(systemctl show "$s" -p ExecMainStartTimestamp --value 2>/dev/null || true)
    result=$(systemctl show "$s" -p Result --value 2>/dev/null || true)
    if [[ -z "$started" || "$started" == "n/a" ]]; then
      info "${s%.service}: not run since boot"
    elif [[ "$result" == "success" ]]; then
      ok "${s%.service}: last run ${started}"
    else
      warn "${s%.service}: last run ${started}, result ${result} (journalctl -u ${s})"
    fi
  done

  if is_runner && [[ -s /var/log/runner-engine-update.log ]]; then
    local upd
    upd=$(grep -E ' (OK|SKIP|FAIL|DRY-RUN) ' /var/log/runner-engine-update.log | tail -1 || true)
    case "$upd" in
      *" FAIL "*) warn "Docker/GitLab Runner update: ${upd}" ;;
      "") ;;
      *) info "Docker/GitLab Runner update: ${upd}" ;;
    esac
  fi
  if is_runner && unit_exists ci-job-isolation.service; then
    if systemctl is-active --quiet ci-job-isolation.service 2>/dev/null; then ok "ci-job-isolation active (metadata and host services closed to jobs)"; else warn "ci-job-isolation not active: jobs can reach the metadata service (systemctl restart ci-job-isolation)"; fi
  fi

  if is_runner && unit_exists ci-image-prewarm.service; then
    local summary noarm failed_n
    summary=$(journalctl -t ci-image-prewarm --since -2d --no-pager -o cat 2>/dev/null | grep 'summary:' | tail -1 || true)
    if [[ -n "$summary" ]]; then
      noarm=$(sed -nE 's/.*no_arm=([0-9]+).*/\1/p' <<<"$summary")
      failed_n=$(sed -nE 's/.*failed=([0-9]+).*/\1/p' <<<"$summary")
      if (( ${failed_n:-0} > 0 )); then
        warn "image prewarm: ${failed_n} failed pulls (journalctl -t ci-image-prewarm | grep FAIL)"
      elif (( ${noarm:-0} > 0 )); then
        warn "image prewarm: ${noarm} tags without arm64 (journalctl -t ci-image-prewarm | grep 'NO ARM')"
      else
        ok "image prewarm: ${summary#*summary: }"
      fi
    fi
  fi
  return 0
}

check_runner() {
  if ! is_runner; then
    skip "runner: not a runner host (skipped)"
    return 0
  fi
  if (( LIMITED )); then
    skip "runner: needs root (skipped)"
    return 0
  fi
  section "GitLab Runner"
  if ! have gitlab-runner; then
    skip "gitlab-runner not installed"
    return 0
  fi
  info "$(gitlab-runner --version 2>/dev/null | awk '/^Version:/ { print "version " $2 }')"

  if [[ ! -f "$CFG" ]]; then
    fail "${CFG} missing"
    return 0
  fi

  local n_runners conc rc perms
  n_runners=$(grep -c '^\[\[runners\]\]' "$CFG" || true)
  conc=$(awk -F' = ' '/^concurrent/ { print $2 }' "$CFG")
  info "${n_runners:-0} runner(s) in config, concurrent = ${conc:-?}"
  if (( ${n_runners:-0} == 0 )); then
    fail "no runners registered"
  fi
  rc=$(grep -c 'request_concurrency' "$CFG" || true)
  if (( ${n_runners:-0} > 0 && ${rc:-0} < n_runners )); then
    warn "request_concurrency missing for some runners (slow job pickup)"
  elif (( ${n_runners:-0} > 0 )); then
    ok "request_concurrency set"
  fi
  perms=$(stat -c '%a' "$CFG")
  if [[ "$perms" == "600" ]]; then
    ok "config.toml permissions 600"
  else
    warn "config.toml permissions ${perms}, expected 600 (contains tokens)"
  fi

  if (( QUICK == 0 )); then
    local v valid invalid
    v=$(timeout 30 gitlab-runner verify 2>&1 || true)
    valid=$(grep -c 'is valid' <<<"$v" || true)
    invalid=$(grep -cE 'is not valid|is removed' <<<"$v" || true)
    if (( ${invalid:-0} > 0 )); then
      fail "${invalid} runner(s) rejected by GitLab (token removed or invalid)"
    elif (( ${valid:-0} > 0 )); then
      ok "${valid} runner(s) verified with GitLab"
    else
      warn "could not verify runners with GitLab"
    fi
  fi

  local jobs
  jobs=$(docker ps -q --filter label=com.gitlab.gitlab-runner.managed=true 2>/dev/null | wc -l || true)
  info "running job containers: ${jobs:-0}"

  if grep -q '^listen_address' "$CFG"; then
    if [[ "$(http_code http://127.0.0.1:9252/metrics 3)" == "200" ]]; then
      ok "metrics endpoint 127.0.0.1:9252"
    else
      fail "metrics endpoint 127.0.0.1:9252 not responding"
    fi
  fi

  local errs
  errs=$(journalctl -u gitlab-runner --since -1h --no-pager -o cat 2>/dev/null | grep -cE 'ERROR|level=error' || true)
  if (( ${errs:-0} > 0 )); then
    warn "${errs} error lines in runner log in the last hour (journalctl -u gitlab-runner --since -1h | grep ERROR)"
  else
    ok "no runner errors in the last hour"
  fi
  return 0
}

check_docker() {
  if ! is_runner && ! have docker; then
    skip "docker: not installed"
    return 0
  fi
  section "Docker"
  if ! have docker; then
    skip "docker not installed"
    return 0
  fi
  if (( LIMITED )) && ! docker info >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    skip "docker: no access (not root, not in the docker group)"
    return 0
  fi
  if ! docker info >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    fail "docker daemon not responding"
    return 0
  fi
  info "version $(docker version -f '{{.Server.Version}}' 2>/dev/null || echo unknown)"

  local mirrors
  mirrors=$(docker info -f '{{range .RegistryConfig.Mirrors}}{{.}} {{end}}' 2>/dev/null || true)
  if [[ -n "${mirrors// /}" ]]; then
    info "registry mirrors: ${mirrors}"
  fi

  local line
  while IFS= read -r line; do
    info "$line"
  done < <(docker system df --format '{{.Type}}: {{.Size}} ({{.Reclaimable}} reclaimable)' 2>/dev/null || true)
  return 0
}

check_containers() {
  if ! is_runner && ! have docker; then
    return 0
  fi
  section "Containers"
  if ! have docker || ! docker info >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    skip "docker not available (or no access)"
    return 0
  fi

  local -a ids
  mapfile -t ids < <(docker ps -q 2>/dev/null || true)
  if (( ${#ids[@]} == 0 )); then
    skip "no running containers"
    return 0
  fi

  local cores cpu_limit now
  cores=$(nproc)
  cpu_limit=$(( cores * 90 ))
  now=$(date +%s)

  local -A s_cpu s_mem s_memp s_pids
  local name cpu mem memp pids
  while IFS=$'\t' read -r name cpu mem memp pids; do
    [[ -z "$name" ]] && continue
    s_cpu[$name]="${cpu%\%}"
    s_mem[$name]="$mem"
    s_memp[$name]="${memp%\%}"
    s_pids[$name]="$pids"
  done < <(docker stats --no-stream --format '{{.Name}}\t{{.CPUPerc}}\t{{.MemUsage}}\t{{.MemPerc}}\t{{.PIDs}}' "${ids[@]}" 2>/dev/null || true)

  local tpl='{{.Name}}|{{.State.Status}}|{{if .State.Health}}{{.State.Health.Status}}{{else}}none{{end}}|{{.RestartCount}}|{{.State.OOMKilled}}|{{.State.StartedAt}}|{{.HostConfig.Memory}}|{{index .Config.Labels "com.gitlab.gitlab-runner.managed"}}'
  local state health restarts oom started memlimit job started_s age short details level reasons
  local n_total=0 n_jobs=0

  while IFS='|' read -r name state health restarts oom started memlimit job; do
    [[ -z "$name" ]] && continue
    name="${name#/}"
    n_total=$((n_total + 1))
    short="$name"
    if (( ${#short} > 30 )); then short="${short:0:27}..."; fi

    started_s=$(date -d "$started" +%s 2>/dev/null || echo "$now")
    age=$(human_age $(( now - started_s )))
    cpu="${s_cpu[$name]:-?}"
    mem="${s_mem[$name]:-?}"
    memp="${s_memp[$name]:-?}"
    pids="${s_pids[$name]:-?}"

    details="up ${age} | cpu ${cpu}% | mem ${mem}"
    if [[ "${memlimit:-0}" == "0" ]]; then details+=" (no limit)"; else details+=" (${memp}%)"; fi
    details+=" | pids ${pids} | health ${health} | restarts ${restarts}"
    if [[ "$job" == "true" ]]; then
      details="CI job | ${details}"
      n_jobs=$((n_jobs + 1))
    fi

    level=ok
    reasons=""
    if [[ "$state" != "running" ]]; then level=fail; reasons+=" state ${state};"; fi
    if [[ "$oom" == "true" ]]; then level=fail; reasons+=" OOM-killed;"; fi
    case "$health" in
      unhealthy) level=fail; reasons+=" healthcheck failing;" ;;
      starting) if [[ "$level" == ok ]]; then level=warn; fi; reasons+=" healthcheck starting;" ;;
    esac
    if (( restarts > 3 )); then
      if [[ "$level" == ok ]]; then level=warn; fi
      reasons+=" ${restarts} restarts;"
    fi
    if [[ "${memlimit:-0}" != "0" ]] && isnum "$memp"; then
      if gt "$memp" 95; then level=fail; reasons+=" memory at limit;"
      elif gt "$memp" 85; then
        if [[ "$level" == ok ]]; then level=warn; fi
        reasons+=" memory near limit;"
      fi
    fi
    if [[ "$job" != "true" ]] && isnum "$cpu" && gt "$cpu" "$cpu_limit"; then
      if [[ "$level" == ok ]]; then level=warn; fi
      reasons+=" high CPU;"
    fi

    case "$level" in
      ok)   ok   "$(printf '%-30s' "$short") ${details}" ;;
      warn) warn "$(printf '%-30s' "$short") ${details} |${reasons%;}" ;;
      fail) fail "$(printf '%-30s' "$short") ${details} |${reasons%;}" ;;
    esac
  done < <(docker inspect -f "$tpl" "${ids[@]}" 2>/dev/null || true)

  if is_runner; then
    info "${n_total} running containers, ${n_jobs} of them CI jobs"
  else
    info "${n_total} running containers"
  fi
  return 0
}

check_caches() {
  if ! is_runner; then
    local d any=0
    for d in /opt/registry-cache /opt/npm-cache /opt/monitoring /opt/logging; do
      [[ -d "$d" ]] && any=1
    done
    if (( any == 0 )); then
      skip "caches, monitoring, logging: not installed"
      return 0
    fi
  fi
  section "Caches and monitoring"
  local c
  if [[ -d /opt/registry-cache ]]; then
    c=$(http_code http://127.0.0.1:5000/v2/ 3)
    if [[ "$c" == "200" ]]; then ok "Docker Hub cache 127.0.0.1:5000"
    else fail "Docker Hub cache not responding (HTTP ${c:-000}), pulls fall back to Docker Hub"; fi
  else
    is_runner && skip "Docker Hub cache not installed"
  fi

  if [[ -d /opt/npm-cache ]]; then
    local bip
    bip=$(ip -4 -o addr show docker0 2>/dev/null | awk '{ print $4 }' | cut -d/ -f1 || true)
    c=$(http_code "http://${bip:-172.17.0.1}:4873/-/ping" 3)
    if [[ "$c" == "200" ]]; then ok "npm cache (Verdaccio) ${bip:-172.17.0.1}:4873"
    else fail "npm cache not responding (HTTP ${c:-000}), npm ci in jobs will fail"; fi
  else
    is_runner && skip "npm cache (Verdaccio) not installed"
  fi

  if [[ -d /srv/ci-cache ]]; then
    info "shared cache /srv/ci-cache: $(du -sh /srv/ci-cache 2>/dev/null | cut -f1)"
  fi

  if [[ -d /opt/monitoring ]]; then
    if [[ "$(http_code "${PROM_URL}/-/ready" 3)" == "200" ]]; then ok "Prometheus ready"
    else fail "Prometheus not ready"; fi
    if [[ "$(http_code http://127.0.0.1:3000/api/health 3)" == "200" ]]; then ok "Grafana healthy"
    else fail "Grafana not healthy"; fi
    if have jq; then
      local down firing
      down=$(curl -fsS -m 5 "${PROM_URL}/api/v1/targets" 2>/dev/null \
        | jq -r '.data.activeTargets[] | select(.health != "up") | .labels.job' 2>/dev/null | tr '\n' ' ' || true)
      if [[ -n "${down// /}" ]]; then fail "Prometheus targets down: ${down}"
      else ok "all Prometheus targets up"; fi
      firing=$(curl -fsS -m 5 "${PROM_URL}/api/v1/alerts" 2>/dev/null \
        | jq -r '.data.alerts[] | select(.state == "firing") | .labels.alertname' 2>/dev/null | sort -u | tr '\n' ' ' || true)
      if [[ -n "${firing// /}" ]]; then warn "alerts firing: ${firing}"
      else ok "no alerts firing"; fi
    fi
  else
    is_runner && skip "monitoring stack not installed"
  fi

  if [[ -d /opt/logging ]]; then
    if [[ "$(http_code http://127.0.0.1:3100/ready 3)" == "200" ]]; then ok "Loki ready"
    else fail "Loki not ready, logs are not stored"; fi
    if [[ "$(http_code http://127.0.0.1:12345/-/ready 3)" == "200" ]]; then ok "Alloy ready"
    else fail "Alloy not ready, logs are not collected"; fi
  else
    is_runner && skip "logging (Loki + Alloy) not installed"
  fi
  return 0
}

check_url() {
  local name="$1" url="$2" expect="$3" out code t
  out=$(curl -s -o /dev/null -m 10 -w '%{http_code} %{time_total}' "$url" 2>/dev/null || true)
  code="${out%% *}"
  t="${out##* }"
  if [[ " ${expect} " == *" ${code} "* ]]; then
    if gt "${t:-0}" 3; then warn "${name} slow (${t}s)"
    else ok "${name} reachable (HTTP ${code}, ${t}s)"; fi
  else
    fail "${name} unreachable (HTTP ${code:-000})"
  fi
}

check_network() {
  (( QUICK == 1 )) && return 0
  section "Network"
  local h unresolved="" hosts
  if is_runner; then
    hosts="gitlab.com registry.gitlab.com registry-1.docker.io registry.npmjs.org"
  else
    hosts="cloudflare.com"
    have docker && hosts+=" registry-1.docker.io"
  fi
  for h in $hosts; do
    getent ahostsv4 "$h" >/dev/null 2>&1 || unresolved="${unresolved}${h} "
  done
  if [[ -n "$unresolved" ]]; then fail "DNS cannot resolve: ${unresolved}"
  elif is_runner; then ok "DNS resolves GitLab, Docker Hub and npm"
  else ok "DNS resolves ${hosts}"; fi

  if is_runner; then
    check_url "gitlab.com API" "https://gitlab.com/api/v4/version" "200 401"
    check_url "GitLab registry" "https://registry.gitlab.com/v2/" "200 401"
    check_url "Docker Hub" "https://registry-1.docker.io/v2/" "200 401"
    check_url "npm registry" "https://registry.npmjs.org/" "200"
  else
    check_url "internet (HTTPS)" "https://cloudflare.com/" "200 301 302 403"
    if have docker; then check_url "Docker Hub" "https://registry-1.docker.io/v2/" "200 401"; fi
  fi

  if grep -qE '^precedence ::ffff:0:0/96' /etc/gai.conf 2>/dev/null; then
    info "IPv4 preferred over IPv6 (gai.conf)"
  fi
  return 0
}

check_trends() {
  is_runner || return 0
  section "Trends (Prometheus, last 24h)"
  if ! have jq; then
    skip "jq not installed, trend checks skipped"
    return 0
  fi
  if [[ "$(http_code "${PROM_URL}/-/ready" 3)" != "200" ]]; then
    skip "Prometheus not available, trend checks skipped"
    return 0
  fi

  local lowest hist_h
  lowest=$(prom_q 'min(prometheus_tsdb_lowest_timestamp_seconds)')
  if isnum "$lowest"; then
    hist_h=$(awk -v n="$(date +%s)" -v l="$lowest" 'BEGIN { printf "%.0f", (n - l) / 3600 }')
    if (( hist_h < 24 )); then
      info "metrics history: ${hist_h}h (less than 24h, trends are approximate)"
    else
      info "metrics history: $(( hist_h / 24 ))d $(( hist_h % 24 ))h"
    fi
  fi

  # A forecast from minutes of data mostly measures the install itself (image pulls, package updates),
  # so it needs FORECAST_MIN_HISTORY_H hours of metrics before it can warn or fail.
  local avail deriv days
  avail=$(prom_q 'node_filesystem_avail_bytes{mountpoint="/",fstype!="rootfs"}')
  deriv=$(prom_q 'deriv(node_filesystem_avail_bytes{mountpoint="/",fstype!="rootfs"}[12h])')
  if ! isnum "${hist_h:-}" || (( hist_h < FORECAST_MIN_HISTORY_H )); then
    skip "disk forecast: needs ${FORECAST_MIN_HISTORY_H}h of metrics (have ${hist_h:-0}h)"
  elif isnum "$avail" && isnum "$deriv"; then
    if awk -v d="$deriv" 'BEGIN { exit !(d < 0) }'; then
      days=$(awk -v a="$avail" -v d="$deriv" 'BEGIN { printf "%.1f", a / (-d) / 86400 }')
      if gt 3 "$days"; then
        fail "disk will be full in ~${days} days at the current rate"
      elif gt 14 "$days"; then
        warn "disk will be full in ~${days} days at the current rate"
      else
        ok "disk forecast: full in ~${days} days at the current rate"
      fi
    else
      ok "disk usage not growing over the last 12h"
    fi
  else
    skip "disk forecast: not enough data yet"
  fi

  local mem_peak p
  mem_peak=$(prom_q 'max_over_time(((1 - node_memory_MemAvailable_bytes / node_memory_MemTotal_bytes) * 100)[24h:1m])')
  if isnum "$mem_peak"; then
    p=$(num "$mem_peak" 0)
    if gt "$p" 95; then fail "memory peaked at ${p}% (OOM risk during builds)"
    elif gt "$p" 85; then warn "memory peaked at ${p}% (close to the limit during builds)"
    else ok "memory peak ${p}%"; fi
  else
    skip "memory peak: no data"
  fi

  local cpu_avg cpu_peak steal
  cpu_avg=$(prom_q 'avg_over_time((100 - avg(rate(node_cpu_seconds_total{mode="idle"}[5m])) * 100)[24h:5m])')
  cpu_peak=$(prom_q 'max_over_time((100 - avg(rate(node_cpu_seconds_total{mode="idle"}[5m])) * 100)[24h:5m])')
  if isnum "$cpu_avg" && isnum "$cpu_peak"; then
    info "CPU average $(num "$cpu_avg" 0)%, peak $(num "$cpu_peak" 0)%"
  fi
  steal=$(prom_q 'avg(rate(node_cpu_seconds_total{mode="steal"}[24h])) * 100')
  if isnum "$steal"; then
    if gt "$steal" 10; then
      warn "CPU steal $(num "$steal" 1)% (other VMs on the host slow your builds)"
    else
      ok "CPU steal $(num "$steal" 1)%"
    fi
  fi

  local up_pct
  up_pct=$(prom_q 'avg_over_time(up{job="gitlab-runner"}[24h]) * 100')
  if isnum "$up_pct"; then
    if gt 99 "$up_pct"; then warn "runner metrics available $(num "$up_pct" 1)% of the last 24h"
    else ok "runner available $(num "$up_pct" 1)% of the last 24h"; fi
  fi

  local total
  total=$(prom_q 'sum(increase(gitlab_runner_jobs_total[24h]))')
  if isnum "$total"; then
    info "jobs in the last 24h: $(num "$total" 0)"
  else
    info "jobs in the last 24h: none recorded"
  fi

  local reason n infra=0 line
  while IFS= read -r line; do
    [[ -z "$line" ]] && continue
    reason="${line%%=*}"
    n=$(num "${line##*=}" 0)
    (( n == 0 )) && continue
    if [[ "$reason" == "script_failure" ]]; then
      info "${n} jobs failed in their scripts (build or tests, not the server)"
    else
      warn "${n} jobs failed with ${reason:-unknown reason} (server or runner side)"
      infra=1
    fi
  done < <(prom_qv 'sum by (failure_reason) (increase(gitlab_runner_failed_jobs_total[24h])) > 0')
  if (( infra == 0 )); then
    ok "no server-side job failures in the last 24h"
  fi

  local p95 base
  p95=$(prom_q 'histogram_quantile(0.95, sum by (le) (rate(gitlab_runner_job_duration_seconds_bucket[24h])))')
  base=$(prom_q 'histogram_quantile(0.95, sum by (le) (rate(gitlab_runner_job_duration_seconds_bucket[7d] offset 1d)))')
  if isnum "$p95"; then
    if isnum "$base" && gt "$base" 0 && gt "$p95" "$(awk -v b="$base" 'BEGIN { print b * 1.5 }')"; then
      warn "job duration p95 $(num "$p95" 0)s, usual $(num "$base" 0)s (1.5x slower)"
    elif isnum "$base"; then
      ok "job duration p95 $(num "$p95" 0)s (usual $(num "$base" 0)s)"
    else
      ok "job duration p95 $(num "$p95" 0)s"
    fi
  else
    skip "job duration: no jobs with timing data yet"
  fi

  local q95
  q95=$(prom_q 'histogram_quantile(0.95, sum by (le) (rate(gitlab_runner_job_queue_duration_seconds_bucket[24h])))')
  if isnum "$q95"; then
    if gt "$q95" 60; then
      warn "jobs waited up to $(num "$q95" 0)s (p95) before pickup"
    else
      ok "job pickup wait p95 $(num "$q95" 0)s"
    fi
  else
    skip "job pickup wait: metric not available in this runner version or no jobs yet"
  fi

  local fired
  fired=$(prom_qv 'count by (alertname) (max_over_time(ALERTS{alertstate="firing"}[24h]))' | cut -d= -f1 | sort -u | tr '\n' ' ' || true)
  if [[ -n "${fired// /}" ]]; then
    warn "alerts fired in the last 24h: ${fired}"
  else
    ok "no alerts fired in the last 24h"
  fi
  return 0
}

# ---- brief mode: a short summary for the login screen and `srv status` -------------------------------------------
BRIEF_DIR="${HEALTH_BRIEF_DIR:-/var/lib/server-health}"
UPDATE_STATE="${SERVER_UPDATE_STATE:-/var/lib/server-update}/state"
CLEANUP_STATE="${SERVER_CLEANUP_STATE:-/var/lib/server-cleanup}"

# kv FILE KEY: value of key=value in a one-line state file (empty when missing)
kv() { sed -nE "s/(^|.* )$2=([^ ]*).*/\2/p" "$1" 2>/dev/null | head -n1 || true; }
# lvl VALUE WARN FAIL: marker for a value where higher is worse
lvl() {
  if (( $1 >= $3 )); then printf ' %sFAIL%s' "$C_FAIL" "$C_N"; elif (( $1 >= $2 )); then printf ' %sWARN%s' "$C_WARN" "$C_N"; fi
  return 0
}
ago() { # ago EPOCH: "3d4h ago" or "never"
  if [[ "${1:-}" =~ ^[0-9]+$ && "$1" -gt 0 ]]; then echo "$(human_age $(( $(date +%s) - $1 ))) ago"; else echo never; fi
}

brief_full() { # run the full report when the last one is older than 6 hours; keep its counts for the summary
  local f="$BRIEF_DIR/full" t=0 out w fl rc=0
  [[ -r "$f" ]] && t=$(kv "$f" time)
  [[ "$t" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]] || t=0
  (( $(date +%s) - t >= 21600 )) || return 0
  out=$(HEALTH_REEXEC=1 "$(readlink -f "$0")" --no-color 2>&1) || rc=$?
  ( umask 077; printf '%s\n' "$out" > "$BRIEF_DIR/last-full.log" )
  w=$(sed -nE 's/^ +[0-9]+ OK +([0-9]+) WARN +([0-9]+) FAIL.*/\1/p' <<<"$out" | tail -n1 || true)
  fl=$(sed -nE 's/^ +[0-9]+ OK +([0-9]+) WARN +([0-9]+) FAIL.*/\2/p' <<<"$out" | tail -n1 || true)
  printf 'time=%s warn=%s fail=%s\n' "$(date +%s)" "${w:-0}" "${fl:-0}" > "$f.tmp" && mv -f "$f.tmp" "$f"
  if (( ${fl:-0} > 0 )) && [[ -x "${SERVER_SBIN:-/usr/local/sbin}/server-notify" ]]; then
    "${SERVER_SBIN:-/usr/local/sbin}/server-notify" health "health: ${fl} FAIL, ${w:-0} WARN; $(grep -E '^ +FAIL' <<<"$out" | head -n2 | tr -s ' ' | tr '\n' ';' | cut -c1-300)" || true
  fi
  return 0
}

brief_render() {
  local up_s d h load dp mp mem_total mem_avail fw="" f2b="" s u r w fl st
  up_s=$(cut -d. -f1 /proc/uptime 2>/dev/null || echo 0); d=$(( up_s / 86400 )); h=$(( up_s % 86400 / 3600 ))
  load=$(cut -d' ' -f1-3 /proc/loadavg 2>/dev/null || echo "?")
  printf '%shost%s  %s  up %sd%sh  load %s  (as of %s)\n' "$C_B" "$C_N" "$(uname -n)" "$d" "$h" "$load" "$(date +%H:%M)"
  dp=$(df -P / | awk 'NR == 2 { gsub(/%/, "", $5); print $5 }')
  mem_total=$(awk '/^MemTotal:/ { print $2 }' /proc/meminfo); mem_avail=$(awk '/^MemAvailable:/ { print $2 }' /proc/meminfo)
  mp=$(( mem_total > 0 ? 100 * mem_avail / mem_total : 100 ))
  printf '%sdisk%s  / %s%% used%s   mem %s%% free%s\n' "$C_B" "$C_N" "$dp" "$(lvl "$dp" "$DISK_WARN" "$DISK_FAIL")" "$mp" \
    "$( if (( mp <= MEM_FAIL )); then lvl 1 2 1; elif (( mp <= MEM_WARN )); then lvl 1 1 2; fi )"
  if (( LIMITED )); then
    printf '%sfw%s    unknown (needs root)\n' "$C_B" "$C_N"
  else
    detect_firewall
    case "$FW_KIND:$FW_STATE" in
      firewalld:active) fw="firewalld active, open: $(firewall-cmd --list-services --list-ports 2>/dev/null | tr -s ' \n' ' ' | sed 's/ $//')" ;;
      ufw:active) fw="ufw active" ;;
      none:*) fw="none installed $(lvl 1 1 2)" ;;
      *) fw="$FW_KIND $FW_STATE $(lvl 1 2 1)" ;;
    esac
    printf '%sfw%s    %s\n' "$C_B" "$C_N" "$fw"
    if have fail2ban-client && st=$(fail2ban-client status sshd 2>/dev/null); then
      f2b=$(sed -nE 's/^[^C]*Currently banned:[[:space:]]*([0-9]+).*/\1/p' <<<"$st" | head -n1 || true)
      printf '%sf2b%s   %s banned now (sshd jail)\n' "$C_B" "$C_N" "${f2b:-0}"
    elif have fail2ban-client || unit_exists fail2ban.service; then
      printf '%sf2b%s   not running %s\n' "$C_B" "$C_N" "$(lvl 1 1 2)"
    fi
  fi
  if [[ -r "$UPDATE_STATE" ]]; then
    u=$(kv "$UPDATE_STATE" updates); r=$(kv "$UPDATE_STATE" reboot)
    printf '%supd%s   %s update(s) pending, reboot needed: %s%s  (checked %s)\n' "$C_B" "$C_N" "${u:-?}" "$( [[ "$r" == 1 ]] && echo yes || echo no )" \
      "$( [[ "$r" == 1 ]] && lvl 1 1 2 )" "$(ago "$(kv "$UPDATE_STATE" time)")"
    s="update $(ago "$(kv "$UPDATE_STATE" updated)") ($(kv "$UPDATE_STATE" uresult || true))"
  else
    printf '%supd%s   unknown: run `srv update --check`\n' "$C_B" "$C_N"
    s="update never"
  fi
  printf '%slast%s  %s, cleanup %s (%s)\n' "$C_B" "$C_N" "$s" "$(ago "$(cat "$CLEANUP_STATE/last-run" 2>/dev/null || true)")" "$(cat "$CLEANUP_STATE/last-result" 2>/dev/null || echo none)"
  if [[ -r "$BRIEF_DIR/full" ]]; then
    w=$(kv "$BRIEF_DIR/full" warn); fl=$(kv "$BRIEF_DIR/full" fail)
    printf '%scheck%s full report %s: %s FAIL, %s WARN%s\n' "$C_B" "$C_N" "$(ago "$(kv "$BRIEF_DIR/full" time)")" "${fl:-0}" "${w:-0}" \
      "$( if (( ${fl:-0} > 0 )); then printf ' %s-> run: srv health%s' "$C_FAIL" "$C_N"; elif (( ${w:-0} > 0 )); then printf ' %s-> run: srv health%s' "$C_WARN" "$C_N"; fi )"
  else
    printf '%scheck%s no full report yet (health, or wait for the timer)\n' "$C_B" "$C_N"
  fi
}

brief_main() {
  if (( WRITE )); then
    (( LIMITED == 0 )) || { echo "health --brief --write needs root" >&2; exit 1; }
    C_OK=""; C_WARN=""; C_FAIL=""; C_B=""; C_D=""; C_N=""
    mkdir -p "$BRIEF_DIR" && chmod 0755 "$BRIEF_DIR"
    brief_full
    local tmp; tmp=$(mktemp "$BRIEF_DIR/.brief.XXXXXX")
    brief_render > "$tmp"
    chmod 0644 "$tmp"; mv -f "$tmp" "$BRIEF_DIR/brief.txt"
  else
    brief_render
  fi
  exit 0
}

if (( BRIEF == 1 )); then brief_main; fi

detect_profile
detect_firewall

if (( ONLY_CONTAINERS == 1 )); then
  printf '%scontainer health%s  %s | %s | profile: %s\n' "$C_B" "$C_N" "$(uname -n)" "$(date '+%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S %Z')" "$PROFILE"
  check_containers
  print_summary_and_exit
fi

printf '%shealth report%s  %s | %s | profile: %s\n' "$C_B" "$C_N" "$(uname -n)" "$(date '+%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S %Z')" "$PROFILE"
if (( LIMITED )); then info "limited mode: not root and sudo needs a password; root-only checks skipped"; fi
if (( RUNNER_UNREGISTERED == 1 )); then info "gitlab-runner is installed but no runner is registered: checked as a plain server (--profile runner to check it)"; fi
if (( RUNNER_UNREGISTERED == 2 )); then info "runner config not readable without root: checked as a plain server (run as root or use --profile runner)"; fi
check_system
check_security
check_firewall
check_updates
check_serverinit
check_securitytools
check_services
check_runner
check_docker
check_containers
check_caches
check_network
check_trends
print_summary_and_exit
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 755 "$R/common/health"

install -d "$R/common"
cat > "$R/common/pin-repo-key.sh" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Download a package repository signing key and install it only if it has the expected fingerprint.
# Usage: pin-repo-key URL FINGERPRINT DEST
# The fingerprint is the 40 hex digits of the primary key (spaces allowed). The file is checked with gpg in an empty
# keyring; only the key with that fingerprint is written to DEST (anything else in the download is dropped).
# On a mismatch nothing is written and the exit code is 1, so the repository is never trusted with a swapped key.
set -Eeuo pipefail

if [[ $# -ne 3 || "$1" == "-h" || "$1" == "--help" ]]; then
  echo "Usage: $0 URL FINGERPRINT DEST" >&2
  exit 2
fi
url="$1"
want=$(printf '%s' "$2" | tr -d ' ' | tr '[:lower:]' '[:upper:]')
dest="$3"
[[ "$want" =~ ^[0-9A-F]{40}$ ]] || { echo "pin-repo-key: fingerprint must be 40 hex digits" >&2; exit 2; }
command -v gpg >/dev/null 2>&1 || { echo "pin-repo-key: gpg is not installed" >&2; exit 1; }

work=$(mktemp -d)
trap 'rm -rf "$work"' EXIT
chmod 0700 "$work"
curl -fsSL --max-time 60 "$url" -o "${work}/key"
export GNUPGHOME="${work}/gnupg"
install -d -m 0700 "$GNUPGHOME"
gpg --batch --quiet --import "${work}/key" 2>/dev/null
if ! gpg --batch --with-colons --list-keys 2>/dev/null \
     | awk -F: '$1 == "pub" { g = 1; next } $1 == "fpr" && g { print $10; g = 0 }' | grep -qx "$want"; then
  echo "pin-repo-key: ${url} has no key with fingerprint ${want}; not installed" >&2
  exit 1
fi
gpg --batch --export --armor "$want" > "${work}/out" 2>/dev/null
[[ -s "${work}/out" ]] || { echo "pin-repo-key: export failed" >&2; exit 1; }
install -D -m 0644 "${work}/out" "$dest"
echo "pin-repo-key: ${dest} installed (${want})"
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 755 "$R/common/pin-repo-key.sh"

install -d "$R/common"
cat > "$R/common/shell-history.sh" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
# shellcheck shell=bash
# Shell history and fzf key bindings for interactive bash (Ctrl+R history search, Ctrl+T file search).
# Installed as /etc/profile.d/90-shell-history.sh (runner hosts) or 90-server-shell.sh (plain servers).
# The same text is embedded in the server/server-init-*.sh scripts (they must stay self-contained):
# keep all copies identical, tools/test-cloud-init.py checks that.
[ -n "${BASH_VERSION-}" ] || return 0
[[ $- == *i* ]] || return 0

__shell_history_vars() {
  HISTSIZE=100000
  HISTFILESIZE=200000
  HISTTIMEFORMAT='%F %T  '
  HISTCONTROL=ignoreboth:erasedups
}
__shell_history_vars
shopt -s histappend cmdhist

# Run before each prompt: append the last command to the history file so parallel sessions share history.
# On Debian and Ubuntu the user's ~/.bashrc runs after this file and lowers the limits, so the first
# prompt applies them again (a later change by hand in a session is kept).
__shell_history_prompt() {
  history -a
  if [[ -z "${__shell_history_done-}" ]]; then
    __shell_history_done=1
    __shell_history_vars
  fi
}
# Keeps an existing PROMPT_COMMAND (string or array) and is safe to source twice.
# When PROMPT_COMMAND is still empty (Fedora/EL login shells read profile.d before /etc/bashrc, which sets
# the terminal title only if PROMPT_COMMAND is empty), use an array with an empty first element: /etc/bashrc
# then fills element 0 with its title command and the hook stays in element 1 (bash 5.1+ runs every element).
if [[ -z "${PROMPT_COMMAND-}" && "$(declare -p PROMPT_COMMAND 2>/dev/null)" != "declare -a"* ]] && (( BASH_VERSINFO[0] > 5 || (BASH_VERSINFO[0] == 5 && BASH_VERSINFO[1] >= 1) )); then
  unset PROMPT_COMMAND
  PROMPT_COMMAND=('' '__shell_history_prompt')
fi
case "$(declare -p PROMPT_COMMAND 2>/dev/null)" in
  "declare -a"*)
    [[ " ${PROMPT_COMMAND[*]} " == *" __shell_history_prompt "* ]] || PROMPT_COMMAND+=('__shell_history_prompt')
    ;;
  *)
    if [[ "${PROMPT_COMMAND-}" != *__shell_history_prompt* ]]; then
      __pc="${PROMPT_COMMAND-}"
      while [[ "$__pc" == *[[:space:]\;] ]]; do __pc="${__pc%?}"; done
      # shellcheck disable=SC2178
      PROMPT_COMMAND="${__pc:+$__pc;}__shell_history_prompt"
      unset __pc
    fi
    ;;
esac

# Debian and Ubuntu install fd as fdfind.
if ! command -v fd >/dev/null 2>&1 && command -v fdfind >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  alias fd=fdfind
  __fd=fdfind
elif command -v fd >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  __fd=fd
fi
if [[ -n "${__fd-}" ]]; then
  export FZF_DEFAULT_COMMAND="${FZF_DEFAULT_COMMAND:-$__fd --type f --hidden --follow --exclude .git}"
  export FZF_CTRL_T_COMMAND="${FZF_CTRL_T_COMMAND:-$FZF_DEFAULT_COMMAND}"
  export FZF_ALT_C_COMMAND="${FZF_ALT_C_COMMAND:-$__fd --type d --hidden --follow --exclude .git}"
fi
unset __fd

if command -v fzf >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  for __f in /usr/share/fzf/shell/key-bindings.bash /usr/share/doc/fzf/examples/key-bindings.bash; do
    if [[ -r "$__f" ]]; then
      # shellcheck source=/dev/null
      . "$__f"
      __fzf_done=1
      break
    fi
  done
  # fzf 0.48+ can print its own bindings when the package ships no file.
  if [[ -z "${__fzf_done-}" ]] && __fzf_b=$(fzf --bash 2>/dev/null); then
    eval "$__fzf_b"
  fi
  unset __f __fzf_done __fzf_b
fi
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 755 "$R/common/shell-history.sh"

install -d "$R/common"
cat > "$R/common/sysctl-hardening.conf" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
# Kernel hardening for runner hosts. A leading "-" ignores a key the running kernel does not have.
-kernel.kptr_restrict = 2
-kernel.dmesg_restrict = 1
-kernel.yama.ptrace_scope = 1
-fs.protected_fifos = 2
-fs.protected_regular = 2
net.ipv4.conf.all.log_martians = 1
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 644 "$R/common/sysctl-hardening.conf"

echo "files written to $R: 51"
bash "$R/runner/install/users.sh" </dev/null
bash "$R/common/admin-tools.sh" </dev/null
bash "$R/runner/install/host-tools.sh" </dev/null
bash "$R/runner/install/host-firewall.sh" </dev/null
bash "$R/runner/install/registry-cache.sh" </dev/null
bash "$R/runner/install/monitoring.sh" </dev/null
bash "$R/runner/install/logging.sh" </dev/null
INSTALL
sudo DESCRIPTION=hetzner-cax11 bash /root/server-init-config/runner/install/register-runner.sh && sudo bash /root/server-init-config/runner/install/npm-cache.sh && sudo bash /root/server-init-config/runner/install/shared-cache.sh && sudo /usr/local/bin/health -q
````
